  CODE
  {

    PROCEDURE SetStyle@5() : Text;
    BEGIN
      IF Status = Status::Complete THEN
         EXIT('Favorable');

      IF Status = Status::Incomplete THEN
        EXIT('Ambiguous');

      IF Status = Status::"Missing for Delivery" THEN
        EXIT('Attention');

      IF Status = Status::Missing THEN
        EXIT('Unfavorable');

      IF Status = Status::Surplus THEN
        EXIT('StrongAccent');

      EXIT('');
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ChangeWarehouseStatus@50000();
    VAR
      WarehouseShipmentHeader@50001 : Record 7320;
      BarcodeMgmt@50000 : Codeunit 50106;
      WarehouseReceiptHeader@50002 : Record 7316;
    BEGIN
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      CASE Rec."Document Type" OF
        Rec."Document Type"::Shipment:
          BEGIN
      //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
            // <ZGNAIMPRRE-10>
            IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(Rec."Document No.") AND NOT (WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized) THEN BEGIN
              WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" := WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
              WarehouseShipmentHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
            END;
            // </ZGNAIMPRRE-10>
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
          END;
        Rec."Document Type"::Receipt:
          BEGIN
            IF WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(Rec."Document No.") AND NOT (WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized) THEN BEGIN
              WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" := WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
              WarehouseReceiptHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
            END;
          END;
      END;
      //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>BARKOD SKLADIÊNA PRIMKA NABAVE
         <TASK>Added new option value in Document Type</TASK>
      </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
    }
    END.
  }
}

    PROCEDURE SetStyle@5() : Text;
    BEGIN
      IF Status = Status::Complete THEN
         EXIT('Favorable');

      IF Status = Status::Incomplete THEN
        EXIT('Ambiguous');

      IF Status = Status::Missing THEN
        EXIT('Attention');

      IF Status = Status::"Missing for Delivery" THEN
        EXIT('Unfavorable');

      IF Status = Status::Surplus THEN
        EXIT('StrongAccent');

      EXIT('');
    END;

    { 90  ;   ;Source Creation DateTime;DateTime  ;DataClassification=ToBeClassified;
                                                   CaptionML=[ENU=Source Creation DateTime;
                                                              HRV=Datum i vrijeme kreiranja izvora] }
    { 100 ;   ;Source User ID      ;Text30        ;DataClassification=ToBeClassified;
                                                   CaptionML=[ENU=Source User ID;
                                                              HRV=ID korisnika izvora] }
    { 110 ;   ;Source Code         ;Text30        ;DataClassification=ToBeClassified;
                                                   CaptionML=[ENU=Source Code;
                                                              HRV=Êifra izvora] }
    { 111 ;   ;Retail Location     ;Boolean       ;DataClassification=ToBeClassified;
                                                   CaptionML=[ENU=Retail Location;
                                                              HRV=MP lokacija] }
    { 120 ;   ;Destination Code    ;Text30        ;DataClassification=ToBeClassified;
                                                   CaptionML=[ENU=Destination Code;
                                                              HRV=Êifra odrediÁta] }
    { 200 ;   ;User ID             ;Code50        ;DataClassification=ToBeClassified;
                                                   CaptionML=[ENU=User ID;
                                                              HRV=ID korisnika] }
    { 210 ;   ;Creation DateTime   ;DateTime      ;DataClassification=ToBeClassified;
                                                   CaptionML=[ENU=Creation DateTime;
                                                              HRV=Datum i vrijeme kreiranja] }
  }
  KEYS
  {
    {    ;Recording Guid                          ;Clustered=Yes }
  }
  FIELDGROUPS
  {
  }
  CODE

    BEGIN
    END.
  }
}

OBJECT Codeunit 50106 BarcodeMgmt
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=16.05.25;
    Time=14:18:50;
    Modified=Yes;
    Version List=PRIMA;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    EventSubscriberInstance=Static-Automatic;
    OnRun=BEGIN
          END;

  }
  CODE
  {
    VAR
      BarcodeItem@50000 : Code[20];
      BarcodeUoM@50001 : Code[10];
      BarcodeQty@50002 : Decimal;
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp@50003 : TEMPORARY Record 50111;
      Rec@50004 : Record 50113;
      ScannedDocumentType@50005 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
      BEMFMgmt@50006 : Codeunit 50107;
      ScannedItem_QtyBuffer@50007 : TEMPORARY Record 50151;
      IsBulkUpdate@50008 : Boolean;
      LineNo@50009 : Integer;

    [External]
    PROCEDURE OnOpenBufferPage@50000(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50002 : Boolean) IsCompletedStatus : Boolean;
    VAR
      WhseShptHdr@50003 : Record 7320;
    BEGIN
      Rec.RESET();

      IF NOT IsBeMobileLocation(DocNo,DocType) THEN
        EXIT;

      Rec.RESET();
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      LineNo := 0;
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      IF Rec.FINDSET() THEN
        Rec.DELETEALL(FALSE); // Delete all from Temp ScannedDocumentLinesBUFFER table

      BEMFMgmt.FindScannedEntries(DocNo,DocType);

      IF SetTempTable(DocNo, DocType) THEN
        EXIT;

      PrepareParentItemFromDoc(DocNo,DocType);
      SortScannedSurplus(DocNo,DocType);

      IsCompletedStatus := ChangeDocStatus(DocNo,DocType,RunChangeDocStatus);

      IF IsCompletedStatus THEN BEGIN
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        //IF WhseShptHdr.GET(DocNo) THEN
        //  BEMFMgmt.DeleteWhseShptBarcodeEntries(WhseShptHdr);
        BEMFMgmt.DeleteBarcodeEntries(DocNo, DocType);
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE PrepareParentItemFromDoc@50002(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50004 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50001 : Record 7321;
      QtyCounter@50002 : Integer;
      RecLineNo@50005 : Integer;
      RecParentlineNo@50006 : Integer;
      RecItemNo@50007 : Code[20];
      Qty@50003 : Decimal;
      UnitofMeasure@50009 : Record 204;
      WarehouseReceiptLine@50008 : Record 7317;
      TransportSheetLine@50010 : Record 50016;
      WhseShpt@50011 : Record 7320;
      CptShpt@50012 : Record 50021;
    BEGIN
      CASE DocType OF
        Rec."Document Type"::Shipment:
        BEGIN
          // Create procedure to get ScannedDocumentLinesTemp with exact doc qty  // Delete curr record from ScannedDocumentLinesTemp // Delete this qty from buffer
          InsertRecForScannedDocumentLineWithQtyOnBarcode(DocNo,DocType); // FIRST TRY TO FIND EXACT SCANNED QTY LIKE DOC QTY
          CalcCumulativeQtyScannedDocumentLinesTemp(DocNo,DocType);

          WarehouseShipmentLine.RESET();
          WarehouseShipmentLine.SETCURRENTKEY("Shipment Date");
          WarehouseShipmentLine.SETRANGE("No.",DocNo);
          WarehouseShipmentLine.SETASCENDING("Shipment Date",TRUE);
          IF WarehouseShipmentLine.FINDSET() THEN
            REPEAT
              CLEAR(Rec);
              Rec.INIT();
              Rec."Document Type" := DocType;
              Rec."Document No." := WarehouseShipmentLine."No.";
              Rec."Line No." := WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.";
              Rec."Parent Line No." := WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.";
              Rec."Document Line No." := WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.";
              Rec.Indentation := 0;
              Rec."Item No." := WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.";
              Rec."Item Description" := WarehouseShipmentLine."Full Item Description";
              // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              //Rec.Quantity := WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity;
              Rec.Quantity := WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship";
              // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              Rec."Scanned Quantity" := 0;
              Rec."Unit of Measure" := WarehouseShipmentLine."Unit of Measure Code";
              Rec."Location Code" := WarehouseShipmentLine."Location Code";
              // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              //Rec."Quantity on Document" := WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity;
              Rec."Quantity on Document" := WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship";
              // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              Rec."Shipment No." := WarehouseShipmentLine."No.";
              Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Incomplete;
              Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";

              IF NOT Rec.GET(Rec."Document Type",Rec."Line No.",Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Line No.",Rec.Version) THEN
                Rec.INSERT(FALSE);

              //Populate local var:
              RecLineNo := Rec."Line No.";
              RecParentlineNo := Rec."Parent Line No.";
              RecItemNo := Rec."Item No.";
              Qty := WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship";

              // I use var instead of Rec due to possible Rec filtering that takes later in the code.
              IF SortChildItemsFromScannedDocumentLines(DocNo,RecItemNo,RecLineNo,DocType,Qty,FALSE) THEN BEGIN// IF NoBOMComponent count parent "Scanned Quantity"
                  Rec.GET(DocType,Rec."Line No.",DocNo,Rec."Line No.",1);
                  SortByQtyFromBuffer(FALSE,Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Type",Rec."Item No.", 1)
                END ELSE
                  SetParentStatusCompleated(DocType,RecParentlineNo,DocNo); // IF Child status is not compleated the parent status will remain Incompleted.

            UNTIL WarehouseShipmentLine.NEXT()=0;
          END;

          Rec."Document Type"::Transport:
          BEGIN
          // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
          // Create procedure to get ScannedDocumentLinesTemp with exact doc qty  // Delete curr record from ScannedDocumentLinesTemp // Delete this qty from buffer
          InsertRecForScannedDocumentLineWithQtyOnBarcode(DocNo,DocType); // FIRST TRY TO FIND EXACT SCANNED QTY LIKE DOC QTY
          CalcCumulativeQtyScannedDocumentLinesTemp(DocNo,DocType);

          TransportSheetLine.RESET();
          TransportSheetLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
          IF TransportSheetLine.FINDSET() THEN
            REPEAT
              CLEAR(Rec);
              Rec.INIT();
              Rec."Document Type" := DocType;
              Rec."Document No." := TransportSheetLine."Document No.";
              Rec."Line No." := TransportSheetLine."Line No.";
              Rec."Parent Line No." := TransportSheetLine."Line No.";
              Rec."Document Line No." := TransportSheetLine."Line No.";
              Rec.Indentation := 0;
              Rec."Item No." := TransportSheetLine."Item No.";
              Rec."Item Description" := TransportSheetLine."Item Description";
              Rec.Quantity := TransportSheetLine.Quantity;
              Rec."Scanned Quantity" := 0;
              Rec."Unit of Measure" := TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure";
              Rec."Location Code" := TransportSheetLine."Location Code";
              Rec."Quantity on Document" := TransportSheetLine.Quantity;
              Rec."Shipment No." := TransportSheetLine."Document No.";
              Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Incomplete;
              Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";

              IF NOT Rec.GET(Rec."Document Type",Rec."Line No.",Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Line No.",Rec.Version) THEN
                Rec.INSERT(FALSE);

              //Populate local var:
              RecLineNo := Rec."Line No.";
              RecParentlineNo := Rec."Parent Line No.";
              RecItemNo := Rec."Item No.";
              Qty := TransportSheetLine.Quantity;

              // I use var instead of Rec due to possible Rec filtering that takes later in the code.
              IF SortChildItemsFromScannedDocumentLines(DocNo,RecItemNo,RecLineNo,DocType,Qty,FALSE) THEN BEGIN// IF NoBOMComponent count parent "Scanned Quantity"
                  Rec.GET(DocType,Rec."Line No.",DocNo,Rec."Line No.",1);
                  SortByQtyFromBuffer(FALSE,Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Type",Rec."Item No.", 1)
                END ELSE
                  SetParentStatusCompleated(DocType,RecParentlineNo,DocNo); // IF Child status is not compleated the parent status will remain Incompleted.

            UNTIL TransportSheetLine.NEXT()=0;
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
          END;
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        Rec."Document Type"::Receipt:
        BEGIN
          // Create procedure to get ScannedDocumentLinesTemp with exact doc qty  // Delete curr record from ScannedDocumentLinesTemp // Delete this qty from buffer
          InsertRecForScannedDocumentLineWithQtyOnBarcode(DocNo,DocType); // FIRST TRY TO FIND EXACT SCANNED QTY LIKE DOC QTY
          CalcCumulativeQtyScannedDocumentLinesTemp(DocNo,DocType);

          WarehouseReceiptLine.RESET();
          WarehouseReceiptLine.SETRANGE("No.",DocNo);
          IF WarehouseReceiptLine.FINDSET() THEN
            REPEAT
              CLEAR(Rec);
              Rec.INIT();
              Rec."Document Type" := DocType;
              Rec."Document No." := WarehouseReceiptLine."No.";
              Rec."Line No." := WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.";
              Rec."Parent Line No." := WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.";
              Rec."Document Line No." := WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.";
              Rec.Indentation := 0;
              Rec."Item No." := WarehouseReceiptLine."Item No.";
              Rec."Item Description" := WarehouseReceiptLine."Full Item Description";
              //Rec.Quantity := WarehouseReceiptLine.Quantity;
              Rec.Quantity := WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive";
              Rec."Scanned Quantity" := 0;
              Rec."Unit of Measure" := WarehouseReceiptLine."Unit of Measure Code";
              Rec."Location Code" := WarehouseReceiptLine."Location Code";
              //Rec."Quantity on Document" := WarehouseReceiptLine.Quantity;
              Rec."Quantity on Document" := WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive";
              Rec."Shipment No." := WarehouseReceiptLine."No.";
              Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Incomplete;
              Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";

              IF NOT Rec.GET(Rec."Document Type",Rec."Line No.",Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Line No.",Rec.Version) THEN
                Rec.INSERT(FALSE);

              //Populate local var:
              RecLineNo := Rec."Line No.";
              RecParentlineNo := Rec."Parent Line No.";
              RecItemNo := Rec."Item No.";
              //Qty := WarehouseReceiptLine.Quantity;
              Qty := WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive";

              // I use var instead of Rec due to possible Rec filtering that takes later in the code.
              IF SortChildItemsFromScannedDocumentLines(DocNo,RecItemNo,RecLineNo,DocType,Qty,FALSE) THEN BEGIN// IF NoBOMComponent count parent "Scanned Quantity"
                  Rec.GET(DocType,Rec."Line No.",DocNo,Rec."Line No.",1);
                  SortByQtyFromBuffer(FALSE,Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Type",Rec."Item No.", 1)
                END ELSE
                  SetParentStatusCompleated(DocType,RecParentlineNo,DocNo); // IF Child status is not compleated the parent status will remain Incompleted.

            UNTIL WarehouseReceiptLine.NEXT()=0;
          END;
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>

        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        Rec."Document Type"::WHCDREC, Rec."Document Type"::WHCDSHIP, Rec."Document Type"::WHCDSHFROM:
        BEGIN
          WhseShpt.GET(DocNo);
          IF (WhseShpt."No. of Pallets" = 0) AND (WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages" = 0) AND (WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages" = 0) AND (WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No." = '') THEN
            EXIT;

          //fpfp CalcCumulQtyScannedDocLinesTempWhseCrossDock(DocNo,DocType);
          CalcCumulativeQtyScannedDocumentLinesTemp(DocNo,DocType);
          CreateWhseShptCrossDockScanned(Rec,WhseShpt,DocType);

          Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
          Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.",Rec."Document No.");
          IF Rec.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
            SortByQtyFromBuffer(FALSE,Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Type",Rec."Item No.", 1)
          UNTIL Rec.NEXT = 0;
        END;

        Rec."Document Type"::CPCDREC, Rec."Document Type"::CPCDSHIP, Rec."Document Type"::CPCDSHFROM:
        BEGIN
          CptShpt.GET(DocNo);

          CalcCumulativeQtyScannedDocumentLinesTemp(DocNo,DocType);
          CreateCptShptCrossDockScanned(Rec,DocNo,DocType);
        END;
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE SortChildItemsFromScannedDocumentLines@50004(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];ItemNo@50004 : Code[20];ParentLineNo@50002 : Integer;DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';QtyOnDoc@50007 : Decimal;SortByExactQtyFromDoc@50009 : Boolean) NoBOMComponent : Boolean;
    VAR
      BOMComponent@50003 : Record 90;
      Counter@50005 : Integer;
      NewLineNo@50006 : Integer;
      ScannedDocumentLinesTempLocal@50008 : TEMPORARY Record 50111;
    BEGIN
      //This function check BOM component's for current item - foreach component get Child Items from ScannedDocumentLines table and sort them to the ScannedDocumentLines BUFFER;
      NewLineNo := ParentLineNo;
      BOMComponent.RESET();
      BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.", ItemNo);
      BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
      IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          NewLineNo += 1;
          ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.RESET();
          ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
          ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
          ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Item No.", BOMComponent."No.");
          IF ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.ISEMPTY() THEN BEGIN  // The BOMComponent is not found in the ScannedDocumentLinesTemp
            Rec.RESET();
            Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type", DocType);
            Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.", DocNo);
            Rec.SETRANGE("Item No.", BOMComponent."No.");
            Rec.SETRANGE("Parent Line No.", ParentLineNo);
            // <ZGNAIMPRRE-10_2>
            //IF NOT Rec.FINDFIRST() AND SortByExactQtyFromDoc THEN BEGIN
            IF NOT Rec.FINDFIRST() THEN BEGIN
            // </ZGNAIMPRRE-10_2>
              NewLineNo += 1;
              Rec."Item No." := BOMComponent."No.";
              Rec."Item Description" := BOMComponent."Full Item Description";
              Rec."Unit of Measure" := BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code";
              Rec.Indentation := 1;
              Rec."Parent Item No." := ItemNo;
              Rec."Parent Line No." := ParentLineNo;
              Rec."Line No." := NewLineNo;
              Rec.Quantity := QtyOnDoc * BOMComponent."Quantity per";
              Rec."Qty per" := BOMComponent."Quantity per";
              Rec."Quantity on Document" := BOMComponent."Quantity per"*QtyOnDoc;
              Rec."Scanned Quantity" := 0;
              Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Missing;
              Rec."Document Line No." := 0;
              Rec."Parent Line No." := ParentLineNo; // Used Parent no. to calculate the status.  // All childs from one parent have parent's "Parent Line No."
              Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";
              Rec.INSERT(FALSE)
            END;
          END ELSE BEGIN // The BOMComponent is FOUND in the ScannedDocumentLinesTemp
            ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.FINDSET();

            Rec.RESET();
            Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type", DocType);
            Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.", DocNo);
            Rec.SETRANGE("Item No.", BOMComponent."No.");
            Rec.SETRANGE("Parent Line No.", ParentLineNo);
            IF Rec.FINDFIRST() THEN BEGIN // Rec with current ScannedDocumentLinesTemp exist do modify.
              Rec.Indentation := 1;
              Rec.Quantity := QtyOnDoc * BOMComponent."Quantity per";
              Rec."Qty per" := BOMComponent."Quantity per";
              Rec."Quantity on Document" := BOMComponent."Quantity per"* QtyOnDoc;
              Rec."Unit of Measure" := BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code";
              Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";
              IF SortByExactQtyFromDoc THEN
                SortByExactDocQty(ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document No.",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document Type",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Item No.",Rec."Quantity on Document")
              ELSE IF (Rec.Status <> Rec.Status::Complete) THEN
                SortByQtyFromBuffer(FALSE,ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document No.",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document Type",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Item No.",1) // Modify
            END ELSE BEGIN
              Rec.TRANSFERFIELDS(ScannedDocumentLinesTemp); // TRANSFER FIELDS FROM var ScannedDocumentLinesTemp TO ScannedDocumentLinesBUFFER Table DO INSERT
              Rec.Indentation := 1;
              Rec."Parent Item No." := ItemNo;
              Rec."Parent Line No." := ParentLineNo;
              Rec."Line No." := NewLineNo;
              Rec.Quantity := QtyOnDoc * BOMComponent."Quantity per";
              Rec."Qty per" := BOMComponent."Quantity per";
              Rec."Quantity on Document" := BOMComponent."Quantity per"*QtyOnDoc;
              Rec."Unit of Measure" := BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code";
              Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Incomplete;
              Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";
              IF SortByExactQtyFromDoc THEN
                SortByExactDocQty(ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document No.",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document Type",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Item No.",Rec."Quantity on Document")
              ELSE
                SortByQtyFromBuffer(TRUE,ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document No.",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document Type",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Item No.",1) // Modify
            END;
          END;
        UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT() = 0
      ELSE // IF Parent don't have BOMComponent
        NoBOMComponent := TRUE;

      EXIT(NoBOMComponent);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetTempTable@50021(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM') ScannedItemEmpty : Boolean;
    VAR
      ScannedDocumentLines@50002 : Record 50111;
      NoScannedItems@50001 : TextConst 'SLV=There are no Scan.DocLines for document %1';
    BEGIN
      //This function inserts data from ScannedDocumentLines to temp var ScannedDocumentLinesTemp
      IF ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.ISTEMPORARY THEN BEGIN
        ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.RESET();
        ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.DELETEALL();
      END;

      ScannedDocumentLines.RESET();
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      IF ScannedDocumentLines.FINDSET() THEN
        REPEAT
          ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.COPY(ScannedDocumentLines);
          ScannedDocumentLines.Processed := TRUE;  //mark scanned item you worked with
          ScannedDocumentLines.Status := ScannedDocumentLines.Status::Incomplete;
          IF ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.ISTEMPORARY THEN
            ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.INSERT(FALSE);
        UNTIL ScannedDocumentLines.NEXT() = 0
      ELSE BEGIN
        ScannedItemEmpty := TRUE;
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE SortScannedSurplus@50031(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50002 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      LastLineNo@50001 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      Rec.RESET();
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      IF Rec.FINDLAST() THEN
        LastLineNo := Rec."Line No.";
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.RESET();
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Document Type", DocType);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETFILTER("Scanned Quantity",'>%1',0);
      IF ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.FINDSET() THEN
        REPEAT
          Rec.TRANSFERFIELDS(ScannedDocumentLinesTemp);
          Rec."Scanned Quantity" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Scanned Quantity";
          Rec.Quantity := 0;
          Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Surplus;
          Rec.Indentation := 0;
          Rec."Line No." := LastLineNo + 10000;
          Rec."Item Description" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Item Description";
          Rec.INSERT(FALSE);
          LastLineNo := Rec."Line No.";
          ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.DELETE();
        UNTIL ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.NEXT() = 0;

      // Reset Temp Buffer Table Filters and display page data.
      Rec.RESET();
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type", DocType);
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.", DocNo);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE SetParentStatusCompleated@50007(DocType@50002 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';LineNo@50005 : Integer;DocNo@50000 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      TotalChilds@50004 : Integer;
      CompleatedChilds@50003 : Integer;
      minQty@50001 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      Rec.RESET();
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      Rec.SETRANGE("Parent Line No.",LineNo);
      Rec.SETRANGE(Indentation,1); // Find Child only
      TotalChilds := Rec.COUNT;
      Rec.SETRANGE(Status, Rec.Status::Complete);
      CompleatedChilds := Rec.COUNT;
      minQty := CalculateMinimumCompletedItems(DocNo,DocType,LineNo);

      IF (TotalChilds = 0) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF (TotalChilds = CompleatedChilds) AND Rec.GET(DocType,LineNo,DocNo,LineNo,1) THEN BEGIN
        Rec."Scanned Quantity" := Rec."Quantity on Document";
        Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Complete;
        Rec.MODIFY(FALSE);
      END ELSE IF Rec.GET(DocType,LineNo,DocNo,LineNo,1) THEN BEGIN// SET SCANNED QTY = 0 FOR PARENT
        Rec."Scanned Quantity" := minQty;
        Rec.MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;

      Rec.RESET();
    END;

    PROCEDURE ChangeDocStatus@50012(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50015 : Boolean) isCompleated : Boolean;
    VAR
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50002 : Record 7321;
      WarehouseShipmentHeader@50003 : Record 7320;
      TotalScannedItems@50004 : Integer;
      CompleatedScanneditems@50005 : Integer;
      MissingScannedItems@50006 : Integer;
      SurplusScannedItems@50007 : Integer;
      CalculatedParentQty@50008 : Integer;
      SurplusText@50010 : Text[250];
      MissingDialogMessage@50012 : TextConst 'ENU=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoje nepodudaranja u skeniranim stavkama, molim provjerite skenirane stavke. Ako ste sigurni da ßelite aßurirati koliüine na dokumentu odaberite Da.;SLV=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
      UserSetup@50013 : Record 91;
      AdminConfirmMessage@50014 : TextConst 'ENU=The action is allowed to the "Scanned Barcodes Admin".;HRV=Akcija je dozvoljena samo za barkod administratora.;SLV=The action is allowed to the "Scanned Barcodes Admin".';
      SurplusDialogMessage@50016 : TextConst 'ENU=There is a surplus of scanned items (%1), please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoji viÁak skeniranih artikala (%1). Ako ste veÜ provjerili skenirane stavke i ßelite sinkronizirati koliüinu, pritisnite Da.;SLV=There is a surplus of scanned items (%1), please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      // code from ChangeDocStatus moved to ChangeWhseShipmentStatus to make ChangeDocStatus uniform
      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::Shipment:
          isCompleated := ChangeWhsShipmentStatus(DocNo, DocType, RunChangeDocStatus);
        DocType::Receipt:
          isCompleated := ChangeWhseReceiptStatus(DocNo, DocType, RunChangeDocStatus);
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        DocType::Transport:
          isCompleated := ChangeTransportSheetStatus(DocNo, DocType, RunChangeDocStatus);
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        DocType::WHCDSHIP, DocType::WHCDREC, DocType::WHCDSHFROM:
          isCompleated := ChangeWhsShipmentStatusCD(DocNo, DocType, RunChangeDocStatus);

        DocType::CPCDSHIP, DocType::CPCDREC, DocType::CPCDSHFROM:
          isCompleated := ChangeComplaintShipmentStatusCD(DocNo, DocType, RunChangeDocStatus);
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Codeunit,5764,OnBeforeConfirmWhseShipmentPost)]
    LOCAL PROCEDURE DisableWarehouseShipmentPost@50018(VAR WhseShptLine@50000 : Record 7321;VAR HideDialog@50001 : Boolean;VAR Invoice@50002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WarehouseShipmentHeader@50003 : Record 7320;
      NotReadyForShip@50004 : TextConst 'SLV=Posting is disabled if "Ready for shipment to cross doc is not enabled".';
    BEGIN
      EXIT; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>

      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(WhseShptLine."No.") AND
        NOT WarehouseShipmentHeader."Ready For Shipment" THEN
            ERROR(NotReadyForShip);
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,50111,OnBeforeModifyEvent)]
    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertArchiveOnBeforeModify@50005(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 50111;VAR xRec@50001 : Record 50111;RunTrigger@50002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive@50003 : Record 50112;
      version@50004 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      IF NOT RunTrigger THEN
        EXIT;
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.RESET();
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.SETRANGE("Document Type", Rec."Document Type");
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.SETRANGE("Document No.", Rec."Document No.");
      version := ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.COUNT();

      IF ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.FINDLAST AND (ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.Version > version) THEN
        version := ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.Version;

      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.TRANSFERFIELDS(xRec);
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.Version := version +1;
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.ArchiveDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME;
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive."Arcived by User" := USERID();
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.INSERT();
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,50111,OnBeforeDeleteEvent)]
    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertArchiveOnBeforeDelete@50010(VAR Rec@50001 : Record 50111;RunTrigger@50002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive@50000 : Record 50112;
      version@50003 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      IF NOT RunTrigger THEN
        EXIT;
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.RESET();
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.SETRANGE("Document Type", Rec."Document Type");
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.SETRANGE("Document No.", Rec."Document No.");
      version := ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.COUNT();

      IF ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.FINDLAST AND (ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.Version > version) THEN
        version := ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.Version;

      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.TRANSFERFIELDS(Rec);
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.Version := version +1;
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.ArchiveDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME;
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive."Arcived by User" := USERID();
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.INSERT();
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,7320,OnBeforeDeleteEvent)]
    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertArchiveOnBeforePostOrDeleteWarehouseShipment@50008(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 7320;RunTrigger@50001 : Boolean);
    VAR
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive@50002 : Record 50112;
      ScannedDocumentLines@50003 : Record 50111;
      version@50004 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      //after posting the document Warehouse Shipment is deleted - this subscriber will be triggered.
      IF NOT RunTrigger THEN
        EXIT;

      ScannedDocumentLines.RESET();
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document No.", Rec."No.");
      version := ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.COUNT();

      IF ScannedDocumentLines.FINDSET() THEN
        REPEAT
          ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.TRANSFERFIELDS(ScannedDocumentLines);
          ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.Version := version +1;
          ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.ArchiveDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME;
          ScannedDocumentLinesArchive."Arcived by User" := USERID();
          ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.INSERT();
        UNTIL ScannedDocumentLines.NEXT() = 0;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CalculateMinimumCompletedItems@50015(DocNo@50002 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';CurrentItemLineNo@50001 : Integer) MinAvailableParentQuantity : Decimal;
    VAR
      LastQuantity@50003 : Decimal;
      CurrentQuantity@50004 : Decimal;
      lastMinValue@50005 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      Rec.RESET();
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type", DocType);
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      Rec.SETRANGE("Parent Line No.", CurrentItemLineNo);
      Rec.SETRANGE(Indentation,1); // Sort Childs
      IF Rec.FINDSET() THEN
        REPEAT
          lastMinValue := MinAvailableParentQuantity;
          IF (MinAvailableParentQuantity = 0) THEN
            MinAvailableParentQuantity := Rec."Scanned Quantity" DIV Rec."Qty per"
          ELSE IF (MinAvailableParentQuantity > Rec."Scanned Quantity" DIV Rec."Qty per") THEN
            MinAvailableParentQuantity := Rec."Scanned Quantity" DIV Rec."Qty per";
          IF lastMinValue > MinAvailableParentQuantity THEN
            lastMinValue := MinAvailableParentQuantity
         UNTIL Rec.NEXT()= 0
      ELSE // if there was no child
        lastMinValue := 1;

      EXIT(lastMinValue);
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,7321,OnAfterDeleteEvent)]
    LOCAL PROCEDURE ChangeWarehouseShipmentStatusOnAfterDeleteLine@50006(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 7321;RunTrigger@50001 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WarehouseShipmentHeader@50002 : Record 7320;
    BEGIN
      IF NOT WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(Rec."No.") THEN
        EXIT;
      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN BEGIN
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        //WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" := WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
        WarehouseShipmentHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized);
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        WarehouseShipmentHeader.MODIFY();
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CalcCumulativeQtyScannedDocumentLinesTemp@50003(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    BEGIN
      ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.RESET();
      ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.DELETEALL();

      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.RESET();
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETFILTER("Scanned Quantity",'>%1',0);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETFILTER(Status,'<>%1',ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.Status::Complete);
      IF ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.FINDSET() THEN
        REPEAT
          IF ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.GET(ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document Type",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document No.",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Item No.") THEN BEGIN
            ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.Qty_Cumulative := ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.Qty_Cumulative + ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Scanned Quantity";
            ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.MODIFY(FALSE);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.INIT();
            ScannedItem_QtyBuffer."Document Type" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document Type";
            ScannedItem_QtyBuffer."Document No." := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document No.";
            ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.ItemNo := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Item No.";
            ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.Qty_Cumulative := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Scanned Quantity";
            ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.INSERT(FALSE);
            END;
            ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Scanned Quantity" := 0; // Set All Qty = 0
            ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.MODIFY(FALSE);
          UNTIL ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.NEXT()= 0;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE NeedInsertSurplus@50013(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RecItemNo@50002 : Code[40]) InsertRecord : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.RESET();
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Item No.",RecItemNo);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETFILTER(Status,'<>%1',ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.Status::Complete);
      IF NOT ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.FINDFIRST() THEN
        InsertRecord := TRUE;

      EXIT (InsertRecord);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertRecForScannedDocumentLineWithQtyOnBarcode@50014(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM') : Boolean;
    VAR
      UnitofMeasure@50004 : Record 204;
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50005 : Record 7321;
      WarehouseReceiptLine@50002 : Record 7317;
      TransportSheetLine@50003 : Record 50016;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::Shipment:
          BEGIN
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
            WarehouseShipmentLine.RESET();
            WarehouseShipmentLine.SETCURRENTKEY("Shipment Date");
            WarehouseShipmentLine.SETRANGE("No.",DocNo);
            WarehouseShipmentLine.SETASCENDING("Shipment Date",TRUE);
            IF WarehouseShipmentLine.FINDSET() THEN REPEAT
              CLEAR(Rec);
              Rec.INIT();
              Rec."Document Type" := DocType;
              Rec."Document No." := WarehouseShipmentLine."No.";
              Rec."Line No." := WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.";
              Rec."Parent Line No." := WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.";
              Rec."Document Line No." := WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.";
              Rec.Indentation := 0;
              Rec."Item No." := WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.";
              Rec."Item Description" := WarehouseShipmentLine."Full Item Description";
              // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              //Rec.Quantity := WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity;
              Rec.Quantity := WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship";
              // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              Rec."Scanned Quantity" := 0;
              Rec."Unit of Measure" := WarehouseShipmentLine."Unit of Measure Code";
              Rec."Location Code" := WarehouseShipmentLine."Location Code";
              // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              //Rec."Quantity on Document" := WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity;
              Rec."Quantity on Document" := WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship";
              // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              Rec."Shipment No." := WarehouseShipmentLine."No.";
              Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Incomplete;
              Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";
              // Check if UoM Insert Qty Barcode boolean if true then find exact doc qty from scanned items and insert.
              IF (UnitofMeasure.GET(Rec."Unit of Measure")) AND ((UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode")) THEN
                // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                //IF SortChildItemsFromScannedDocumentLines(DocNo,Rec."Item No.",Rec."Line No.",DocType,WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity,TRUE) THEN  // IF NoBOMComponent count parent "Scanned Quantity"
                IF SortChildItemsFromScannedDocumentLines(DocNo,Rec."Item No.",Rec."Line No.",DocType,WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship",TRUE) THEN  // IF NoBOMComponent count parent "Scanned Quantity"
                // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                  SortByExactDocQty(Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Type",Rec."Item No.",Rec.Quantity)
                ELSE
                  SetParentStatusCompleated(DocType,Rec."Parent Line No.",DocNo); // IF Child status is not compleated the parent status will remain Incompleted.

            UNTIL WarehouseShipmentLine.NEXT()=0;
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
          END;
        DocType::Receipt:
          BEGIN
            WarehouseReceiptLine.RESET();
            WarehouseReceiptLine.SETRANGE("No.",DocNo);
            IF WarehouseReceiptLine.FINDSET() THEN REPEAT
              CLEAR(Rec);
              Rec.INIT();
              Rec."Document Type" := DocType;
              Rec."Document No." := WarehouseReceiptLine."No.";
              Rec."Line No." := WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.";
              Rec."Parent Line No." := WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.";
              Rec."Document Line No." := WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.";
              Rec.Indentation := 0;
              Rec."Item No." := WarehouseReceiptLine."Item No.";
              Rec."Item Description" := WarehouseReceiptLine."Full Item Description";
              //Rec.Quantity := WarehouseReceiptLine.Quantity;
              Rec.Quantity := WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive";
              Rec."Scanned Quantity" := 0;
              Rec."Unit of Measure" := WarehouseReceiptLine."Unit of Measure Code";
              Rec."Location Code" := WarehouseReceiptLine."Location Code";
              //Rec."Quantity on Document" := WarehouseReceiptLine.Quantity;
              Rec."Quantity on Document" := WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive";
              Rec."Shipment No." := WarehouseReceiptLine."No.";
              Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Incomplete;
              Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";
              // Check if UoM Insert Qty Barcode boolean if true then find exact doc qty from scanned items and insert.
              IF (UnitofMeasure.GET(Rec."Unit of Measure")) AND ((UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode")) THEN
                //IF SortChildItemsFromScannedDocumentLines(DocNo,Rec."Item No.",Rec."Line No.",DocType,WarehouseReceiptLine.Quantity,TRUE) THEN  // IF NoBOMComponent count parent "Scanned Quantity"
                IF SortChildItemsFromScannedDocumentLines(DocNo,Rec."Item No.",Rec."Line No.",DocType,WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive",TRUE) THEN  // IF NoBOMComponent count parent "Scanned Quantity"
                  SortByExactDocQty(Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Type",Rec."Item No.",Rec.Quantity)
                ELSE
                  SetParentStatusCompleated(DocType,Rec."Parent Line No.",DocNo); // IF Child status is not compleated the parent status will remain Incompleted.

            UNTIL WarehouseReceiptLine.NEXT()=0;
          END;
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        DocType::Transport:
          BEGIN
            TransportSheetLine.RESET();
            TransportSheetLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
            IF TransportSheetLine.FINDSET() THEN REPEAT
              CLEAR(Rec);
              Rec.INIT();
              Rec."Document Type" := DocType;
              Rec."Document No." := TransportSheetLine."Document No.";
              Rec."Line No." := TransportSheetLine."Line No.";
              Rec."Parent Line No." := TransportSheetLine."Line No.";
              Rec."Document Line No." := TransportSheetLine."Line No.";
              Rec.Indentation := 0;
              Rec."Item No." := TransportSheetLine."Item No.";
              Rec."Item Description" := TransportSheetLine."Item Description";
              Rec.Quantity := TransportSheetLine.Quantity;
              Rec."Scanned Quantity" := 0;
              Rec."Unit of Measure" := TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure";
              Rec."Location Code" := TransportSheetLine."Location Code";
              Rec."Quantity on Document" := TransportSheetLine.Quantity;
              Rec."Shipment No." := TransportSheetLine."Document No.";
              Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Incomplete;
              Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";
              // Check if UoM Insert Qty Barcode boolean if true then find exact doc qty from scanned items and insert.
              IF (UnitofMeasure.GET(Rec."Unit of Measure")) AND ((UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode")) THEN
                IF SortChildItemsFromScannedDocumentLines(DocNo,Rec."Item No.",Rec."Line No.",DocType,TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Receive",TRUE) THEN  // IF NoBOMComponent count parent "Scanned Quantity"
                  SortByExactDocQty(Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Type",Rec."Item No.",Rec.Quantity)
                ELSE
                  SetParentStatusCompleated(DocType,Rec."Parent Line No.",DocNo); // IF Child status is not compleated the parent status will remain Incompleted.

            UNTIL WarehouseReceiptLine.NEXT()=0;
          END;
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE SortByQtyFromBuffer@50011(IsInsert@50005 : Boolean;DocNo@50003 : Code[20];DocType@50002 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';ItemNo@50001 : Code[40];Indentation@50004 : Integer) : Boolean;
    VAR
      ScannedDocumentLinesTempLocal@50000 : TEMPORARY Record 50111;
    BEGIN
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.RESET();
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Item No.",ItemNo);
      {ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETFILTER(Status,'<>%1',ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.Status::Complete);}
      IF ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.FINDSET() {AND (Rec.GET(DocType,Rec."Line No.",DocNo,Rec."Line No.",Indentation))} THEN BEGIN
        IF ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.GET(ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document Type",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document No.",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Item No.") THEN
          IF Rec.Quantity <= ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.Qty_Cumulative THEN BEGIN // if there are enough scanned quantities
            Rec."Scanned Quantity" := Rec.Quantity;
            Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Complete;
          END ELSE
            Rec."Scanned Quantity" := ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.Qty_Cumulative ;// if there is NOT enough scanned quantities

        IF IsInsert THEN
          Rec.INSERT(FALSE)
        ELSE
          Rec.MODIFY(FALSE);

        ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.Qty_Cumulative  := ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.Qty_Cumulative - Rec."Scanned Quantity";
        ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.MODIFY(FALSE); // reduce the amount in the buffer

        ScannedDocumentLinesTempLocal.TRANSFERFIELDS(ScannedDocumentLinesTemp);
        ScannedDocumentLinesTempLocal."Scanned Quantity" := ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.Qty_Cumulative;
        IF ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.GET(ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document Type",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Line No.",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document No.",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Document Line No.",ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.Version) THEN
          ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.DELETE(FALSE); // Delete the used item

        IF (ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.Qty_Cumulative > 0) THEN // if surplus exist
          IF NeedInsertSurplus(ScannedDocumentLinesTempLocal."Document No.",ScannedDocumentLinesTempLocal."Document Type",ScannedDocumentLinesTempLocal."Item No.") THEN BEGIN // No items in ScannedDocumentLinesTemp table
            ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.INIT();
            ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.TRANSFERFIELDS(ScannedDocumentLinesTempLocal);
            ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.INSERT(FALSE);
          END ELSE BEGIN
            ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."Scanned Quantity" := ScannedItem_QtyBuffer.Qty_Cumulative;
            ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.MODIFY(FALSE);
          END;

          EXIT(TRUE);
      END;

      EXIT(FALSE);
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE SortByExactDocQty@50016(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';ItemNo@50002 : Code[20];Qty@50003 : Decimal);
    BEGIN
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.RESET();
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Item No.", ItemNo);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETRANGE("Scanned Quantity",Qty);
      ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.SETFILTER(Status,'<>%1',ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.Status::Complete);

      IF ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.FINDFIRST() THEN BEGIN
        Rec."Scanned Quantity" := Rec.Quantity;
        Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Complete;
        IF NOT Rec.GET(Rec."Document Type",Rec."Line No.",Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Line No.",Rec.Version) THEN
          Rec.INSERT(FALSE)
        ELSE
          Rec.MODIFY(FALSE);

        ScannedDocumentLinesTemp.DELETE(FALSE);
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsBeMobileLocation@50001(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM') : Boolean;
    VAR
      Location@50002 : Record 14;
      WarehouseShipmentHeader@50003 : Record 7320;
      WarehouseReceiptHeader@50004 : Record 7316;
      TransportSheetHeader@50005 : Record 50015;
      ResponsibilityCenter@50006 : Record 5714;
      ComplaintShpt@50007 : Record 50021;
    BEGIN
      CASE DocType OF
       Rec."Document Type"::Shipment:
        BEGIN
          IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN
            IF Location.GET(WarehouseShipmentHeader."Location Code") AND Location."Use in BE-Mobile (shipment)" THEN
              EXIT(TRUE);
        END;
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        Rec."Document Type"::Receipt:
        BEGIN
          IF WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN
            IF Location.GET(WarehouseReceiptHeader."Location Code") AND Location."Use in BE-Mobile (receipt)" THEN
              EXIT(TRUE);
        END;
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        Rec."Document Type"::Transport:
        BEGIN
          IF TransportSheetHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN
            IF Location.GET(TransportSheetHeader."Receiving Location") AND Location."Use in BE-Mobile (receipt)" THEN
              EXIT(TRUE);
        END;
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        Rec."Document Type"::WHCDSHIP,Rec."Document Type"::WHCDREC,Rec."Document Type"::WHCDSHFROM:
          BEGIN
            IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN
              IF Location.GET(WarehouseShipmentHeader."Location Code") AND Location."Use Cross-Dock Warehouse" THEN
                EXIT(TRUE);
          END;
        Rec."Document Type"::CPCDSHIP,Rec."Document Type"::CPCDREC,Rec."Document Type"::CPCDSHFROM:
          IF ComplaintShpt.GET(DocNo) AND ResponsibilityCenter.GET(ComplaintShpt."Store Responsible Center") AND ResponsibilityCenter."Use Cross-Dock Warehouse" THEN
            EXIT(TRUE);
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE ChangeWhsShipmentStatus@50034(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50015 : Boolean) isCompleated : Boolean;
    VAR
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50002 : Record 7321;
      WarehouseShipmentHeader@50003 : Record 7320;
      TotalScannedItems@50004 : Integer;
      CompleatedScanneditems@50005 : Integer;
      MissingScannedItems@50006 : Integer;
      SurplusScannedItems@50007 : Integer;
      CalculatedParentQty@50008 : Integer;
      SurplusText@50010 : Text[250];
      MissingDialogMessage@50012 : TextConst 'ENU=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoje nepodudaranja u skeniranim stavkama, molim provjerite skenirane stavke. Ako ste sigurni da ßelite aßurirati koliüine na dokumentu odaberite Da.;SLV=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
      UserSetup@50013 : Record 91;
      AdminConfirmMessage@50014 : TextConst 'ENU=The action is allowed to the "Scanned Barcodes Admin".;HRV=Akcija je dozvoljena samo za barkod administratora.;SLV=The action is allowed to the "Scanned Barcodes Admin".';
      SurplusDialogMessage@50016 : TextConst 'ENU=There is a surplus of scanned items (%1), please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoji viÁak skeniranih artikala (%1). Ako ste veÜ provjerili skenirane stavke i ßelite sinkronizirati koliüinu, pritisnite Da.;SLV=There is a surplus of scanned items (%1), please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059> - move code from ChangeDocStatus to ChangeWhsShipmentStatus to make it uniform
      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN BEGIN
        IF (WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed) THEN
          EXIT;
        IF WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized THEN BEGIN
          // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
          //WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" := WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
          WarehouseShipmentHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized);
          // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
          WarehouseShipmentHeader.MODIFY();
        END;
      END;

      Rec.RESET();
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      TotalScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();           // Count Totals Items

      Rec.SETRANGE(Status, Rec.Status::Complete); // Count Completed
      CompleatedScanneditems := Rec.COUNT();

      IF (CompleatedScanneditems <> TotalScannedItems) AND NOT RunChangeDocStatus THEN
        EXIT;

      Rec.SETFILTER(Status,'%1|%2', Rec.Status::Incomplete,Rec.Status::Missing); // Count Missing
      MissingScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();

      Rec.SETRANGE(Status, Rec.Status::Surplus); // Count Surplus
      SurplusScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();

      isCompleated := CompleatedScanneditems = TotalScannedItems;

      IF NOT isCompleated AND NOT IsBulkUpdate THEN
        IF ((MissingScannedItems > 0) OR (SurplusScannedItems > 0)) AND GUIALLOWED THEN
          IF (DIALOG.CONFIRM(MissingDialogMessage, FALSE, MissingScannedItems)) THEN BEGIN
            UserSetup.GET(USERID());
            UserSetup.TESTFIELD("Scanned Barcodes Admin",TRUE);
            isCompleated := TRUE;
          END;

      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(DocNo) AND (NOT isCompleated) THEN BEGIN
        IF (WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized) OR (WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship" <> Rec."Scanned Quantity") THEN BEGIN // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
          // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
          //WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" := WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
          WarehouseShipmentHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized);
          // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
          // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
          //WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship" := Rec."Scanned Quantity";
          // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
          IF WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. Outstanding" <= Rec."Scanned Quantity" THEN
            WarehouseShipmentLine.VALIDATE("Qty. to Ship", WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. Outstanding")
          ELSE
          // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
            WarehouseShipmentLine.VALIDATE("Qty. to Ship", Rec."Scanned Quantity");
          // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
          WarehouseShipmentHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
        END; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
      END ELSE BEGIN
        Rec.RESET();
        Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type", DocType);
        Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.", DocNo);
        Rec.SETRANGE(Indentation,0);
        IF Rec.FINDSET() AND isCompleated THEN BEGIN
          //Foreach Line from Rec GET WarehouseShipmentLine and validate QTY
          REPEAT
            IF WarehouseShipmentLine.GET(DocNo, Rec."Document Line No.") THEN BEGIN
              IF (WarehouseShipmentLine."Scanned Quantity" <> Rec."Scanned Quantity") OR (WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship" <> Rec."Scanned Quantity") THEN BEGIN // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
                WarehouseShipmentLine."Scanned Quantity" := Rec."Scanned Quantity";
                // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                //WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship" := Rec."Scanned Quantity";
                // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
                IF WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. Outstanding" <= Rec."Scanned Quantity" THEN
                  WarehouseShipmentLine.VALIDATE("Qty. to Ship", WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. Outstanding")
                ELSE
                // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
                  WarehouseShipmentLine.VALIDATE("Qty. to Ship", Rec."Scanned Quantity");
                // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                WarehouseShipmentLine.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
            END
          UNTIL Rec.NEXT() = 0;
          // Set Warehouse Status Processed
          IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN BEGIN
            IF WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN BEGIN // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
              // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
              //WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" := WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed;
              WarehouseShipmentHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed);
              // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
              WarehouseShipmentHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
            END; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
          END;
        END;
      END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
    END;

    PROCEDURE ChangeWhseReceiptStatus@50035(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50015 : Boolean) isCompleated : Boolean;
    VAR
      WarehouseReceiptLine@50002 : Record 7317;
      WarehouseReceiptHeader@50003 : Record 7316;
      TotalScannedItems@50004 : Integer;
      CompleatedScanneditems@50005 : Integer;
      MissingScannedItems@50006 : Integer;
      SurplusScannedItems@50007 : Integer;
      CalculatedParentQty@50008 : Integer;
      SurplusText@50010 : Text[250];
      MissingDialogMessage@50012 : TextConst 'ENU=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoje nepodudaranja u skeniranim stavkama, molim provjerite skenirane stavke. Ako ste sigurni da ßelite aßurirati koliüine na dokumentu odaberite Da.;SLV=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
      UserSetup@50013 : Record 91;
      AdminConfirmMessage@50014 : TextConst 'ENU=The action is allowed to the "Scanned Barcodes Admin".;HRV=Akcija je dozvoljena samo za barkod administratora.;SLV=The action is allowed to the "Scanned Barcodes Admin".';
      SurplusDialogMessage@50016 : TextConst 'ENU=There is a surplus of scanned items (%1), please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoji viÁak skeniranih artikala (%1). Ako ste veÜ provjerili skenirane stavke i ßelite sinkronizirati koliüinu, pritisnite Da.;SLV=There is a surplus of scanned items (%1), please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059> - copy of function ChangeWhseShipmentStatus
      IF WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN BEGIN
        IF (WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed) THEN
          EXIT;

        IF WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized THEN BEGIN // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
          // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
          //WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" := WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
          WarehouseReceiptHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized);
          // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
          WarehouseReceiptHeader.MODIFY();
        END; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
      END;

      Rec.RESET();
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      TotalScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();           // Count Totals Items

      Rec.SETRANGE(Status, Rec.Status::Complete); // Count Completed
      CompleatedScanneditems := Rec.COUNT();

      IF (CompleatedScanneditems <> TotalScannedItems) AND NOT RunChangeDocStatus THEN
        EXIT;

      Rec.SETFILTER(Status,'%1|%2', Rec.Status::Incomplete,Rec.Status::Missing); // Count Missing
      MissingScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();

      Rec.SETRANGE(Status, Rec.Status::Surplus); // Count Surplus
      SurplusScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();

      IF CompleatedScanneditems = TotalScannedItems THEN
        isCompleated := TRUE
      ELSE BEGIN
        IF ((MissingScannedItems > 0) OR (SurplusScannedItems > 0)) AND GUIALLOWED THEN
          IF NOT IsBulkUpdate THEN
            IF (DIALOG.CONFIRM(MissingDialogMessage, FALSE, MissingScannedItems)) THEN BEGIN
              UserSetup.GET(USERID());
              UserSetup.TESTFIELD("Scanned Barcodes Admin",TRUE);
              isCompleated := TRUE;
            END;
      END;

      IF WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(DocNo) AND (NOT isCompleated) THEN BEGIN
        IF (WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized) OR (WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive" <> Rec."Scanned Quantity") THEN BEGIN // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
          // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
          //WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" := WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
          WarehouseReceiptHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized);
          // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
          // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
          //WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive" := Rec."Scanned Quantity";
          // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
          IF WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. Outstanding" <= Rec."Scanned Quantity" THEN
            WarehouseReceiptLine.VALIDATE("Qty. to Receive", WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. Outstanding")
          ELSE
          // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
            WarehouseReceiptLine.VALIDATE("Qty. to Receive", Rec."Scanned Quantity");
          // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
          WarehouseReceiptHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
        END;// <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
      END ELSE BEGIN
        Rec.RESET();
        Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type", DocType);
        Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.", DocNo);
        Rec.SETRANGE(Indentation,0);
        IF Rec.FINDSET() AND isCompleated THEN BEGIN
          //Foreach Line from Rec GET WarehouseReceiptLine and validate QTY
          REPEAT
            IF WarehouseReceiptLine.GET(DocNo, Rec."Document Line No.") THEN BEGIN
              IF (WarehouseReceiptLine."Scanned Quantity" <> Rec."Scanned Quantity") OR (WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive" <> Rec."Scanned Quantity") THEN BEGIN // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
                WarehouseReceiptLine."Scanned Quantity" := Rec."Scanned Quantity";
                // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                //WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive" := Rec."Scanned Quantity";
                // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
                IF WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. Outstanding" <= Rec."Scanned Quantity" THEN
                  WarehouseReceiptLine.VALIDATE("Qty. to Receive", WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. Outstanding")
                ELSE
                // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
                  WarehouseReceiptLine.VALIDATE("Qty. to Receive", Rec."Scanned Quantity");
                // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>;
                WarehouseReceiptLine.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;// <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
            END
          UNTIL Rec.NEXT() = 0;
          // Set Warehouse Status Processed
          IF WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN BEGIN
            IF WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN BEGIN // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
              // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
              //WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" := WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed;
              WarehouseReceiptHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed);
              // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
              WarehouseReceiptHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
            END; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
          END;
        END;
      END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Codeunit,5760,OnBeforeRun)]
    LOCAL PROCEDURE DisableWarehouseReceiptPost@50019(VAR WarehouseReceiptLine@50001 : Record 7317);
    VAR
      WarehouseReceiptHeader@50000 : Record 7316;
      Error001@50002 : TextConst 'ENU=It is not allowed to post Warehouse Receipt if Barcode status is not Procesed.;HRV=Nije moguÜe knjißiti SkladiÁnu primku ako je barkod status razliüit od procesuirano.';
      Location@50003 : Record 14;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(WarehouseReceiptLine."No.");
      IF Location.GET(WarehouseReceiptHeader."Location Code") THEN;
      IF Location."Use in BE-Mobile (receipt)" AND (WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed) THEN
        ERROR(Error001);
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,7316,OnBeforeDeleteEvent)]
    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertArchiveOnBeforePostOrDeleteWarehouseReceipt@50020(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 7316;RunTrigger@50001 : Boolean);
    VAR
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive@50002 : Record 50112;
      ScannedDocumentLines@50003 : Record 50111;
      version@50004 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      ScannedDocumentLines.RESET();
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document No.", Rec."No.");
      version := ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.COUNT();

      IF ScannedDocumentLines.FINDSET() THEN
        REPEAT
          ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.TRANSFERFIELDS(ScannedDocumentLines);
          ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.Version := version +1;
          ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.ArchiveDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME;
          ScannedDocumentLinesArchive."Arcived by User" := USERID();
          ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.INSERT();
        UNTIL ScannedDocumentLines.NEXT() = 0;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,7317,OnAfterDeleteEvent)]
    LOCAL PROCEDURE ChangeWarehouseReceiptStatusOnAfterDeleteLine@50024(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 7317;RunTrigger@50001 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WarehouseReceiptHeader@50002 : Record 7316;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      IF NOT RunTrigger THEN
        EXIT;

      IF NOT WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(Rec."No.") THEN
        EXIT;
      IF WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN BEGIN
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        //WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" := WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
        WarehouseReceiptHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized);
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        WarehouseReceiptHeader.MODIFY();
      END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsCrossDockLocation@50028(LocationCode@50000 : Code[20]) : Boolean;
    VAR
      Location@50002 : Record 14;
    BEGIN
      EXIT(Location.GET(LocationCode) AND Location."Use Cross-Dock Warehouse"); // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246/>
    END;

    PROCEDURE ChangeTransportSheetStatus@50022(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt';RunChangeDocStatus@50015 : Boolean) isCompleated : Boolean;
    VAR
      TotalScannedItems@50004 : Integer;
      CompleatedScanneditems@50005 : Integer;
      MissingScannedItems@50006 : Integer;
      SurplusScannedItems@50007 : Integer;
      CalculatedParentQty@50008 : Integer;
      SurplusText@50010 : Text[250];
      MissingDialogMessage@50012 : TextConst 'ENU=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoje nepodudaranja u skeniranim stavkama, molim provjerite skenirane stavke. Ako ste sigurni da ßelite aßurirati koliüine na dokumentu odaberite Da.;SLV=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
      UserSetup@50013 : Record 91;
      AdminConfirmMessage@50014 : TextConst 'ENU=The action is allowed to the "Scanned Barcodes Admin".;HRV=Akcija je dozvoljena samo za barkod administratora.;SLV=The action is allowed to the "Scanned Barcodes Admin".';
      SurplusDialogMessage@50016 : TextConst 'ENU=There is a surplus of scanned items (%1), please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoji viÁak skeniranih artikala (%1). Ako ste veÜ provjerili skenirane stavke i ßelite sinkronizirati koliüinu, pritisnite Da.;SLV=There is a surplus of scanned items (%1), please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
      TransportSheetHeader@50002 : Record 50015;
      TransportSheetLine@50003 : Record 50016;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      IF NOT TransportSheetHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF (TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status" = TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed) THEN
        EXIT(TRUE);
      IF TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status" <> TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized THEN BEGIN
        TransportSheetHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized);
        TransportSheetHeader.MODIFY();
      END;

      Rec.RESET();
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      TotalScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();           // Count Totals Items

      Rec.SETRANGE(Status, Rec.Status::Complete); // Count Completed
      CompleatedScanneditems := Rec.COUNT();

      Rec.SETFILTER(Status,'%1|%2', Rec.Status::Incomplete,Rec.Status::Missing); // Count Missing
      MissingScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();

      Rec.SETRANGE(Status, Rec.Status::Surplus); // Count Surplus
      SurplusScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();

      Rec.SETRANGE(Status);
      Rec.SETRANGE(Indentation,0);
      IF Rec.FINDSET() THEN
        REPEAT
          TransportSheetLine.GET(TransportSheetHeader."Document No.",Rec."Document Line No.");
          IF TransportSheetLine."Scanned Quantity" <> Rec."Scanned Quantity" THEN BEGIN
            TransportSheetLine."Scanned Quantity" := Rec."Scanned Quantity";
            TransportSheetLine.MODIFY(FALSE);
          END
        UNTIL Rec.NEXT() = 0;

      IF (CompleatedScanneditems <> TotalScannedItems) AND NOT RunChangeDocStatus THEN
        EXIT;

      IF CompleatedScanneditems = TotalScannedItems THEN
        isCompleated := TRUE
      ELSE IF ((MissingScannedItems > 0) OR (SurplusScannedItems > 0)) AND NOT IsBulkUpdate AND GUIALLOWED THEN
        IF (DIALOG.CONFIRM(MissingDialogMessage, FALSE, MissingScannedItems)) THEN BEGIN
          UserSetup.GET(USERID());
          UserSetup.TESTFIELD("Scanned Barcodes Admin",TRUE);
          isCompleated := TRUE;
        END;

      IF NOT isCompleated THEN BEGIN
        IF TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status" <> TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized THEN BEGIN
          TransportSheetHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized);
          TransportSheetHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
        END;
      END ELSE  // Set Status Processed
        IF TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status" <> TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN BEGIN
          TransportSheetHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed);
          TransportSheetHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
        END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateWhseShptCrossDockScanned@50025(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 50113;WhseShpt@50002 : Record 7320;DocType@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      i@50001 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      FOR i:=1 TO WhseShpt."No. of Pallets" DO BEGIN
        LineNo += 10000;
        CLEAR(Rec);
        Rec.INIT();
        Rec."Document Type" := DocType;
        Rec."Document No." := WhseShpt."No.";
        Rec."Line No." := LineNo;
        Rec."Parent Line No." := LineNo;
        Rec."Document Line No." := LineNo;
        Rec.Indentation := 0;
        Rec."Item No." := BEMFMgmt.GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.",i,WhseShpt."No. of Pallets",1);
        Rec."Item Description" := BEMFMgmt.GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.",i,WhseShpt."No. of Pallets",2);
        Rec.Quantity := 1;
        Rec."Scanned Quantity" := 0;
        Rec."Unit of Measure" := '';
        Rec."Location Code" := WhseShpt."Location Code";
        Rec."Quantity on Document" := 1;
        Rec."Shipment No." := WhseShpt."No.";
        Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Incomplete;
        Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";

        IF NOT Rec.GET(Rec."Document Type",Rec."Line No.",Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Line No.",Rec.Version) THEN
          Rec.INSERT(FALSE);

        IF Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
          Rec.INSERT(FALSE);
      END;

      FOR i:=1 TO WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages" DO BEGIN
        LineNo += 10000;
        CLEAR(Rec);
        Rec.INIT();
        Rec."Document Type" := DocType;
        Rec."Document No." := WhseShpt."No.";
        Rec."Line No." := LineNo;
        Rec."Parent Line No." := LineNo;
        Rec."Document Line No." := LineNo;
        Rec.Indentation := 0;
        Rec."Item No." := BEMFMgmt.GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.",i,WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages",3);
        Rec."Item Description" := BEMFMgmt.GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.",i,WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages",4);
        Rec.Quantity := 1;
        Rec."Scanned Quantity" := 0;
        Rec."Unit of Measure" := '';
        Rec."Location Code" := WhseShpt."Location Code";
        Rec."Quantity on Document" := 1;
        Rec."Shipment No." := WhseShpt."No.";
        Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Incomplete;
        Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";

        IF NOT Rec.GET(Rec."Document Type",Rec."Line No.",Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Line No.",Rec.Version) THEN
          Rec.INSERT(FALSE);

        IF Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
          Rec.INSERT(FALSE);
      END;

      FOR i:=1 TO WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages" DO BEGIN
        LineNo += 10000;
        CLEAR(Rec);
        Rec.INIT();
        Rec."Document Type" := DocType;
        Rec."Document No." := WhseShpt."No.";
        Rec."Line No." := LineNo;
        Rec."Parent Line No." := LineNo;
        Rec."Document Line No." := LineNo;
        Rec.Indentation := 0;
        Rec."Item No." := BEMFMgmt.GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.",i,WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages",5);
        Rec."Item Description" := BEMFMgmt.GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.",i,WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages",6);
        Rec.Quantity := 1;
        Rec."Scanned Quantity" := 0;
        Rec."Unit of Measure" := '';
        Rec."Location Code" := WhseShpt."Location Code";
        Rec."Quantity on Document" := 1;
        Rec."Shipment No." := WhseShpt."No.";
        Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Incomplete;
        Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";

        IF NOT Rec.GET(Rec."Document Type",Rec."Line No.",Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Line No.",Rec.Version) THEN
          Rec.INSERT(FALSE);

        IF Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
          Rec.INSERT(FALSE);
      END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    PROCEDURE ChangeWhsShipmentStatusCD@50027(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50015 : Boolean) isCompleated : Boolean;
    VAR
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50002 : Record 7321;
      WhseShpt@50003 : Record 7320;
      TotalScannedItems@50004 : Integer;
      CompleatedScanneditems@50005 : Integer;
      MissingScannedItems@50006 : Integer;
      SurplusScannedItems@50007 : Integer;
      CalculatedParentQty@50008 : Integer;
      SurplusText@50010 : Text[250];
      MissingDialogMessage@50012 : TextConst 'ENU=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoje nepodudaranja u skeniranim stavkama, molim provjerite skenirane stavke. Ako ste sigurni da ßelite aßurirati koliüine na dokumentu odaberite Da.;SLV=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
      UserSetup@50013 : Record 91;
      AdminConfirmMessage@50014 : TextConst 'ENU=The action is allowed to the "Scanned Barcodes Admin".;HRV=Akcija je dozvoljena samo za barkod administratora.;SLV=The action is allowed to the "Scanned Barcodes Admin".';
      SurplusDialogMessage@50016 : TextConst 'ENU=There is a surplus of scanned items (%1), please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoji viÁak skeniranih artikala (%1). Ako ste veÜ provjerili skenirane stavke i ßelite sinkronizirati koliüinu, pritisnite Da.;SLV=There is a surplus of scanned items (%1), please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      IF NOT WhseShpt.GET(DocNo) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF NOT RunChangeDocStatus THEN
        EXIT;

      IF IsBulkUpdate THEN
        WhseShpt.SetHideValidationDialog(TRUE);

      Rec.RESET();
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      TotalScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();           // Count Totals Items

      Rec.SETRANGE(Status, Rec.Status::Complete); // Count Completed
      CompleatedScanneditems := Rec.COUNT();
