      Rec.SETFILTER(Status,'%1|%2', Rec.Status::Incomplete,Rec.Status::Missing); // Count Missing
      MissingScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();

      Rec.SETRANGE(Status, Rec.Status::Surplus); // Count Surplus
      SurplusScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();

      isCompleated := CompleatedScanneditems = TotalScannedItems;

      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::WHCDSHIP:
          BEGIN
            IF NOT isCompleated THEN BEGIN
              IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Ready for CD Shipment" THEN BEGIN
                WhseShpt.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)");
                WhseShpt.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)") OR (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Ready for CD Shipment") THEN BEGIN
                WhseShpt.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD");
                WhseShpt.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END;
          END;

        DocType::WHCDREC:
          BEGIN
            IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::" " THEN
              EXIT;

            IF NOT isCompleated THEN BEGIN
              IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)" THEN BEGIN
                WhseShpt.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)");
                WhseShpt.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD" THEN BEGIN
                WhseShpt.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD");
                WhseShpt.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END;
          END;

        DocType::WHCDSHFROM:
          BEGIN
            IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::" " THEN
              EXIT;

            IF NOT isCompleated THEN BEGIN
              IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD (sync)" THEN BEGIN
                WhseShpt.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD (sync)");
                WhseShpt.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD" THEN BEGIN
                WhseShpt.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD");
                WhseShpt.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END;
          END;
      END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateCptShptCrossDockScanned@50040(VAR Rec@50003 : Record 50113;DocNo@50002 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      CptShptLines@50000 : Record 50022;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      CptShptLines.RESET();
      CptShptLines.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      IF CptShptLines.FINDSET() THEN REPEAT
        CLEAR(Rec);
        Rec.INIT();
        Rec."Document Type" := DocType;
        Rec."Document No." := CptShptLines."Document No.";
        Rec."Line No." := CptShptLines."Line No.";
        Rec."Parent Line No." := CptShptLines."Line No.";
        Rec."Document Line No." := CptShptLines."Line No.";
        Rec.Indentation := 0;
        Rec."Item No." := CptShptLines."Complaint No.";
        Rec."Item Description" := CptShptLines."Customer No" + ' - ' + CptShptLines."Document No.";
        Rec.Quantity := CptShptLines."Package Quantity";
        Rec."Scanned Quantity" := 0;
        Rec."Unit of Measure" := '';
        Rec."Location Code" := '';
        Rec."Quantity on Document" := CptShptLines."Package Quantity";
        Rec."Shipment No." := CptShptLines."Document No.";
        Rec.Status := Rec.Status::Incomplete;
        Rec."User ID" := ScannedDocumentLinesTemp."User ID";

        IF NOT Rec.GET(Rec."Document Type",Rec."Line No.",Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Line No.",Rec.Version) THEN
          Rec.INSERT(FALSE);

        Rec.GET(DocType,Rec."Line No.",DocNo,Rec."Line No.",1);
        SortByQtyFromBuffer(FALSE,Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Type",Rec."Item No.", 1)
      UNTIL CptShptLines.NEXT()=0;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    PROCEDURE ChangeComplaintShipmentStatusCD@50032(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50015 : Boolean) isCompleated : Boolean;
    VAR
      TotalScannedItems@50004 : Integer;
      CompleatedScanneditems@50005 : Integer;
      MissingScannedItems@50006 : Integer;
      SurplusScannedItems@50007 : Integer;
      CalculatedParentQty@50008 : Integer;
      SurplusText@50010 : Text[250];
      MissingDialogMessage@50012 : TextConst 'ENU=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoje nepodudaranja u skeniranim stavkama, molim provjerite skenirane stavke. Ako ste sigurni da ßelite aßurirati koliüine na dokumentu odaberite Da.;SLV=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
      UserSetup@50013 : Record 91;
      AdminConfirmMessage@50014 : TextConst 'ENU=The action is allowed to the "Scanned Barcodes Admin".;HRV=Akcija je dozvoljena samo za barkod administratora.;SLV=The action is allowed to the "Scanned Barcodes Admin".';
      SurplusDialogMessage@50016 : TextConst 'ENU=There is a surplus of scanned items (%1), please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoji viÁak skeniranih artikala (%1). Ako ste veÜ provjerili skenirane stavke i ßelite sinkronizirati koliüinu, pritisnite Da.;SLV=There is a surplus of scanned items (%1), please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
      ComplaintShptHdr@50002 : Record 50021;
      ComplaintShptLine@50003 : Record 50022;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      IF NOT ComplaintShptHdr.GET(DocNo) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF NOT RunChangeDocStatus THEN
        EXIT;

      IF IsBulkUpdate THEN
        ComplaintShptHdr.SetHideValidationDialog(TRUE);

      Rec.RESET();
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      TotalScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();           // Count Totals Items

      Rec.SETRANGE(Status, Rec.Status::Complete); // Count Completed
      CompleatedScanneditems := Rec.COUNT();

      Rec.SETFILTER(Status,'%1|%2', Rec.Status::Incomplete,Rec.Status::Missing); // Count Missing
      MissingScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();

      Rec.SETRANGE(Status, Rec.Status::Surplus); // Count Surplus
      SurplusScannedItems := Rec.COUNT();

      isCompleated := CompleatedScanneditems = TotalScannedItems;

      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::CPCDSHIP:
          BEGIN
            IF NOT isCompleated THEN BEGIN
              IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Ready For Shipment" THEN BEGIN
                ComplaintShptHdr.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)");
                ComplaintShptHdr.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF (ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)")
                  OR (ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Ready For Shipment") THEN BEGIN
                ComplaintShptHdr.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD");
                ComplaintShptHdr.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END;
          END;

        DocType::CPCDREC:
          BEGIN
            IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::" " THEN
              EXIT;

            IF NOT isCompleated THEN BEGIN
              IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)" THEN BEGIN
                ComplaintShptHdr.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)");
                ComplaintShptHdr.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD" THEN BEGIN
                ComplaintShptHdr.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD");
                ComplaintShptHdr.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END;
          END;

        DocType::CPCDSHFROM:
          BEGIN
            IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::" " THEN
              EXIT;

            IF NOT isCompleated THEN BEGIN
              IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD (sync)" THEN BEGIN
                ComplaintShptHdr.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD (sync)");
                ComplaintShptHdr.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD" THEN BEGIN
                ComplaintShptHdr.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD");
                ComplaintShptHdr.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END;
          END;
      END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetIsBulkUpdate@50036(NewIsBulkUpdate@50000 : Boolean);
    BEGIN
      IsBulkUpdate := NewIsBulkUpdate;
    END;

    BEGIN
    END.
  }
}

OBJECT Codeunit 50107 BEMF Mgmt
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=06.10.26;
    Time=16:27:14;
    Modified=Yes;
    Version List=PRIMA;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    OnRun=BEGIN
            IF NOT AutoUpdateBarcodeStatusOnSync() THEN;
          END;

  }
  CODE
  {
    VAR
      DocumentNotExists@50001 : TextConst 'ENU=Document does not exists - %1;HRV=Dokument ne postoji - %1';
      MultipleValueBarcode@50002 : Boolean;
      BarcodeQty@50003 : Decimal;
      BarcodeUoM@50004 : Code[10];
      BarcodeItem@50005 : Code[40];
      DocumentLineNo@50006 : Integer;
      BarcodeMgmt@50007 : Codeunit 50106;
      LineNo@50009 : Integer;
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation@50010 : Record 50009;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,5401813,OnAfterModifyEvent)]
    LOCAL PROCEDURE BEMFRecording_OnAfterModify@50003(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 5401813;VAR xRec@50001 : Record 5401813;RunTrigger@50002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      BEMFRecording@50003 : Record 5401813;
      DocNo@50004 : Code[20];
      ScannedDocumentType@50005 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      //due to SOAP call, cannot use OnAfterInsert
      IF Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      IF Rec.orderId <> '' THEN
        DocNo := Rec.orderId
      ELSE
        DocNo := Rec.description;

      IF DocNo = '' THEN
        ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocumentNotExists, Rec));

      CASE Rec.orderType OF
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Shipment)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Shipment,DocNo);
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Prereceipt)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Prereceipt,DocNo);
        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Receipt)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Receipt,DocNo);
        //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Transport)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Transport,DocNo);
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDSHIP)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDSHIP,DocNo);
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDREC)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDREC,DocNo);
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDSHFROM)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDSHFROM,DocNo);
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDSHIP)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDSHIP,DocNo);
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDREC)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDREC,DocNo);
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDSHFROM)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDSHFROM,DocNo);
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      END;

      CreateSyncRecord(DocNo, Rec.orderType, Rec."User ID"); //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,5401813,OnBeforeModifyEvent)]
    LOCAL PROCEDURE BEMFRecording_OnBeforeModify@50012(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 5401813;VAR xRec@50001 : Record 5401813;RunTrigger@50002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      BEMFRecording@50003 : Record 5401813;
      DocNo@50004 : Code[20];
    BEGIN
      IF Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      Rec."User ID" := USERID;
      Rec."Creation DateTime" := CURRENTDATETIME;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckDocumentStatus@50006(DocumentType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';DocumentNo@50001 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      WhseShpt@50002 : Record 7320;
      DocDoesntExists@50003 : TextConst 'ENU=%1 %2 does not exists or has unallowed status.;HRV=%1 %2 ne postoji ili je u nedozvoljenom statusu.';
      WhseRcpt@50004 : Record 7316;
      TransSheet@50005 : Record 50015;
      ComplaintShpt@50006 : Record 50021;
    BEGIN
      CASE DocumentType OF
        DocumentType::Shipment :
          BEGIN
            WhseShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT (WhseShpt.GET(DocumentNo)) OR (WhseShpt."Barcode Status" = WhseShpt."Barcode Status"::Processed) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (WhseShpt."Barcode Status" = WhseShpt."Barcode Status"::Blank) THEN BEGIN
              WhseShpt."Barcode Status" := WhseShpt."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
              WhseShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        DocumentType::Receipt:
          BEGIN
            WhseRcpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT (WhseRcpt.GET(DocumentNo)) OR (WhseRcpt."Barcode Status" = WhseRcpt."Barcode Status"::Processed) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseRcpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo));

            IF (WhseRcpt."Barcode Status" = WhseRcpt."Barcode Status"::Blank) THEN BEGIN
              WhseRcpt."Barcode Status" := WhseRcpt."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
              WhseRcpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        DocumentType::Transport:
        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
          BEGIN
            TransSheet.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT (TransSheet.GET(DocumentNo)) OR (TransSheet."Barcode Status" = TransSheet."Barcode Status"::Processed) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,TransSheet.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo));

            IF (TransSheet."Barcode Status" = TransSheet."Barcode Status"::Blank) THEN BEGIN
              TransSheet."Barcode Status" := TransSheet."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
              TransSheet.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        DocumentType::WHCDSHIP:
          BEGIN
            WhseShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT WhseShpt.GET(DocumentNo) OR (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" > WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD") THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)") THEN BEGIN
              WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" := WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)";
              WhseShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        DocumentType::WHCDREC:
          BEGIN
            WhseShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT WhseShpt.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)") OR (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" > WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD") THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)") THEN BEGIN
              WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" := WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)";
              WhseShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        DocumentType::WHCDSHFROM:
          BEGIN
            WhseShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT WhseShpt.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)") THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)") THEN BEGIN
              WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" := WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)";
              WhseShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;

        DocumentType::CPCDSHIP:
          BEGIN
            ComplaintShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT ComplaintShpt.GET(DocumentNo) OR (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" > ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD") THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,ComplaintShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)") THEN BEGIN
              ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" := ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)";
              ComplaintShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        DocumentType::CPCDREC:
          BEGIN
            ComplaintShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT ComplaintShpt.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,ComplaintShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)") OR (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" > ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD") THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,ComplaintShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)") THEN BEGIN
              ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" := ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)";
              ComplaintShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        DocumentType::CPCDSHFROM:
          BEGIN
            ComplaintShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT ComplaintShpt.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,ComplaintShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)") THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,ComplaintShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)") THEN BEGIN
              ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" := ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)";
              ComplaintShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      END;
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Codeunit,7310,OnBeforeRelease)]
    PROCEDURE WhseShptStatus_OnBeforeRelease@50000(VAR WarehouseShipmentHeader@50000 : Record 7320);
    VAR
      WhseShptFound@50006 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      FillWhseShptBarcodeEntries(WarehouseShipmentHeader,FALSE);
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Codeunit,7310,OnBeforeReopen)]
    PROCEDURE WhseShptStatus_OnBeforeReOpen@50007(VAR WarehouseShipmentHeader@50000 : Record 7320);
    VAR
      BarcodeStatusErr@50001 : TextConst 'ENU=Barcode status of document %1 must not be %2.;HRV=Barkod status dokumenta %1 ne smije biti %2.';
      ScannedDocumentType@50002 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(BarcodeStatusErr,WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed));

      //DeleteWhseShptBarcodeEntries(WarehouseShipmentHeader);   //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.Status = WarehouseShipmentHeader.Status::Open THEN
        EXIT;
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      DeleteBarcodeEntries(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.", ScannedDocumentType::Shipment);  //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,7320,OnAfterDeleteEvent)]
    PROCEDURE WhseShpt_OnAfterDelete@50019(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 7320;RunTrigger@50001 : Boolean);
    BEGIN
      IF Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      IF Rec.Status = Rec.Status::Open THEN
        EXIT;
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      //DeleteWhseShptBarcodeEntries(Rec); //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>

      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      //DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."No.", ScannedDocumentType::Shipment);  //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
      DeleteBarcodeEntriesOnDeleteDocument(Rec."No.");
      DeleteScannedEntries(Rec."No.");
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillWhseShptBarcodeEntries@50004(Rec@50000 : Record 7320;SkipRelease@50006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      BEMFOrder@50003 : Record 5401810;
      BEMFOrderLine@50002 : Record 5401811;
      BEMFOrderLineItem@50001 : Record 5401812;
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50004 : Record 7321;
      BOMComponent@50005 : Record 90;
      UnitofMeasure@50007 : Record 204;
      BarcodeNo@50008 : Text[200];
      DocTypeLoc@50009 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
      IsRetailLocation@50010 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      IF NOT BarcodeMgmt.IsBeMobileLocation(Rec."No.",DocTypeLoc::Shipment) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF Rec.Status = Rec.Status::Released THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      IF Rec."Barcode Status" = Rec."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT;

      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      Rec.CALCFIELDS("Retail Location");
      IsRetailLocation := Rec."Retail Location";
      //IF BEMFOrder.GET(Rec."No.") THEN
      IF BEMFOrder.GET(Rec."No.",FORMAT(DocTypeLoc::Shipment)) THEN
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        //DeleteWhseShptBarcodeEntries(Rec); // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
        DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."No.",DocTypeLoc::Shipment);

      WarehouseShipmentLine.RESET;
      WarehouseShipmentLine.SETRANGE("No.",Rec."No.");
      IF NOT WarehouseShipmentLine.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      BEMFOrder.INIT;
      BEMFOrder.orderId := Rec."No.";
      BEMFOrder.orderType := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc::Shipment);
      BEMFOrder.description_1 := FORMAT(Rec."Posting Date")+', '+ Rec.FIELDCAPTION("Destination No.")+ ': ' +Rec."Destination No."+', '+ Rec.FIELDCAPTION("Location Code")+ ': ' +Rec."Location Code";
      BEMFOrder.description_2 := FORMAT(Rec."Location Code");
      BEMFOrder.description_4 := FORMAT(Rec."Posting Date");
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      //BEMFOrder.description_3 := FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME);
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.SETRANGE("Location Code",Rec."Location Code");
      IF ResponsibilityCenterLocation.FINDFIRST THEN
        BEMFOrder.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center";
      BEMFOrder.RetailLocation := IsRetailLocation;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>

      BEMFOrder.INSERT;
      REPEAT
        BOMComponent.RESET;
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.");
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
        IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            LineNo+=10000;
            BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(BOMComponent."No.",BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code",BOMComponent."Variant Code");
            IF UnitofMeasure.GET(BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
              BarcodeNo += '|' + BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(BOMComponent."Quantity per");
              InsertSplitBEMFEntries(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo)
            END ELSE
              //InsertBEMFLineEntry(BEMFOrder,WarehouseShipmentLine,TRUE,BOMComponent, BarcodeNo); //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
              InsertBEMFLineEntry(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent, BarcodeNo); //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;

        END ELSE BEGIN //normal items
          LineNo+=10000;
          BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.",WarehouseShipmentLine."Unit of Measure Code",WarehouseShipmentLine."Variant Code");
          IF UnitofMeasure.GET(WarehouseShipmentLine."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
            // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
            //BarcodeNo += '|' + WarehouseShipmentLine."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity);
            BarcodeNo += '|' + WarehouseShipmentLine."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship");
            // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
            InsertSplitBEMFEntries(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo)
          END ELSE
            //InsertBEMFLineEntry(BEMFOrder,WarehouseShipmentLine,FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);  //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
            InsertBEMFLineEntry(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);  //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
        END;
      UNTIL WarehouseShipmentLine.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteBarcodeEntries@50005(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocTypeLoc@50009 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      BEMFOrder@50003 : Record 5401810;
      BEMFOrderLine@50002 : Record 5401811;
      BEMFOrderLineItem@50001 : Record 5401812;
      WhseShptFound@50004 : Boolean;
      WarehouseShipmentHeader@50006 : Record 7320;
      WarehouseReceiptHeader@50007 : Record 7316;
      FusionSetup@50008 : Record 5401802;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      {
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      //IF Rec.Status = Rec.Status::Open THEN
      //  EXIT;
      {CASE DocType OF
        DocType::Shipment:
          BEGIN
            WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(DocNo);
            IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.Status = WarehouseShipmentHeader.Status::Open THEN
              EXIT;
          END;
        DocType::Receipt:
          BEGIN
            WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(DocNo);
            IF WarehouseReceiptHeader.Status = WarehouseReceiptHeader.Status::Open THEN
              EXIT;
          END;
      END;}
      //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>

      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      //IF BEMFOrder.GET(Rec."No.") THEN
      IF BEMFOrder.GET(DocNo) THEN
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        WhseShptFound := TRUE
      ELSE BEGIN
        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        //BEMFOrder.SETRANGE(description_1,Rec."No.");
        //BEMFOrder.SETFILTER(orderType,FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Shipment));
        BEMFOrder.SETRANGE(description_1,DocNo);
        BEMFOrder.SETFILTER(orderType, '%1',FORMAT(DocType));
        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        IF BEMFOrder.FINDFIRST THEN
          WhseShptFound := TRUE;
      END;
      }
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      {IF NOT FusionSetup.FINDFIRST THEN
        EXIT;

      BEMFOrder.SETRANGE(orderType,FusionSetup."Primary Key");
      BEMFOrder.SETRANGE(orderId,DocNo);
      BEMFOrder.SETRANGE(orderType,FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Shipment));
      }

      IF BEMFOrder.GET(DocNo, FORMAT(DocTypeLoc)) THEN
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        WhseShptFound := TRUE
      ELSE BEGIN
        BEMFOrder.SETRANGE(description_1,DocNo);
        BEMFOrder.SETRANGE(orderType,FORMAT(DocTypeLoc)); // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
        BEMFOrder.SETRANGE(orderId);
        IF BEMFOrder.FINDFIRST THEN
          WhseShptFound := TRUE;
      END;

      IF WhseShptFound THEN BEGIN
        BEMFOrderLineItem.SETRANGE(orderId,BEMFOrder.orderId);
        BEMFOrderLineItem.SETRANGE(orderType,FORMAT(DocTypeLoc)); // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>

        IF BEMFOrderLineItem.FINDSET THEN
          BEMFOrderLineItem.DELETEALL;
        BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(orderId,BEMFOrder.orderId);
        BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(orderType,FORMAT(DocTypeLoc)); // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
        IF BEMFOrderLine.FINDSET THEN
          BEMFOrderLine.DELETEALL;
        BEMFOrder.DELETE;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteScannedEntries@50002(DocNo@50001 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      ScannedDocumentLines@50002 : Record 50111;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      {
      CASE DocType OF
        ScannedDocumentType::Shipment: BEGIN
          WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(DocNo);
          DeleteBarcodeEntries(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.", DocType);
        END;
        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        ScannedDocumentType::Receipt: BEGIN
          WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(DocNo);
          DeleteBarcodeEntries(WarehouseReceiptHeader."No.", DocType);
        END;
        //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        ScannedDocumentType::Prereceipt:;
      END;
      }

      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      IF ScannedDocumentLines.FINDSET THEN
        ScannedDocumentLines.DELETEALL(FALSE);
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetItemBarcode@50010(ItemNo@50000 : Code[20];UomCode@50003 : Code[10];VariantCode@50004 : Code[10]) : Code[50];
    VAR
      Barcodes@50001 : Record 99001451;
      Item@50002 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      IF (NOT Item.GET(ItemNo)) OR (Item.Type = Item.Type::Service) THEN
        EXIT;

      Barcodes.RESET;
      Barcodes.SETRANGE("Item No.",ItemNo);
      Barcodes.SETRANGE("Unit of Measure Code", UomCode);
      IF VariantCode <> '' THEN
        Barcodes.SETRANGE("Variant Code",VariantCode);
      IF Barcodes.FINDLAST THEN
        EXIT(Barcodes."Barcode No.")
      ELSE
        EXIT(ItemNo);
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetBarcodeFields@50011(BEMFRecording@50001 : Record 5401813);
    VAR
      Barcodes@50002 : Record 99001451;
      Item@50003 : Record 27;
      BarcodeNo@50000 : Text[40];
    BEGIN
      CLEAR(BarcodeItem);
      CLEAR(BarcodeUoM);
      CLEAR(BarcodeQty);
      BarcodeNo := CONVERTSTR(BEMFRecording.barcode,',','.');

      IF STRPOS(BarcodeNo,'|') <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        BarcodeItem := COPYSTR(BarcodeNo,1,STRPOS(BarcodeNo,'|')-1);
        BarcodeNo := COPYSTR(BarcodeNo,STRLEN(BarcodeItem)+2,STRLEN(BarcodeNo));
        BarcodeUoM := COPYSTR(BarcodeNo,1,STRPOS(BarcodeNo,'|')-1);
        BarcodeQty := BEMFRecording.quantity;
        EXIT;
      END;

      IF Barcodes.GET(BEMFRecording.barcode) THEN BEGIN
        BarcodeItem := Barcodes."Item No.";
        BarcodeUoM := Barcodes."Unit of Measure Code";
        BarcodeQty := BEMFRecording.quantity;
      END ELSE IF Item.GET(BEMFRecording.barcode) THEN BEGIN
        BarcodeItem := Item."No.";
        BarcodeUoM := Item."Base Unit of Measure";
        BarcodeQty := BEMFRecording.quantity;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE FindScannedEntries@50009(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      BEMFRecording@50003 : Record 5401813;
      EntriesInserted@50004 : Boolean;
      BarcodeAppMgmt@50002 : Codeunit 50112;
    BEGIN
      CLEAR(EntriesInserted);

      BarcodeAppMgmt.FindScannedEntries(DocNo,DocType); // <PRICO-2025-67/>

      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::Shipment:
          BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(orderType,UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Shipment)));
        DocType::Prereceipt:
          BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(orderType,UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Prereceipt)));
        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        DocType::Receipt:
          BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(orderType,UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Receipt)));
        //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        DocType::Transport:
          BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(orderType,UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Transport)));
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        DocType::WHCDSHIP:
          BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(orderType,UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::WHCDSHIP)));
        DocType::WHCDREC:
          BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(orderType,UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::WHCDREC)));
        DocType::WHCDSHFROM:
          BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(orderType,UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::WHCDSHFROM)));
        DocType::CPCDSHIP:
          BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(orderType,UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::CPCDSHIP)));
        DocType::CPCDREC:
          BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(orderType,UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::CPCDREC)));
        DocType::CPCDSHFROM:
          BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(orderType,UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::CPCDSHFROM)));
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      END;

      BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(orderId,DocNo);
      IF BEMFRecording.FINDSET(TRUE) THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          InsertScannedEntries(BEMFRecording,DocNo,DocType);
          BEMFRecording.DELETE(TRUE);
          COMMIT;
          EntriesInserted := TRUE;
        UNTIL BEMFRecording.NEXT = 0;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(orderId);
        BEMFRecording.SETRANGE(description,DocNo);
        IF BEMFRecording.FINDSET(TRUE) THEN BEGIN
          InsertScannedEntries(BEMFRecording,DocNo,DocType);
          BEMFRecording.DELETE(TRUE);
          COMMIT;
          EntriesInserted := TRUE;
        END;
      END;

      IF EntriesInserted THEN
        DeleteBarcodeEntries(DocNo,DocType);
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertScannedEntries@50008(BEMFRecording@50000 : Record 5401813;DocNo@50004 : Code[20];DocType@50005 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      ScannedDocumentLines@50001 : Record 50111;
      Item@50002 : Record 27;
      DescriptionValue@50003 : Text[110];
      NoValue@50006 : Text;
    BEGIN
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE(uuid,BEMFRecording.uuid);
      IF NOT ScannedDocumentLines.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        CASE DocType OF
          DocType::WHCDSHIP,DocType::WHCDREC,DocType::WHCDSHFROM:
            BEGIN
              NoValue := BEMFRecording.barcode;
              BarcodeItem := BEMFRecording.barcode;
              BarcodeQty := 1;
              BarcodeUoM := 'KOM'; //change this
              DescriptionValue := BEMFRecording.description;
            END ;
          DocType::CPCDSHIP,DocType::CPCDREC,DocType::CPCDSHFROM:
            BEGIN
              NoValue := BEMFRecording.barcode;
              BarcodeItem := BEMFRecording.barcode;
              BarcodeQty := BEMFRecording.quantity;
              DescriptionValue := BEMFRecording.description;
            END;
          ELSE BEGIN
            GetBarcodeFields(BEMFRecording);
            IF Item.GET(BarcodeItem) THEN;
            NoValue := Item."No.";
            DescriptionValue := Item."Full Description";
          END;
        END;

        {
        GetBarcodeFields(BEMFRecording);
        IF Item.GET(BarcodeItem) THEN;
        }
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        ScannedDocumentLines.RESET;
        ScannedDocumentLines.INIT;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Document Type" := DocType;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Document No." := DocNo;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Item No." := NoValue;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Item Description" := DescriptionValue;
        ScannedDocumentLines.Barcode := BarcodeItem;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Full Barcode Value" := BEMFRecording.barcode;
        ScannedDocumentLines.Quantity := BarcodeQty;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Scanned Quantity" := BarcodeQty;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Unit of Measure" := BarcodeUoM;
        ScannedDocumentLines."User ID" := BEMFRecording."User ID";
        ScannedDocumentLines."Creation DateTime" := BEMFRecording."Creation DateTime";
        ScannedDocumentLines.uuid := BEMFRecording.uuid;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Entry Inserted" := CURRENTDATETIME;
        ScannedDocumentLines.INSERT;
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertBEMFLineEntry@50014(BEMFOrder@50009 : Record 5401810;DocType@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';Document_LineNo@50004 : Integer;IsBOM@50002 : Boolean;BOMComponent@50001 : Record 90;BarcodeNo@50006 : Text[200]);
    VAR
      BEMFOrderLine@50005 : Record 5401811;
      Item@50000 : Record 27;
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50007 : Record 7321;
      WarehouseReceiptLine@50008 : Record 7317;
      Qty@50010 : Decimal;
      LocationCode@50011 : Code[20];
      ItemNo@50012 : Code[20];
      FullItemDescription@50013 : Text[110];
      TransportSheetLine@50014 : Record 50016;
      TransportSheetHeader@50015 : Record 50015;
      ComplaintShipmentHeaderPrim@50016 : Record 50021;
      ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima@50017 : Record 50022;
    BEGIN
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::Shipment:
          BEGIN
            IF WarehouseShipmentLine.GET(BEMFOrder.orderId, Document_LineNo) THEN BEGIN
              // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              //Qty := WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity;
              Qty := WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship";
              // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              LocationCode := WarehouseShipmentLine."Location Code";
              ItemNo := WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.";
              FullItemDescription := WarehouseShipmentLine."Full Item Description";
            END;
          END;
        DocType::Receipt:
          BEGIN
            IF WarehouseReceiptLine.GET(BEMFOrder.orderId, Document_LineNo) THEN BEGIN
              // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              //Qty := WarehouseReceiptLine.Quantity;
              Qty := WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive";
              // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              LocationCode := WarehouseReceiptLine."Location Code";
              ItemNo := WarehouseReceiptLine."Item No.";
              FullItemDescription := WarehouseReceiptLine."Full Item Description";
            END;
          END;
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        DocType::Transport:
          BEGIN
            IF TransportSheetLine.GET(BEMFOrder.orderId, Document_LineNo) THEN BEGIN
              TransportSheetHeader.GET(BEMFOrder.orderId);
              Qty := TransportSheetLine.Quantity;
              LocationCode := TransportSheetHeader."Receiving Location";
              ItemNo := TransportSheetLine."Item No.";
              FullItemDescription := TransportSheetLine."Item Description";
            END;
          END;
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      END;

      //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      BEMFOrderLine.RESET;
      BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(orderId, BEMFOrder.orderId);
      BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(orderType, FORMAT(DocType));
      IF IsBOM THEN
        BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(description_1, BOMComponent."No.")
      ELSE
        //BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(description_1, WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No."); <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
        BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(description_1, ItemNo); //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>

      IF BEMFOrderLine.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN //insert entry
        IF IsBOM THEN
          //BEMFOrderLine.quantity += WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity * BOMComponent."Quantity per" //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          BEMFOrderLine.quantity += Qty * BOMComponent."Quantity per"  //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
        ELSE
          //BEMFOrderLine.quantity += WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity;  //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          BEMFOrderLine.quantity += Qty; //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
        BEMFOrderLine.MODIFY;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        BEMFOrderLine.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLine.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        //BEMFOrderLine.orderType := FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Shipment); //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
        BEMFOrderLine.orderType := FORMAT(DocType); //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
        IF IsBOM THEN BEGIN
          //BEMFOrderLine.quantity := WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity * BOMComponent."Quantity per";  //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          BEMFOrderLine.quantity := Qty * BOMComponent."Quantity per";  //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          BEMFOrderLine.description_1 := BOMComponent."No.";
          BEMFOrderLine.description_2 := BOMComponent."Full Item Description";
          BEMFOrderLine.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center"; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246/>
          //BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := WarehouseShipmentLine."Location Code";  //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := LocationCode;  //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
        END ELSE BEGIN
          //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          {BEMFOrderLine.quantity := WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity;
          BEMFOrderLine.description_1 := WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.";
          BEMFOrderLine.description_2 := WarehouseShipmentLine."Full Item Description";
          BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := WarehouseShipmentLine."Location Code";}
          BEMFOrderLine.quantity := Qty;
          BEMFOrderLine.description_1 := ItemNo;
          BEMFOrderLine.description_2 := FullItemDescription;
          BEMFOrderLine.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center"; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246/>
          BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := LocationCode;
          //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
        END;

        BEMFOrderLine.RetailLocation := BEMFOrder.RetailLocation; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246/>
        BEMFOrderLine.INSERT;

        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        CASE DocType OF
          DocType::Shipment:
            InsertBEMFLineItemEntries(BEMFOrder,DocType,WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.", LocationCode,IsBOM,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);
          DocType::Receipt:
            InsertBEMFLineItemEntries(BEMFOrder,DocType,WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No." , LocationCode,IsBOM,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);
          // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
          DocType::Transport:
            InsertBEMFLineItemEntries(BEMFOrder,DocType,TransportSheetLine."Line No.", LocationCode,IsBOM,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);
          // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        END;
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertSplitBEMFEntries@50016(BEMFOrder@50008 : Record 5401810;DocType@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt';Document_LineNo@50009 : Integer;IsBOM@50006 : Boolean;BOMComponent@50004 : Record 90;BarcodeNo@50000 : Text[200]);
    VAR
      BEMFOrderLine@50001 : Record 5401811;
      BEMFOrderLineItem@50002 : Record 5401812;
      UnitofMeasure@50007 : Record 204;
      Item@50005 : Record 27;
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50010 : Record 7321;
      WarehouseReceiptLine@50011 : Record 7317;
      Qty@50012 : Decimal;
      LocationCode@50013 : Code[20];
      ItemNo@50014 : Code[20];
      FullItemDescription@50015 : Text[110];
      BarcodeExistsLineNo@50016 : Text;
      TransportSheetLine@50017 : Record 50016;
      TransportSheetHeader@50018 : Record 50015;
    BEGIN
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::Shipment:
          BEGIN
            IF WarehouseShipmentLine.GET(BEMFOrder.orderId, Document_LineNo) THEN BEGIN
              // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              //Qty := WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity;
              Qty := WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship";
              // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              LocationCode := WarehouseShipmentLine."Location Code";
              ItemNo := WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.";
              FullItemDescription := WarehouseShipmentLine."Full Item Description";
            END;
          END;
        DocType::Receipt:
          BEGIN
            IF WarehouseReceiptLine.GET(BEMFOrder.orderId, Document_LineNo) THEN BEGIN
              // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              //Qty := WarehouseReceiptLine.Quantity;
              Qty := WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive";
              // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              LocationCode := WarehouseReceiptLine."Location Code";
              ItemNo := WarehouseReceiptLine."Item No.";
              FullItemDescription := WarehouseReceiptLine."Full Item Description";
            END;
          END;
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        DocType::Transport:
          BEGIN
            IF TransportSheetLine.GET(BEMFOrder.orderId, Document_LineNo) THEN BEGIN
              TransportSheetHeader.GET(BEMFOrder.orderId);
              Qty := TransportSheetLine.Quantity;
              LocationCode := TransportSheetHeader."Receiving Location";
              ItemNo := TransportSheetLine."Item No.";
              FullItemDescription := TransportSheetLine."Item Description";
            END;
          END;
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      END;
      //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>

      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1101>
      //IF BarcodeExists(BEMFOrder,IsBOM,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo) THEN BEGIN //check if same item and same barcode
      CLEAR(BarcodeExistsLineNo);
      BarcodeExistsLineNo := BarcodeExists(BEMFOrder,IsBOM,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);
      IF BarcodeExistsLineNo <> '' THEN BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1101>
        BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(orderId, BEMFOrder.orderId);
        BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(orderType, BEMFOrder.orderType);
        BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(orderLineId,BarcodeExistsLineNo); // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1101/>
        IF IsBOM THEN
          BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(description_1,BOMComponent."No.")
        ELSE
          //BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(description_1,WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No."); //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(description_1,ItemNo); //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>

        IF BEMFOrderLine.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          IF IsBOM THEN
            //BEMFOrderLine.quantity += WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity * BOMComponent."Quantity per" //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
            BEMFOrderLine.quantity += Qty * BOMComponent."Quantity per"//<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          ELSE
            //BEMFOrderLine.quantity += WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity; //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
            BEMFOrderLine.quantity += Qty; //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          BEMFOrderLine.MODIFY;
        END;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        BEMFOrderLine.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLine.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLine.orderType := BEMFOrder.orderType;
        IF IsBOM THEN BEGIN
          //BEMFOrderLine.quantity := WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity * BOMComponent."Quantity per"; //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          BEMFOrderLine.quantity := Qty* BOMComponent."Quantity per"; //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          BEMFOrderLine.description_1 := BOMComponent."No.";
          BEMFOrderLine.description_2 := BOMComponent."Full Item Description";
          BEMFOrderLine.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center"; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246/>
          //BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := WarehouseShipmentLine."Location Code"; //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
          BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := LocationCode; //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
        END ELSE BEGIN
          //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
          {BEMFOrderLine.quantity := WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity;
          BEMFOrderLine.description_1 := WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.";
          BEMFOrderLine.description_2 := WarehouseShipmentLine."Full Item Description";
          BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := WarehouseShipmentLine."Location Code";
          }
          BEMFOrderLine.quantity := Qty;
          BEMFOrderLine.description_1 := ItemNo;
          BEMFOrderLine.description_2 := FullItemDescription;
          BEMFOrderLine.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center"; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246/>
          BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := LocationCode;
          //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        END;
        BEMFOrderLine.RetailLocation := BEMFOrder.RetailLocation; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246/>
        BEMFOrderLine.INSERT;

        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        CASE DocType OF
          DocType::Shipment:
            InsertBEMFLineItemEntries(BEMFOrder,DocType,WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.", LocationCode,IsBOM,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);
          DocType::Receipt:
            InsertBEMFLineItemEntries(BEMFOrder,DocType,WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No." , LocationCode,IsBOM,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
          DocType::Transport:
            InsertBEMFLineItemEntries(BEMFOrder,DocType,TransportSheetLine."Line No.", LocationCode,IsBOM,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        END;
        //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      END;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertBEMFLineItemEntries@50018(BEMFOrder@50004 : Record 5401810;DocType@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt';Document_LineNo@50008 : Integer;LocationCode@50009 : Code[20];IsBOM@50002 : Boolean;BOMComponent@50001 : Record 90;BarcodeNo@50000 : Text[200]);
    VAR
      BEMFOrderLineItem@50005 : Record 5401812;
      Item@50006 : Record 27;
      UnitofMeasure@50007 : Record 204;
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50010 : Record 7321;
      WarehouseReceiptLine@50011 : Record 7317;
      TransportSheetLine@50012 : Record 50016;
      TransportSheetHeader@50013 : Record 50015;
    BEGIN
      BEMFOrderLineItem.RESET;
      BEMFOrderLineItem.INIT;
      BEMFOrderLineItem.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
      BEMFOrderLineItem.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
      BEMFOrderLineItem.orderType := BEMFOrder.orderType;
      BEMFOrderLineItem.barcode := BarcodeNo;
      IF IsBOM THEN BEGIN
        IF UnitofMeasure.GET(BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN
          BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := BOMComponent."Quantity per"
        ELSE IF BOMComponent."Package Qty." <> 0 THEN
          BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := BOMComponent."Package Qty."
        ELSE IF Item."Package Qty." <> 0 THEN BEGIN
          Item.GET(BOMComponent."No.");
          BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := Item."Package Qty."
        END ELSE
          BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := 1;

        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_1 := BOMComponent."No.";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_2 := BOMComponent."Full Item Description";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center"; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246/>
        //BEMFOrderLineItem.description_4 := WarehouseShipmentLine."Location Code"; //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_4 := LocationCode;   //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
      END ELSE BEGIN
        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        CASE DocType OF
          DocType::Shipment:
            BEGIN
              WarehouseShipmentLine.GET(BEMFOrder.orderId, Document_LineNo);
              //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              IF UnitofMeasure.GET(WarehouseShipmentLine."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN
                // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                //BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := WarehouseShipmentLine.Quantity
                BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship"
                // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              ELSE BEGIN
                Item.GET(WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.");
                IF Item."Package Qty." <> 0 THEN
                  BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := Item."Package Qty."
                ELSE
                  BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := 1;
              END;
              BEMFOrderLineItem.description_1 := WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.";
              BEMFOrderLineItem.description_2 := WarehouseShipmentLine."Full Item Description";
              BEMFOrderLineItem.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center"; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246/>
              BEMFOrderLineItem.description_4 := WarehouseShipmentLine."Location Code";
          //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
            END;
         DocType::Receipt:
            BEGIN
              WarehouseReceiptLine.GET(BEMFOrder.orderId, Document_LineNo);
              IF UnitofMeasure.GET(WarehouseReceiptLine."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN
                // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                //BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := WarehouseReceiptLine.Quantity
                BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive"
                // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
              ELSE BEGIN
                Item.GET(WarehouseReceiptLine."Item No.");
                IF Item."Package Qty." <> 0 THEN
                  BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := Item."Package Qty."
                ELSE
                  BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := 1;
              END;
              BEMFOrderLineItem.description_1 := WarehouseReceiptLine."Item No.";
              BEMFOrderLineItem.description_2 := WarehouseReceiptLine."Full Item Description";
              BEMFOrderLineItem.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center"; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246/>
              BEMFOrderLineItem.description_4 := WarehouseReceiptLine."Location Code";
            END;
          // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
          DocType::Transport:
            BEGIN
              TransportSheetLine.GET(BEMFOrder.orderId, Document_LineNo);
              IF UnitofMeasure.GET(TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN
                BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := TransportSheetLine.Quantity
              ELSE BEGIN
                Item.GET(TransportSheetLine."Item No.");
                IF Item."Package Qty." <> 0 THEN
                  BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := Item."Package Qty."
                ELSE
                  BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := 1;
              END;
              BEMFOrderLineItem.description_1 := TransportSheetLine."Item No.";
              BEMFOrderLineItem.description_2 := TransportSheetLine."Item Description";
              BEMFOrderLineItem.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center"; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246/>
              TransportSheetHeader.GET(TransportSheetLine."Document No.");
              BEMFOrderLineItem.description_4 := TransportSheetHeader."Receiving Location";
            END;
          // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        END;
        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      END;
      BEMFOrderLineItem.RetailLocation := BEMFOrder.RetailLocation; // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246/>
      BEMFOrderLineItem.INSERT;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE BarcodeExists@50013(BEMFOrder@50004 : Record 5401810;IsBOM@50002 : Boolean;BOMComponent@50001 : Record 90;BarcodeNo@50000 : Text[200]) : Text;
    VAR
      BEMFOrderLineItem@50005 : Record 5401812;
    BEGIN
      BEMFOrderLineItem.SETRANGE(orderId,BEMFOrder.orderId);
      BEMFOrderLineItem.SETRANGE(orderType,BEMFOrder.orderType);
      BEMFOrderLineItem.SETRANGE(barcode,BarcodeNo);
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1101>
      // EXIT(NOT BEMFOrderLineItem.ISEMPTY);
      IF BEMFOrderLineItem.FINDLAST THEN
        EXIT(BEMFOrderLineItem.orderLineId);
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1101>
    END;

    PROCEDURE CheckScannedEntries@50001(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      ScannedDocumentLines@50002 : Record 50111;
      ErrorStatusChange@50003 : TextConst 'ENU=You cannot change Barcode status to Empty because there are scanned entries.;HRV=Ne moße se promijeniti barkod status u prazno jer postoje skenirane stavke.';
    BEGIN
      {CASE DocType OF
      ScannedDocumentType::Shipment: BEGIN
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document Type",ScannedDocumentLines."Document Type"::Shipment);
      } //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/> - commented
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);  //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      IF NOT ScannedDocumentLines.ISEMPTY THEN
        ERROR(ErrorStatusChange);
      {END;
      ScannedDocumentType::Prereceipt:;
      END;
      } //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/> -commented
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillWhseReceiptBarcodeEntries@50015(Rec@50000 : Record 7316;SkipRelease@50006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      BEMFOrder@50003 : Record 5401810;
      BEMFOrderLine@50002 : Record 5401811;
      BEMFOrderLineItem@50001 : Record 5401812;
      WarehouseReceiptLine@50004 : Record 7317;
      BOMComponent@50005 : Record 90;
      UnitofMeasure@50007 : Record 204;
      BarcodeNo@50008 : Text[200];
      DocTypeLoc@50009 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059> - copy of function FillWhseShptBarcodeEntries
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      IF NOT BarcodeMgmt.IsBeMobileLocation(Rec."No.",DocTypeLoc::Receipt) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF Rec.Status = Rec.Status::Released THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      IF Rec."Barcode Status" = Rec."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT;

      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      //IF BEMFOrder.GET(Rec."No.") THEN
      IF BEMFOrder.GET(Rec."No.",'RECEIPT') THEN
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."No.",DocTypeLoc::Receipt);

      WarehouseReceiptLine.RESET;
      WarehouseReceiptLine.SETRANGE("No.",Rec."No.");
      IF NOT WarehouseReceiptLine.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      BEMFOrder.INIT;
      BEMFOrder.orderId := Rec."No.";
      BEMFOrder.orderType := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc::Receipt);
      BEMFOrder.description_1 := FORMAT(Rec."Posting Date")+', ' + Rec.FIELDCAPTION("Location Code")+ ': ' +Rec."Location Code";
      BEMFOrder.description_2 := FORMAT(Rec."Location Code");
      BEMFOrder.description_4 := FORMAT(Rec."Posting Date");
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      //BEMFOrder.description_3 := FORMAT(CURRENTDATETIME);
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.SETRANGE("Location Code",Rec."Location Code");
      IF ResponsibilityCenterLocation.FINDFIRST THEN
        BEMFOrder.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center";

      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      Rec.CALCFIELDS("Retail Location");
      BEMFOrder.RetailLocation := BEMFOrder.RetailLocation;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>

      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      BEMFOrder.INSERT;

      REPEAT
        BOMComponent.RESET;
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",WarehouseReceiptLine."Item No.");
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
        IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            LineNo+=10000;
            BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(BOMComponent."No.",BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code",BOMComponent."Variant Code");
            IF UnitofMeasure.GET(BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
              BarcodeNo += '|' + BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(BOMComponent."Quantity per");
              InsertSplitBEMFEntries(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Receipt,WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo)
            END ELSE
              InsertBEMFLineEntry(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Receipt, WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent, BarcodeNo);
          UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;

        END ELSE BEGIN //normal items
          LineNo+=10000;
          BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(WarehouseReceiptLine."Item No.",WarehouseReceiptLine."Unit of Measure Code",WarehouseReceiptLine."Variant Code");
          IF UnitofMeasure.GET(WarehouseReceiptLine."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
            // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
            //BarcodeNo += '|' + WarehouseReceiptLine."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(WarehouseReceiptLine.Quantity);
            BarcodeNo += '|' + WarehouseReceiptLine."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive");
            // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
            InsertSplitBEMFEntries(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Receipt,WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo)
          END ELSE
            InsertBEMFLineEntry(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Receipt, WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);
        END;
      UNTIL WarehouseReceiptLine.NEXT = 0;
      //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Codeunit,50127,OnBeforeRelease)]
    PROCEDURE WhseRcptStatus_OnBeforeRelease@50017(VAR WarehouseReceiptHeader@50000 : Record 7316);
    VAR
      WhseRcptFound@50006 : Boolean;
      Location@50001 : Record 14;
    BEGIN
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      IF WarehouseReceiptHeader.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      //IF Location.GET(WarehouseReceiptHeader."Location Code") AND Location."Use in BE-Mobile" AND Location."Central Warehouse" THEN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      //IF Location.GET(WarehouseReceiptHeader."Location Code") AND Location."Use in BE-Mobile (receipt)" AND Location."Central Warehouse" THEN
      IF Location.GET(WarehouseReceiptHeader."Location Code") AND Location."Use in BE-Mobile (receipt)" THEN
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        FillWhseReceiptBarcodeEntries(WarehouseReceiptHeader, FALSE);
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Codeunit,50127,OnBeforeReopen)]
    PROCEDURE WhseRcptStatus_OnBeforeReOpen@50020(VAR WarehouseReceiptHeader@50000 : Record 7316);
    VAR
      BarcodeStatusErr@50001 : TextConst 'ENU=Barcode status of document %1 must not be %2.;HRV=Barkod status dokumenta %1 ne smije biti %2.';
      Location@50002 : Record 14;
      ScannedDocumentType@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      IF WarehouseReceiptHeader.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      IF WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(BarcodeStatusErr,WarehouseReceiptHeader."No.",WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed));

      IF WarehouseReceiptHeader.Status = WarehouseReceiptHeader.Status::Open THEN
        EXIT;

      DeleteBarcodeEntries(WarehouseReceiptHeader."No.", ScannedDocumentType::Receipt);
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,7316,OnAfterDeleteEvent)]
    PROCEDURE WhseRcpt_OnAfterDelete@50023(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 7316;RunTrigger@50001 : Boolean);
    BEGIN
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      IF Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      IF Rec.Status = Rec.Status::Open THEN
        EXIT;

      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      //DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."No.", ScannedDocumentType::Receipt);
      DeleteBarcodeEntriesOnDeleteDocument(Rec."No.");
      DeleteScannedEntries(Rec."No.");
      //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateSyncRecord@50021(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : Code[40];UserID@50002 : Code[50]);
    VAR
      BarcodeSyncRecord@50003 : Record 50161;
    BEGIN
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      {
      BarcodeSyncRecord.SETRANGE("Document No.", DocNo);
      BarcodeSyncRecord.SETRANGE("Document Type", DocType);
      IF BarcodeSyncRecord.ISEMPTY() THEN BEGIN
      }
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      BarcodeSyncRecord.INIT;
      BarcodeSyncRecord."Document No." := DocNo;

      BarcodeSyncRecord."Document Type" := DocType;
      BarcodeSyncRecord."User ID" := UserID;
      BarcodeSyncRecord."Syncronization DateTime" := CURRENTDATETIME;
      BarcodeSyncRecord.INSERT;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245> END;
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillTransportSheetBarcodeEntries@50025(Rec@50000 : Record 50015;SkipRelease@50006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      BEMFOrder@50003 : Record 5401810;
      BEMFOrderLine@50002 : Record 5401811;
      BEMFOrderLineItem@50001 : Record 5401812;
      BOMComponent@50005 : Record 90;
      UnitofMeasure@50007 : Record 204;
      BarcodeNo@50008 : Text[200];
      TransportSheetLine@50004 : Record 50016;
      DocTypeLoc@50009 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1246> - copy of function FillWhseShptBarcodeEntries
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      IF NOT BarcodeMgmt.IsBeMobileLocation(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF Rec.Status <> Rec.Status::"Shipped - Awaiting Receipt" THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      IF Rec."Barcode Status" = Rec."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT;

      IF BEMFOrder.GET(Rec."Document No.",'TRANSPORT') THEN
        DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport);

      TransportSheetLine.RESET;
      TransportSheetLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",Rec."Document No.");
      IF NOT TransportSheetLine.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      BEMFOrder.INIT;
      BEMFOrder.orderId := Rec."Document No.";
      BEMFOrder.orderType := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc::Transport);
      BEMFOrder.description_1 := FORMAT(Rec."Posting Date")+', ' + Rec.FIELDCAPTION("Receiving Location")+ ': ' +Rec."Receiving Location";
      BEMFOrder.description_2 := FORMAT(Rec."Receiving Location");
      BEMFOrder.description_4 := FORMAT(Rec."Posting Date");
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.SETRANGE("Location Code",Rec."Receiving Location");
      IF ResponsibilityCenterLocation.FINDFIRST THEN
        BEMFOrder.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center";
      BEMFOrder.INSERT;

      REPEAT
        BOMComponent.RESET;
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Item No.");
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
        IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            LineNo+=10000;
            BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(BOMComponent."No.",BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code",BOMComponent."Variant Code");
            IF UnitofMeasure.GET(BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
              BarcodeNo += '|' + BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(BOMComponent."Quantity per");
              InsertSplitBEMFEntries(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo)
            END ELSE
              InsertBEMFLineEntry(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent, BarcodeNo);
          UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;
        END ELSE BEGIN //normal items
          LineNo+=10000;
          BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(TransportSheetLine."Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure",'');
          IF UnitofMeasure.GET(TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
            BarcodeNo += '|' + TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure" + '|' + FORMAT(TransportSheetLine.Quantity);
            InsertSplitBEMFEntries(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo)
          END ELSE
            InsertBEMFLineEntry(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);
        END;
      UNTIL TransportSheetLine.NEXT = 0;
      //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillPartiallyTransportSheetBarcodeEntries@50039(Rec@50000 : Record 50015;SkipRelease@50006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      BEMFOrder@50003 : Record 5401810;
      BEMFOrderLine@50002 : Record 5401811;
      BEMFOrderLineItem@50001 : Record 5401812;
      BOMComponent@50005 : Record 90;
      UnitofMeasure@50007 : Record 204;
      BarcodeNo@50008 : Text[200];
      TransportSheetLine@50004 : Record 50016;
      DocTypeLoc@50009 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
      Item@50010 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      IF NOT BarcodeMgmt.IsBeMobileLocation(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport) THEN
        EXIT;
      // TODO - new app
      IF Rec.Status <> Rec.Status::"Partially Received" THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      IF Rec."Barcode Status" = Rec."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT;

      IF BEMFOrder.GET(Rec."Document No.",'TRANSPORT') THEN
        DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport);

      TransportSheetLine.RESET;
      TransportSheetLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",Rec."Document No.");
      TransportSheetLine.SETFILTER("Qty. to Receive", '>0');
      IF NOT TransportSheetLine.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      BEMFOrder.INIT;
      BEMFOrder.orderId := Rec."Document No.";
      BEMFOrder.orderType := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc::Transport);
      BEMFOrder.description_1 := FORMAT(Rec."Posting Date")+', ' + Rec.FIELDCAPTION("Receiving Location")+ ': ' +Rec."Receiving Location";
      BEMFOrder.description_2 := FORMAT(Rec."Receiving Location");
      BEMFOrder.description_4 := FORMAT(Rec."Posting Date");
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.SETRANGE("Location Code",Rec."Receiving Location");
      IF ResponsibilityCenterLocation.FINDFIRST THEN
        BEMFOrder.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center";
      BEMFOrder.INSERT;

      REPEAT
        BOMComponent.RESET;
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Item No.");
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
        IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            LineNo+=10000;
            BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(BOMComponent."No.",BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code",BOMComponent."Variant Code");
            IF UnitofMeasure.GET(BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
              BarcodeNo += '|' + BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(BOMComponent."Quantity per");
              InsertSplitBEMFEntries(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo)
            END ELSE
              InsertBEMFLineEntry(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent, BarcodeNo);
          UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;
        END ELSE BEGIN //normal items
          LineNo+=10000;
          BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(TransportSheetLine."Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure",'');
          IF UnitofMeasure.GET(TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
            BarcodeNo += '|' + TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure" + '|' + FORMAT(TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2");
            InsertSplitBEMFEntries(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo)
          END ELSE
            InsertBEMFLineEntry(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);
        END;

        BEMFOrderLine.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLine.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLine.orderType := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc);
        BEMFOrderLine.quantity := TransportSheetLine.Quantity;
        BEMFOrderLine.description_1 := TransportSheetLine."Item No.";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_2 := TransportSheetLine."Item Description";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_3 := Rec."Destination No.";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := Rec."Receiving Location";
        BEMFOrderLine.INSERT;

        BEMFOrderLineItem.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderType := BEMFOrder.orderType;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.barcode := BarcodeNo;

        IF UnitofMeasure.GET(TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN
          BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2"
        ELSE BEGIN
          Item.GET(TransportSheetLine."Item No.");
          IF Item."Package Qty." <> 0 THEN
            BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := Item."Package Qty."
          ELSE
            BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := 1;
        END;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_1 := TransportSheetLine."Item No.";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_2 := TransportSheetLine."Item Description";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_3 := Rec."Destination No.";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_4 := Rec."Receiving Location";

        BEMFOrderLineItem.INSERT;

      UNTIL TransportSheetLine.NEXT = 0;
      //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillPartiallyReceivedTransportSheetBarcodeEntries@50046(Rec@50000 : Record 50015;SkipRelease@50006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      BEMFOrder@50003 : Record 5401810;
      BEMFOrderLine@50002 : Record 5401811;
      BEMFOrderLineItem@50001 : Record 5401812;
      BOMComponent@50005 : Record 90;
      UnitofMeasure@50007 : Record 204;
      BarcodeNo@50008 : Text[200];
      TransportSheetLine@50004 : Record 50016;
      DocTypeLoc@50009 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
      Item@50010 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      IF NOT BarcodeMgmt.IsBeMobileLocation(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF Rec.Status <> Rec.Status::"Shipped - Awaiting Receipt" THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      IF Rec."Barcode Status" = Rec."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT;

      IF BEMFOrder.GET(Rec."Document No.",'TRANSPORT') THEN
        DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport);

      TransportSheetLine.RESET;
      TransportSheetLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",Rec."Document No.");
      TransportSheetLine.SETFILTER("Qty. to Ship 2", '>0');
      IF NOT TransportSheetLine.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      BEMFOrder.INIT;
      BEMFOrder.orderId := Rec."Document No.";
      BEMFOrder.orderType := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc::Transport);
      BEMFOrder.description_1 := FORMAT(Rec."Posting Date")+', ' + Rec.FIELDCAPTION("Receiving Location")+ ': ' +Rec."Receiving Location";
      BEMFOrder.description_2 := FORMAT(Rec."Receiving Location");
      BEMFOrder.description_4 := FORMAT(Rec."Posting Date");
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.SETRANGE("Location Code",Rec."Receiving Location");
      IF ResponsibilityCenterLocation.FINDFIRST THEN
        BEMFOrder.description_3 := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center";
      BEMFOrder.INSERT;

      REPEAT
        BOMComponent.RESET;
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Item No.");
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
        IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            LineNo+=10000;
            BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(BOMComponent."No.",BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code",BOMComponent."Variant Code");
            IF UnitofMeasure.GET(BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
              BarcodeNo += '|' + BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(BOMComponent."Quantity per");
              InsertSplitBEMFEntries(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo)
            END ELSE
              InsertBEMFLineEntry(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent, BarcodeNo);
          UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;
        END ELSE BEGIN //normal items
          LineNo+=10000;
          BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(TransportSheetLine."Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure",'');
          IF UnitofMeasure.GET(TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
            BarcodeNo += '|' + TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure" + '|' + FORMAT(TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2");
            InsertSplitBEMFEntries(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo)
          END ELSE
            InsertBEMFLineEntry(BEMFOrder,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo);
        END;

        BEMFOrderLine.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLine.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLine.orderType := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc);
        BEMFOrderLine.quantity := TransportSheetLine.Quantity;
        BEMFOrderLine.description_1 := TransportSheetLine."Item No.";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_2 := TransportSheetLine."Item Description";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_3 := Rec."Destination No.";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := Rec."Receiving Location";
        BEMFOrderLine.INSERT;

        BEMFOrderLineItem.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderType := BEMFOrder.orderType;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.barcode := BarcodeNo;

        IF UnitofMeasure.GET(TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN
          BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2"
        ELSE BEGIN
          Item.GET(TransportSheetLine."Item No.");
          IF Item."Package Qty." <> 0 THEN
            BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := Item."Package Qty."
          ELSE
            BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := 1;
        END;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_1 := TransportSheetLine."Item No.";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_2 := TransportSheetLine."Item Description";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_3 := Rec."Destination No.";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_4 := Rec."Receiving Location";

        BEMFOrderLineItem.INSERT;

      UNTIL TransportSheetLine.NEXT = 0;
      //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,50015,OnAfterDeleteEvent)]
