    PROCEDURE TransportSheet_OnAfterDelete@50044(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 50015;RunTrigger@50001 : Boolean);
    BEGIN

      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      IF Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      IF Rec.Status = Rec.Status::Open THEN
        EXIT;

      DeleteBarcodeEntriesOnDeleteDocument(Rec."Document No.");
      DeleteScannedEntries(Rec."Document No.");
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillComplaintBarcodeEntries@50024(Rec@50000 : Record 50021;SkipRelease@50006 : Boolean;DocTypeLoc@50005 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      BEMFOrder@50003 : Record 5401810;
      BEMFOrderLine@50002 : Record 5401811;
      BEMFOrderLineItem@50001 : Record 5401812;
      ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima@50004 : Record 50022;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      IF NOT IsCrossDockRespCenter(Rec."Store Responsible Center") THEN
        EXIT;

      Rec.TESTFIELD(Status,Rec.Status::Released);

      IF BEMFOrder.GET(Rec."Document No.",FORMAT(DocTypeLoc)) THEN
        DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc);

      ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima.RESET;
      ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima.SETRANGE("Document No.",Rec."Document No.");
      IF NOT ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      BEMFOrder.INIT;
      BEMFOrder.orderId := Rec."Document No.";
      BEMFOrder.orderType := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc);
      BEMFOrder.description_1 := FORMAT(Rec."Shipment Date")+', '+ Rec.FIELDCAPTION(Store)+ ': ' +Rec.Store+', '+ Rec.FIELDCAPTION("Store Responsible Center")+ ': ' +Rec."Store Responsible Center";
      BEMFOrder.description_2 := Rec."Store Responsible Center";
      BEMFOrder.description_3 := FORMAT(Rec.Store);
      BEMFOrder.description_4 := FORMAT(Rec."Shipment Date");
      BEMFOrder.INSERT;

      REPEAT
        LineNo+=10000;
        BEMFOrderLine.RESET();
        BEMFOrderLine.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderType := BEMFOrder.orderType;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLine.quantity := ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima."Package Quantity";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_1 := FORMAT(Rec."Shipment Date")+', '+ Rec.FIELDCAPTION(Store)+ ': ' +Rec.Store+', '+ Rec.FIELDCAPTION("Store Responsible Center")+ ': ' +Rec."Store Responsible Center";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_2 := ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima."Complaint No.";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_3 := FORMAT(Rec."Shipment Date");
        BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := Rec."Store Responsible Center";
        BEMFOrderLine.INSERT;

        BEMFOrderLineItem.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderType := BEMFOrder.orderType;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLineItem.barcode := ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima."Complaint No.";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := 1;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_1 := FORMAT(Rec."Shipment Date")+', '+ Rec.FIELDCAPTION(Store)+ ': ' +Rec.Store+', '+ Rec.FIELDCAPTION("Store Responsible Center")+ ': ' +Rec."Store Responsible Center";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_2 := FORMAT(Rec."Shipment Date");
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_3 := FORMAT(Rec.Store);
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_4 := Rec."Store Responsible Center";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.INSERT;
      UNTIL ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima.NEXT = 0;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillWhseShptBarcodeEntriesCrossDock@50036(WhseShpt@50002 : Record 7320;DocTypeLoc@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      BEMFOrder@50001 : Record 5401810;
      BEMFOrderLine@50005 : Record 5401811;
      BEMFOrderLineItem@50000 : Record 5401812;
      i@50004 : Integer;
      NoOfPallets@50006 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      IF (WhseShpt."No. of Pallets" = 0) AND (WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages" = 0) AND (WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages" = 0) AND (WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No." = '') THEN
        EXIT;

      CASE WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" OF
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Ready for CD Shipment":
          DocTypeLoc:=DocTypeLoc::WHCDSHIP;
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD",WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)":
          DocTypeLoc:=DocTypeLoc::WHCDREC;
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD",WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)":
          DocTypeLoc:=DocTypeLoc::WHCDSHFROM;
        ELSE BEGIN
          IF BEMFOrder.GET(WhseShpt."No.",FORMAT(DocTypeLoc)) THEN
            DeleteBarcodeEntries(WhseShpt."No.",DocTypeLoc);
          EXIT;
        END;
      END;

      IF BEMFOrder.GET(WhseShpt."No.",FORMAT(DocTypeLoc)) THEN
        DeleteBarcodeEntries(WhseShpt."No.",DocTypeLoc);

      BEMFOrder.INIT;
      BEMFOrder.orderId := WhseShpt."No.";
      BEMFOrder.orderType := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc);
      BEMFOrder.description_1 := FORMAT(WhseShpt."Posting Date")+', '+ WhseShpt.FIELDCAPTION("Location Code")+ ': ' + WhseShpt."Location Code" + ', '+ WhseShpt.FIELDCAPTION("Destination No.")+ ': ' +WhseShpt."Destination No.";
      BEMFOrder.description_2 := FORMAT(WhseShpt."Location Code");
      BEMFOrder.description_4 := FORMAT(WhseShpt."Posting Date");
      BEMFOrder.description_3 := WhseShpt."Destination No.";
      BEMFOrder.INSERT;

      IF WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No." <> '' THEN
        NoOfPallets := 1
      ELSE
        NoOfPallets := WhseShpt."No. of Pallets";

      FOR i := 1 TO NoOfPallets DO BEGIN
        LineNo += 10000;
        BEMFOrderLine.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLine.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLine.orderType := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc);
        BEMFOrderLine.quantity := 1;
        BEMFOrderLine.description_1 := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(BEMFOrder.orderId, i, NoOfPallets, 2);
        IF WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No." <> '' THEN
          BEMFOrderLine.description_1 += ' - ' + WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No.";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_3 := WhseShpt."Destination No.";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := WhseShpt."Location Code";
        BEMFOrderLine.INSERT;

        BEMFOrderLineItem.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderType := BEMFOrder.orderType;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.barcode := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(BEMFOrder.orderId, i, NoOfPallets, 1);
        BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := 1;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_1 := BEMFOrderLine.description_1;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_3 := WhseShpt."Destination No.";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_4 := WhseShpt."Location Code";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.INSERT;
      END;

      FOR i := 1 TO WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages" DO BEGIN
        LineNo += 10000;
        BEMFOrderLine.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLine.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLine.orderType := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc);
        BEMFOrderLine.quantity := 1;
        BEMFOrderLine.description_1 := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(BEMFOrder.orderId, i, WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages", 4);
        BEMFOrderLine.description_3 := WhseShpt."Destination No.";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := WhseShpt."Location Code";
        BEMFOrderLine.INSERT;

        BEMFOrderLineItem.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderType := BEMFOrder.orderType;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.barcode := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(BEMFOrder.orderId, i, WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages", 3);
        BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := 1;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_1 :=  BEMFOrderLine.description_1;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_3 := WhseShpt."Destination No.";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_4 := WhseShpt."Location Code";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.INSERT;
      END;

      FOR i := 1 TO WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages" DO BEGIN // TODO
        LineNo += 10000;
        BEMFOrderLine.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLine.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLine.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLine.orderType := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc);
        BEMFOrderLine.quantity := 1;
        BEMFOrderLine.description_1 := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(BEMFOrder.orderId, i, WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages", 6);
        BEMFOrderLine.description_3 := WhseShpt."Destination No.";
        BEMFOrderLine.description_4 := WhseShpt."Location Code";
        BEMFOrderLine.INSERT;

        BEMFOrderLineItem.RESET;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.INIT;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderId := BEMFOrder.orderId;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderLineId := FORMAT(LineNo);
        BEMFOrderLineItem.orderType := BEMFOrder.orderType;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.barcode := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(BEMFOrder.orderId, i, WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages", 5);
        BEMFOrderLineItem.quantity := 1;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_1 :=  BEMFOrderLine.description_1;
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_3 := WhseShpt."Destination No.";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.description_4 := WhseShpt."Location Code";
        BEMFOrderLineItem.INSERT;
      END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsCrossDockLocation@50027(LocationNo@50000 : Code[20]) : Boolean;
    VAR
      Location@50001 : Record 14;
    BEGIN
      EXIT(Location.GET(LocationNo) AND Location."Use Cross-Dock Warehouse"); // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsCrossDockRespCenter@50028(RespCenterNo@50000 : Code[20]) : Boolean;
    VAR
      ResponsibilityCenter@50001 : Record 5714;
    BEGIN
      EXIT(ResponsibilityCenter.GET(RespCenterNo) AND ResponsibilityCenter."Use Cross-Dock Warehouse"); // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsValidCrossDockStatus@50022(PreviousStatusIndex@50003 : Integer;NextStatusIndex@50002 : Integer) : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      IF PreviousStatusIndex = NextStatusIndex THEN
        EXIT(TRUE);

      CASE TRUE OF
        (PreviousStatusIndex = 0) AND (NextStatusIndex = 1):
          EXIT(TRUE);
        (PreviousStatusIndex = 1) AND (NextStatusIndex = 2) OR (NextStatusIndex = 3):
          EXIT(TRUE);
        (NextStatusIndex = 4) AND (NextStatusIndex = 5):
          EXIT(TRUE);
        (PreviousStatusIndex = 6) AND (NextStatusIndex = 7):
          EXIT(TRUE);
        NextStatusIndex = 8: // complaint finish? TODO
          EXIT(TRUE);
      END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode@50026(DocNo@50000 : Code[40];PackageNo@50001 : Integer;TotalPackageNo@50003 : Integer;LineType@50002 : Integer) : Code[40];
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      CASE LineType OF
        1: EXIT(DocNo + 'PAL' + FORMAT(PackageNo) + '/' + FORMAT(TotalPackageNo));
        2: EXIT(DocNo + ' PALETA ' + FORMAT(PackageNo) + '/' + FORMAT(TotalPackageNo));
        3: EXIT(DocNo + 'PK' + FORMAT(PackageNo) + '/' + FORMAT(TotalPackageNo));
        4: EXIT(DocNo + ' DODATNIH PAKETA ' + FORMAT(PackageNo) + '/' + FORMAT(TotalPackageNo));
        5: EXIT(DocNo + 'PLDP' + FORMAT(PackageNo) + '/' + FORMAT(TotalPackageNo));
        6: EXIT(DocNo + ' PALETA DOD. PAKETA ' + FORMAT(PackageNo) + '/' + FORMAT(TotalPackageNo));
      END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertWhseShptBarcodeStatusLog@50030(WhseShpt@50001 : Record 7320);
    VAR
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog@50000 : Record 50167;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog.INIT;

      CASE WhseShpt."Barcode Status" OF
        WhseShpt."Barcode Status"::Blank:
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::" ";
        WhseShpt."Barcode Status"::Synchronized:
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
        WhseShpt."Barcode Status"::Processed:
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::Processed;
      END;

      BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Document No." := WhseShpt."No.";
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Document Type" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Document Type"::"Warehouse Shipment";
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog."User ID" := USERID;
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Creation DateTime" := CURRENTDATETIME;
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog.INSERT;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertWhseShptCDBarcodeStatusLog@50029(WhseShpt@50001 : Record 7320);
    VAR
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog@50000 : Record 50167;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog.INIT;

      CASE WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" OF
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::" ":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::" ";
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Ready for CD Shipment":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Ready for CD Shipment";
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)";
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Shipped to CD";
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Received on CD (sync)";
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Received on CD";
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD (sync)":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Shipped from CD (sync)";
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Shipped from CD";
      END;

      BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Document No." := WhseShpt."No.";
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Document Type" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Document Type"::"Warehouse Shipment";
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog."User ID" := USERID;
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Creation DateTime" := CURRENTDATETIME;
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog.INSERT;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertComplaintCDBarcodeStatusLog@50031(ComplaintShpt@50001 : Record 50021);
    VAR
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog@50000 : Record 50167;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog.INIT;

      CASE ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" OF
        ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::" ":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::" ";
        ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Ready For Shipment":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)";
        ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Shipped to CD";
        ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Received on CD (sync)";
        ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Received on CD";
        ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Shipped from CD (sync)";
        ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD (sync)":
          BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Barcode Status"::"Shipped from CD";
      END;

      BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Document No." := ComplaintShpt."Document No.";
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Document Type" := BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Document Type"::"Complaint Shipment";
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog."User ID" := USERID;
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog."Creation DateTime" := CURRENTDATETIME;
      BarcodeStatusChangeLog.INSERT;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteBarcodeEntriesOnDeleteDocument@50042(DocNo@50000 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      BEMFOrder@50003 : Record 5401810;
      BEMFOrderLine@50002 : Record 5401811;
      BEMFOrderLineItem@50001 : Record 5401812;
      DocFound@50004 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      BEMFOrder.SETRANGE(orderId, DocNo);
      IF BEMFOrder.FINDSET THEN
        DocFound := TRUE
      ELSE BEGIN
        BEMFOrder.SETRANGE(description_1,DocNo);
        BEMFOrder.SETRANGE(orderId);
        IF BEMFOrder.FINDSET THEN
          DocFound := TRUE;
      END;

      IF DocFound THEN BEGIN
        REPEAT
          BEMFOrderLineItem.SETRANGE(orderId,BEMFOrder.orderId);
          IF BEMFOrderLineItem.FINDSET THEN
            BEMFOrderLineItem.DELETEALL;
          BEMFOrderLine.SETRANGE(orderId,BEMFOrder.orderId);
          IF BEMFOrderLine.FINDSET THEN
            BEMFOrderLine.DELETEALL;
          BEMFOrder.DELETE;
        UNTIL BEMFOrder.NEXT = 0;
      END;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    [TryFunction]
    PROCEDURE AutoUpdateBarcodeStatusOnSync@50043();
    VAR
      BarcodeMgmt@50000 : Codeunit 50106;
      BarcodeAppMgmt@50004 : Codeunit 50112;
      BarcodeSyncRecord@50001 : Record 50161;
      DocType@50002 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
      TempProcessedRecord@50003 : TEMPORARY Record 50161;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      {
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      BarcodeMgmt.SetIsBulkUpdate(TRUE);

      BarcodeSyncRecord.SETRANGE("Entry Processed",FALSE);
      BarcodeSyncRecord.SETFILTER("Syncronization DateTime",'..%1',CREATEDATETIME(TODAY,TIME - (5*60*1000)));// process all entries 5 minutes after sync
      IF BarcodeSyncRecord.FINDSET(TRUE) THEN REPEAT
        TempProcessedRecord.SETRANGE("Document No.",BarcodeSyncRecord."Document No.");
        TempProcessedRecord.SETRANGE("Document Type",BarcodeSyncRecord."Document Type");
        IF NOT TempProcessedRecord.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
          TempProcessedRecord.INIT;
          TempProcessedRecord.TRANSFERFIELDS(BarcodeSyncRecord);
          IF TempProcessedRecord.ISTEMPORARY() THEN
            TempProcessedRecord.INSERT();

          CASE TempProcessedRecord."Document Type" OF
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Shipment)):
                BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::Shipment,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Prereceipt)):
                BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::Prereceipt,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Receipt)):
                BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::Receipt,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Transport)):
                BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::Transport,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::WHCDSHIP)):
                BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::WHCDSHIP,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::WHCDREC)):
                BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::WHCDREC,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::WHCDSHFROM)):
                BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::WHCDSHFROM,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::CPCDSHIP)):
                BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::CPCDSHIP,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::CPCDREC)):
                BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::CPCDREC,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::CPCDSHFROM)):
                BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::CPCDSHFROM,TRUE);
            END;
          COMMIT;
        END;

        BarcodeSyncRecord."Entry Processed" := TRUE;
        BarcodeSyncRecord.MODIFY;
      UNTIL BarcodeSyncRecord.NEXT = 0;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      }
      // Moved to Barcode App Mgmt; kept so job queue entries that run this codeunit keep working
      BarcodeAppMgmt.AutoUpdateBarcodeStatusOnSync;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE BulkWhseShptStatusUpdate@50034(VAR WarehouseShipmentHeader@50000 : Record 7320);
    VAR
      BarcodeStatusLbl@50001 : TextConst 'ENU=Do you want to update Barcode Status on %1 Warehouse Shipments?;HRV=¶elite li aßurirati barkod status na %1 skladiÁnih otpremnica?';
      BarcodeMgmt@50002 : Codeunit 50106;
      BarcodeAppMgmt@50007 : Codeunit 50112;
      WhseCount@50004 : Integer;
      UpdateLbl@50005 : TextConst 'ENU=There is nothing for update.;HRV=Ne postoji niÁta za aßuriranje.';
      isCompleatedStatus@50006 : Boolean;
      DocType@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      {
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      WhseCount := WarehouseShipmentHeader.COUNT;
      IF WhseCount = 0 THEN BEGIN
        IF GUIALLOWED THEN
          MESSAGE(UpdateLbl);
        EXIT;
      END;

      IF GUIALLOWED THEN
        IF NOT CONFIRM(STRSUBSTNO(BarcodeStatusLbl,WhseCount), TRUE) THEN
          EXIT;

      BarcodeMgmt.SetIsBulkUpdate(TRUE);
      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.FINDSET(TRUE) THEN REPEAT
        BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",DocType::Shipment,TRUE);
        // commit??
        BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",DocType::WHCDSHIP,TRUE);
        BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",DocType::WHCDREC,TRUE);
        BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",DocType::WHCDSHFROM,TRUE);
      UNTIL WarehouseShipmentHeader.NEXT = 0;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      }
      // Moved to Barcode App Mgmt; kept for callers outside these objects
      BarcodeAppMgmt.BulkWhseShptStatusUpdate(WarehouseShipmentHeader);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;
