    PROCEDURE CreateLocationRespCenterDialog@50032(FusionBuilder_PC@50003 : Codeunit 5401801;VAR BEMFInput_PX@50000 : XMLport 5401800;VAR BEMFState_PX@50001 : XMLport 5401800;VAR BEMFPage_PX@50002 : XMLport 5401801;SetupName@50005 : Text;WebServiceName@50009 : Text;ApplyMethodName@50010 : Text);
    VAR
      FusionSetup@50004 : Record 5401802;
      Location@50006 : Record 14;
      ResponsibilityCenter@50007 : Record 5714;
      SelectID@50008 : Text;
      LocRespCenterNotConfErr@50011 : TextConst 'ENU=Location / Resp. Center selection not configured.';
      SelectLbl@50012 : TextConst 'ENU=Select %1';
      SelectPlaceLbl@50013 : TextConst 'ENU=Please select a %1';
      ApplyLbl@50014 : TextConst 'ENU=Apply';
      ScrollContainer@50017 : Text;
      List@50015 : Text;
      ListEntryContainer@50016 : Text;
      ClickAction@50018 : Text;
      TempLocation@50019 : TEMPORARY Record 14;
    BEGIN
      // BETERNA OW 05.02.24...
      FusionBuilder_PC.Init(BEMFPage_PX,BEMFState_PX,BEMFInput_PX);

      FusionBuilder_PC.AddPage('LocationRespCenterSelect');
      FusionBuilder_PC.AttachActionHome('');
      FusionBuilder_PC.LinkAction('', 'backAction');

      ScrollContainer := FusionBuilder_PC.AddScrollContainer();
      FusionBuilder_PC.PlaceElementOnPage();

      FusionSetup.GET(SetupName);

      IF (NOT FusionSetup."Enable Location Sel. Action") AND
        (NOT FusionSetup."Enable Res. Cent. Sel. Action")
      THEN
        ERROR(LocRespCenterNotConfErr);

      IF FusionSetup."Enable Location Sel. Action" THEN BEGIN
        FusionBuilder_PC.AttachTitle(STRSUBSTNO(SelectLbl,Location.TABLECAPTION));
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        CASE FusionSetup."Document Type" OF
          FusionSetup."Document Type"::Shipment:
            BEGIN
              Location.SETRANGE("Use in BE-Mobile (shipment)",TRUE);
              Location.SETRANGE("Is Retail Location",FALSE);
            END;
          FusionSetup."Document Type"::"Retail Shipment":
            BEGIN
              Location.SETRANGE("Use in BE-Mobile (shipment)",TRUE);
              Location.SETRANGE("Is Retail Location",TRUE);
            END;
          FusionSetup."Document Type"::Receipt:
            BEGIN
              Location.SETRANGE("Use in BE-Mobile (receipt)",TRUE);
              Location.SETRANGE("Is Retail Location",FALSE);
            END;
          FusionSetup."Document Type"::"Retail Receipt":
            BEGIN
              Location.SETRANGE("Use in BE-Mobile (receipt)",TRUE);
              Location.SETRANGE("Is Retail Location",TRUE);
            END;
          FusionSetup."Document Type"::Transport:
              Location.SETRANGE("Use in BE-Mobile (transport)",TRUE);
        END;

        IF FusionSetup."Destination Filter" <> '' THEN BEGIN
          CreateDestinationLocations(TempLocation,FusionSetup);
          TempLocation.RESET();
          IF TempLocation.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN BEGIN
            FusionBuilder_PC.AddLabelPreset(STRSUBSTNO(SelectPlaceLbl,Location.TABLECAPTION), '');
            FusionBuilder_PC.AttachStyle('bemf-text-bold');
            FusionBuilder_PC.AttachStyle('bemf-text-lg');
            FusionBuilder_PC.SetParentElement(ScrollContainer);
            List := FusionBuilder_PC.AddList();
            FusionBuilder_PC.SetParentElement(ScrollContainer);
            REPEAT
              ListEntryContainer := FusionBuilder_PC.AddContainer(0);
              FusionBuilder_PC.AttachActionRequest('', WebServiceName, ApplyMethodName);
              FusionBuilder_PC.LinkAction('', 'onClick');
              ClickAction := FusionBuilder_PC.GetCurrentActionRefId();
              FusionBuilder_PC.AttachActionParameter('SelectedPlace', ClickAction, TempLocation.Code);
              FusionBuilder_PC.SetParentElement(List);
              FusionBuilder_PC.AddLabelPreset(TempLocation.Code + ' - ' + TempLocation.Name, '');
              FusionBuilder_PC.SetParentElement(ListEntryContainer);
            UNTIL TempLocation.NEXT = 0;
          END;
        END ELSE
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
        IF Location.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          FusionBuilder_PC.AddLabelPreset(STRSUBSTNO(SelectPlaceLbl,Location.TABLECAPTION), '');
          FusionBuilder_PC.AttachStyle('bemf-text-bold');
          FusionBuilder_PC.AttachStyle('bemf-text-lg');
          FusionBuilder_PC.SetParentElement(ScrollContainer);
          List := FusionBuilder_PC.AddList();
          FusionBuilder_PC.SetParentElement(ScrollContainer);
          REPEAT
            ListEntryContainer := FusionBuilder_PC.AddContainer(0);
            FusionBuilder_PC.AttachActionRequest('', WebServiceName, ApplyMethodName);
            FusionBuilder_PC.LinkAction('', 'onClick');
            ClickAction := FusionBuilder_PC.GetCurrentActionRefId();
            FusionBuilder_PC.AttachActionParameter('SelectedPlace', ClickAction, Location.Code);
            FusionBuilder_PC.SetParentElement(List);
            FusionBuilder_PC.AddLabelPreset(Location.Code + ' - ' + Location.Name, '');
            FusionBuilder_PC.SetParentElement(ListEntryContainer);
          UNTIL Location.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END ELSE IF FusionSetup."Enable Res. Cent. Sel. Action" THEN BEGIN
        FusionBuilder_PC.AttachTitle(STRSUBSTNO(SelectLbl,ResponsibilityCenter.TABLECAPTION));
        ResponsibilityCenter.SETRANGE("Use in BE-Mobile (complaint)",TRUE); // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245/>
        IF ResponsibilityCenter.FINDSET(FALSE,FALSE) THEN BEGIN
          FusionBuilder_PC.AddLabelPreset(STRSUBSTNO(SelectPlaceLbl,ResponsibilityCenter.TABLECAPTION), '');
          FusionBuilder_PC.AttachStyle('bemf-text-bold');
          FusionBuilder_PC.AttachStyle('bemf-text-lg');
          FusionBuilder_PC.SetParentElement(ScrollContainer);
          List := FusionBuilder_PC.AddList();
          FusionBuilder_PC.SetParentElement(ScrollContainer);
          REPEAT
            ListEntryContainer := FusionBuilder_PC.AddContainer(0);
            FusionBuilder_PC.AttachActionRequest('', WebServiceName, ApplyMethodName);
            FusionBuilder_PC.LinkAction('', 'onClick');
            ClickAction := FusionBuilder_PC.GetCurrentActionRefId();
            FusionBuilder_PC.AttachActionParameter('SelectedPlace', ClickAction, ResponsibilityCenter.Code);
            FusionBuilder_PC.SetParentElement(List);
            FusionBuilder_PC.AddLabelPreset(ResponsibilityCenter.Code + ' - ' + ResponsibilityCenter.Name, '');
            FusionBuilder_PC.SetParentElement(ListEntryContainer);
          UNTIL ResponsibilityCenter.NEXT = 0;
        END;
      END;
      // ...BETERNA OW 05.02.24
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateDestinationLocations@50037(VAR TempLocation@50000 : TEMPORARY Record 14;FusionSetup@50004 : Record 5401802);
    VAR
      WhseShptHdr@50001 : Record 7320;
      Location@50002 : Record 14;
      Customer@50003 : Record 18;
    BEGIN
      // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
      WhseShptHdr.SETFILTER("Destination No.",FusionSetup."Destination Filter");
      IF FusionSetup."Primary Key" = 'WHCDSHFROM' THEN
        WhseShptHdr.SETFILTER("Cross-Dock Shipment Status", '%1|%2',WhseShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD",WhseShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)");
      IF WhseShptHdr.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          IF NOT TempLocation.GET(WhseShptHdr."Destination No.") THEN
            IF Customer.GET(WhseShptHdr."Destination No.") THEN BEGIN
              TempLocation.Code := Customer."No.";
              TempLocation.Name := Customer.Name;
              IF TempLocation.ISTEMPORARY() THEN
                TempLocation.INSERT;
            END ELSE IF Location.GET(WhseShptHdr."Destination No.") THEN BEGIN
              TempLocation.Code := Location.Code;
              TempLocation.Name := Location.Name;
              IF TempLocation.ISTEMPORARY() THEN
                TempLocation.INSERT;
            END;
        UNTIL WhseShptHdr.NEXT = 0;
      // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      <PRIMA>
        <ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>BARKOD SKLADIÊNA PRIMKA NABAVE
         <TASK>Expand option values in parameter of functions </TASK>
        </ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        <ZGNVSUPPCO-1101>Barkod - neispravno zbrajanje barkodova za radne ploüe</ZGNVSUPPCO-1101>
      </PRIMA>

      BETERNA OW 05.02.24 - Dialog for location / responsibility center select
    }
    END.
  }
}

OBJECT Codeunit 50112 Barcode App Mgmt
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=06.10.26;
    Time=16:27:14;
    Modified=Yes;
    Version List=PRIMA;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    OnRun=BEGIN
            // <PRICO-2025-67>
            IF NOT AutoUpdateBarcodeStatusOnSync THEN;
            // </PRICO-2025-67>
          END;

  }
  CODE
  {
    VAR
      LineNo@50000 : Integer;
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation@50001 : Record 50009;
      BarcodeMgmt@50002 : Codeunit 50106;
      IsBulkUpdate@50003 : Boolean;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,50180,OnBeforeModifyEvent)]
    LOCAL PROCEDURE BarcodeAppRecordings_OnBeforeModify@50001(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 50180;VAR xRec@50001 : Record 50180;RunTrigger@50002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      DocumentNotExists@50003 : TextConst 'HRV=Dokument %1 ne postoji.';
      ScannedDocumentType@50005 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM,RetailShpt,RetailRcpt';
    BEGIN
      Rec."Creation DateTime" := CURRENTDATETIME;
      Rec."User ID" := USERID;

      IF Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      IF Rec."Document No." = '' THEN
        ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocumentNotExists, Rec));

      CASE Rec."Document Type" OF
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Shipment)):
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Shipment,Rec."Document No.");
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Prereceipt)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Prereceipt,Rec."Document No.");
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Receipt)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Receipt,Rec."Document No.");
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Receipt)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Receipt,Rec."Document No.");
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Transport)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Transport,Rec."Document No.");
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDSHIP)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDSHIP,Rec."Document No.");
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDREC)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDREC,Rec."Document No.");
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDSHFROM)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDSHFROM,Rec."Document No.");
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDSHIP)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDSHIP,Rec."Document No.");
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDREC)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDREC,Rec."Document No.");
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDSHFROM)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDSHFROM,Rec."Document No.");
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::RetailShpt)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::RetailShpt,Rec."Document No.");
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::RetailRcpt)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::RetailRcpt,Rec."Document No.");
        ELSE
          ERROR('Krivi tip');
      END;

      CreateSyncRecord(Rec."Document No.", Rec."Document Type", USERID());
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Table,5401813,OnAfterModifyEvent)]
    LOCAL PROCEDURE BEMFRecording_OnAfterModify@50003(VAR Rec@50000 : Record 5401813;VAR xRec@50001 : Record 5401813;RunTrigger@50002 : Boolean);
    VAR
      BEMFRecording@50003 : Record 5401813;
      DocNo@50004 : Code[20];
      ScannedDocumentType@50005 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      {
      //due to SOAP call, cannot use OnAfterInsert
      IF Rec.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      IF Rec.orderId <> '' THEN
        DocNo := Rec.orderId
      ELSE
        DocNo := Rec.description;

      IF DocNo = '' THEN
        ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocumentNotExists, Rec));

      CASE Rec.orderType OF
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Shipment)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Shipment,DocNo);
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Prereceipt)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Prereceipt,DocNo);
        //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Receipt)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Receipt,DocNo);
        //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::Transport)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::Transport,DocNo);
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDSHIP)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDSHIP,DocNo);
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDREC)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDREC,DocNo);
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDSHFROM)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::WHCDSHFROM,DocNo);
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDSHIP)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDSHIP,DocNo);
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDREC)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDREC,DocNo);
        UPPERCASE(FORMAT(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDSHFROM)) :
          CheckDocumentStatus(ScannedDocumentType::CPCDSHFROM,DocNo);
        // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1246>
      END;
      CreateSyncRecord(DocNo, Rec.orderType, Rec."User ID"); //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
      }
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckDocumentStatus@50006(DocumentType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM,RetailShpt,RetailRcpt';DocumentNo@50001 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      WhseShpt@50002 : Record 7320;
      DocDoesntExists@50003 : TextConst 'ENU=%1 %2 does not exists or has unallowed status.;HRV=%1 %2 ne postoji ili je u nedozvoljenom statusu.';
      WhseRcpt@50004 : Record 7316;
      TransSheet@50005 : Record 50015;
      ComplaintShpt@50006 : Record 50021;
    BEGIN
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
      CASE DocumentType OF
        DocumentType::Shipment, DocumentType::RetailShpt:
          BEGIN
            WhseShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT (WhseShpt.GET(DocumentNo)) OR (WhseShpt."Barcode Status" = WhseShpt."Barcode Status"::Processed) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (WhseShpt."Barcode Status" = WhseShpt."Barcode Status"::Blank) THEN BEGIN
              WhseShpt."Barcode Status" := WhseShpt."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
              WhseShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        DocumentType::Receipt,DocumentType::RetailRcpt:
          BEGIN
            WhseRcpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT (WhseRcpt.GET(DocumentNo)) OR (WhseRcpt."Barcode Status" = WhseRcpt."Barcode Status"::Processed) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseRcpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo));

            IF (WhseRcpt."Barcode Status" = WhseRcpt."Barcode Status"::Blank) THEN BEGIN
              WhseRcpt."Barcode Status" := WhseRcpt."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
              WhseRcpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        DocumentType::Transport:
          BEGIN
            TransSheet.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT (TransSheet.GET(DocumentNo)) OR (TransSheet."Barcode Status" = TransSheet."Barcode Status"::Processed) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,TransSheet.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo));

            IF (TransSheet."Barcode Status" = TransSheet."Barcode Status"::Blank) THEN BEGIN
              TransSheet."Barcode Status" := TransSheet."Barcode Status"::Synchronized;
              TransSheet.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        DocumentType::WHCDSHIP:
          BEGIN
            WhseShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT WhseShpt.GET(DocumentNo) OR (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" > WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD") THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)") THEN BEGIN
              WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" := WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)";
              WhseShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        DocumentType::WHCDREC:
          BEGIN
            WhseShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT WhseShpt.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)") OR (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" > WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD") THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)") THEN BEGIN
              WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" := WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)";
              WhseShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        DocumentType::WHCDSHFROM:
          BEGIN
            WhseShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT WhseShpt.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)") THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,WhseShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)") THEN BEGIN
              WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" := WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)";
              WhseShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;

        DocumentType::CPCDSHIP:
          BEGIN
            ComplaintShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT ComplaintShpt.GET(DocumentNo) OR (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" > ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD") THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,ComplaintShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)") THEN BEGIN
              ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" := ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)";
              ComplaintShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        DocumentType::CPCDREC:
          BEGIN
            ComplaintShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT ComplaintShpt.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,ComplaintShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)") OR (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" > ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD") THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,ComplaintShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)") THEN BEGIN
              ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" := ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)";
              ComplaintShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
        DocumentType::CPCDSHFROM:
          BEGIN
            ComplaintShpt.LOCKTABLE(TRUE);
            IF NOT ComplaintShpt.GET(DocumentNo) THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,ComplaintShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)") THEN
              ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(DocDoesntExists,ComplaintShpt.TABLECAPTION,DocumentNo))
            ELSE IF (ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)") THEN BEGIN
              ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" := ComplaintShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)";
              ComplaintShpt.MODIFY;
            END;
          END;
      END;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateSyncRecord@50021(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : Code[40];UserID@50002 : Code[50]);
    VAR
      BarcodeSyncRecord@50003 : Record 50161;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      BarcodeSyncRecord.INIT;
      BarcodeSyncRecord."Document No." := DocNo;
      BarcodeSyncRecord."Document Type" := DocType;
      BarcodeSyncRecord."User ID" := UserID;
      BarcodeSyncRecord."Syncronization DateTime" := CURRENTDATETIME;
      BarcodeSyncRecord.INSERT;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateBarcodeAppEntries@50045(WarehouseShipmentHeader@50002 : Record 7320;SkipRelease@50010 : Boolean);
    VAR
      DocTypeLoc@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
      BOMComponent@50004 : Record 90;
      BarcodeNo@50006 : Text[200];
      UnitofMeasure@50007 : Record 204;
      IsRetailLoc@50008 : Boolean;
      DocNo@50009 : Code[20];
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50003 : Record 7321;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      //IF NOT BarcodeMgmt.IsBeMobileLocation(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",DocTypeLoc::Shipment) THEN
      IF NOT IsBarcodeAppDocument(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",DocTypeLoc::Shipment) THEN
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
        EXIT;

      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.Status = WarehouseShipmentHeader.Status::Released THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT;

      DocNo := WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.";
      DeleteBarcodeEntries(DocNo,DocTypeLoc::Shipment);

      WarehouseShipmentHeader.CALCFIELDS("Retail Location");

      IF (COPYSTR(WarehouseShipmentHeader."Location Code",1,2) = 'MP') AND (COPYSTR(WarehouseShipmentHeader."Destination No.",1,2) = 'MP') THEN
        IsRetailLoc := TRUE;

      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.RESET;
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.SETRANGE("Location Code",WarehouseShipmentHeader."Location Code");
      IF NOT ResponsibilityCenterLocation.FINDFIRST THEN
        ResponsibilityCenterLocation.INIT;

      WarehouseShipmentLine.RESET;
      WarehouseShipmentLine.SETRANGE("No.",WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.");
      IF NOT WarehouseShipmentLine.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      REPEAT
        BOMComponent.RESET;
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.");
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
        IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            LineNo += 10000;
            BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(BOMComponent."No.",BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code",BOMComponent."Variant Code");
            IF UnitofMeasure.GET(BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
              BarcodeNo += '|' + BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(BOMComponent."Quantity per");
              InsertSplitBEMFEntries(DocNo,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,IsRetailLoc,WarehouseShipmentHeader."Posting Date",0)
            END ELSE
              InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(DocNo,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,IsRetailLoc,WarehouseShipmentHeader."Posting Date",0);
          UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;
        END ELSE BEGIN //normal items
          LineNo += 10000;
          BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.",WarehouseShipmentLine."Unit of Measure Code",WarehouseShipmentLine."Variant Code");
          IF UnitofMeasure.GET(WarehouseShipmentLine."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
            BarcodeNo += '|' + WarehouseShipmentLine."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship");
            InsertSplitBEMFEntries(DocNo,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,IsRetailLoc,WarehouseShipmentHeader."Posting Date",0)
          END ELSE
            InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(DocNo,DocTypeLoc::Shipment,WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,IsRetailLoc,WarehouseShipmentHeader."Posting Date",0);
        END;
      UNTIL WarehouseShipmentLine.NEXT = 0;
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillWhseReceiptBarcodeEntries@50015(Rec@50000 : Record 7316;SkipRelease@50006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      WarehouseReceiptLine@50004 : Record 7317;
      BOMComponent@50005 : Record 90;
      UnitofMeasure@50007 : Record 204;
      BarcodeNo@50008 : Text[200];
      DocTypeLoc@50009 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
      IsRetailLoc@50010 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      //IF NOT BarcodeMgmt.IsBeMobileLocation(Rec."No.",DocTypeLoc::Receipt) THEN
      IF NOT IsBarcodeAppDocument(Rec."No.",DocTypeLoc::Receipt) THEN
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
        EXIT;

      IF Rec.Status = Rec.Status::Released THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      IF Rec."Barcode Status" = Rec."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT;

      DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."No.",DocTypeLoc::Receipt);

      Rec.CALCFIELDS("Retail Location");

      IF (COPYSTR(Rec."Location Code",1,2) = 'MP') AND (COPYSTR(Rec."Source No.",1,2) <> 'CS') THEN
        IsRetailLoc := TRUE;

      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.RESET;
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.SETRANGE("Location Code",Rec."Location Code");
      IF NOT ResponsibilityCenterLocation.FINDFIRST THEN
        ResponsibilityCenterLocation.INIT;

      WarehouseReceiptLine.RESET;
      WarehouseReceiptLine.SETRANGE("No.",Rec."No.");
      IF NOT WarehouseReceiptLine.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      REPEAT
        BOMComponent.RESET;
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",WarehouseReceiptLine."Item No.");
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
        IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            LineNo += 10000;
            BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(BOMComponent."No.",BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code",BOMComponent."Variant Code");
            IF UnitofMeasure.GET(BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
              BarcodeNo += '|' + BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(BOMComponent."Quantity per");
              InsertSplitBEMFEntries(Rec."No.",DocTypeLoc::Receipt,WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,IsRetailLoc,Rec."Posting Date",0)
            END ELSE
              InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(Rec."No.",DocTypeLoc::Receipt,WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,IsRetailLoc,Rec."Posting Date",0);
          UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;
        END ELSE BEGIN //normal items
          LineNo += 10000;
          BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(WarehouseReceiptLine."Item No.",WarehouseReceiptLine."Unit of Measure Code",WarehouseReceiptLine."Variant Code");
          IF UnitofMeasure.GET(WarehouseReceiptLine."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
            BarcodeNo += '|' + WarehouseReceiptLine."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive");
            InsertSplitBEMFEntries(Rec."No.",DocTypeLoc::Receipt,WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,IsRetailLoc,Rec."Posting Date",0)
          END ELSE
            InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(Rec."No.",DocTypeLoc::Receipt,WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,IsRetailLoc,Rec."Posting Date",0);
        END;
      UNTIL WarehouseReceiptLine.NEXT = 0;
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillTransportSheetBarcodeEntries@50025(Rec@50000 : Record 50015;SkipRelease@50006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      BOMComponent@50005 : Record 90;
      UnitofMeasure@50007 : Record 204;
      BarcodeNo@50008 : Text[200];
      TransportSheetLine@50004 : Record 50016;
      DocTypeLoc@50009 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      {
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      IF NOT BarcodeMgmt.IsBeMobileLocation(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF Rec.Status <> Rec.Status::"Shipped - Awaiting Receipt" THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      IF Rec."Barcode Status" = Rec."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT;

      DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport);

      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.RESET;
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.SETRANGE("Location Code",Rec."Receiving Location");
      IF NOT ResponsibilityCenterLocation.FINDFIRST THEN
        ResponsibilityCenterLocation.INIT;

      TransportSheetLine.RESET;
      TransportSheetLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",Rec."Document No.");
      IF NOT TransportSheetLine.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      REPEAT
        BOMComponent.RESET;
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Item No.");
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
        IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            LineNo += 10000;
            BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(BOMComponent."No.",BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code",BOMComponent."Variant Code");
            IF UnitofMeasure.GET(BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
              BarcodeNo += '|' + BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(BOMComponent."Quantity per");
              InsertSplitBEMFEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,Rec."Posting Date",0)
            END ELSE
              InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,Rec."Posting Date",0);
          UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;
        END ELSE BEGIN //normal items
          LineNo += 10000;
          BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(TransportSheetLine."Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure",'');
          IF UnitofMeasure.GET(TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
            BarcodeNo += '|' + TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure" + '|' + FORMAT(TransportSheetLine.Quantity);
            InsertSplitBEMFEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,Rec."Posting Date",0)
          END ELSE
            InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,Rec."Posting Date",0);
        END;
      UNTIL TransportSheetLine.NEXT = 0;
      }
      IF Rec.Status <> Rec.Status::"Shipped - Awaiting Receipt" THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      CreateTransportSheetEntries(Rec);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillPartiallyTransportSheetBarcodeEntries@50039(Rec@50000 : Record 50015;SkipRelease@50006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      BOMComponent@50005 : Record 90;
      UnitofMeasure@50007 : Record 204;
      BarcodeNo@50008 : Text[200];
      TransportSheetLine@50004 : Record 50016;
      DocTypeLoc@50009 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      {
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      IF NOT BarcodeMgmt.IsBeMobileLocation(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF Rec.Status <> Rec.Status::"Shipped - Awaiting Receipt" THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      IF Rec."Barcode Status" = Rec."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT;

      DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport);

      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.RESET;
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.SETRANGE("Location Code",Rec."Receiving Location");
      IF NOT ResponsibilityCenterLocation.FINDFIRST THEN
        ResponsibilityCenterLocation.INIT;

      TransportSheetLine.RESET;
      TransportSheetLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",Rec."Document No.");
      TransportSheetLine.SETFILTER("Qty. to Ship 2", '>0');
      IF NOT TransportSheetLine.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      REPEAT
        BOMComponent.RESET;
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Item No.");
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
        IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            LineNo += 10000;
            BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(BOMComponent."No.",BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code",BOMComponent."Variant Code");
            IF UnitofMeasure.GET(BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
              BarcodeNo += '|' + BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(BOMComponent."Quantity per");
              InsertSplitBEMFEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,Rec."Posting Date",TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2")
            END ELSE
              InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,Rec."Posting Date",TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2");
          UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;
        END ELSE BEGIN //normal items
          LineNo += 10000;
          BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(TransportSheetLine."Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure",'');
          IF UnitofMeasure.GET(TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
            BarcodeNo += '|' + TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure" + '|' + FORMAT(TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2");
            InsertSplitBEMFEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,Rec."Posting Date",TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2")
          END ELSE
            InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,Rec."Posting Date",TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2");
        END;
      UNTIL TransportSheetLine.NEXT = 0;
      }
      IF Rec.Status <> Rec.Status::"Partially Shipped" THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      CreateTransportSheetEntries(Rec);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillPartiallyReceivedTransportSheetBarcodeEntries@50002(Rec@50000 : Record 50015;SkipRelease@50006 : Boolean);
    VAR
      BOMComponent@50005 : Record 90;
      UnitofMeasure@50007 : Record 204;
      BarcodeNo@50008 : Text[200];
      TransportSheetLine@50004 : Record 50016;
      DocTypeLoc@50009 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      {
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      IF NOT BarcodeMgmt.IsBeMobileLocation(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF Rec.Status <> Rec.Status::"Partially Received" THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      IF Rec."Barcode Status" = Rec."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT;

      DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport);

      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.RESET;
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.SETRANGE("Location Code",Rec."Receiving Location");
      IF NOT ResponsibilityCenterLocation.FINDFIRST THEN
        ResponsibilityCenterLocation.INIT;

      TransportSheetLine.RESET;
      TransportSheetLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",Rec."Document No.");
      TransportSheetLine.SETFILTER("Qty. to Receive", '>0');
      IF NOT TransportSheetLine.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      REPEAT
        BOMComponent.RESET;
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Item No.");
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
        IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            LineNo += 10000;
            BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(BOMComponent."No.",BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code",BOMComponent."Variant Code");
            IF UnitofMeasure.GET(BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
              BarcodeNo += '|' + BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(BOMComponent."Quantity per");
              InsertSplitBEMFEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,Rec."Posting Date",TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2")
            END ELSE
              InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,Rec."Posting Date",TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2");
          UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;
        END ELSE BEGIN //normal items
          LineNo += 10000;
          BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(TransportSheetLine."Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure",'');
          IF UnitofMeasure.GET(TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
            BarcodeNo += '|' + TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure" + '|' + FORMAT(TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2");
            InsertSplitBEMFEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,Rec."Posting Date",TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2")
          END ELSE
            InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,Rec."Posting Date",TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Ship 2");
        END;
      UNTIL TransportSheetLine.NEXT = 0;
      }
      IF Rec.Status <> Rec.Status::"Partially Received" THEN
        IF NOT SkipRelease THEN
          EXIT;

      CreateTransportSheetEntries(Rec);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateTransportSheetEntries@50233(TransportSheetHeader@50000 : Record 50015);
    VAR
      BOMComponent@50001 : Record 90;
      UnitofMeasure@50002 : Record 204;
      BarcodeNo@50003 : Text[200];
      TransportSheetLine@50004 : Record 50016;
      DocTypeLoc@50005 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      // Every receiving status offers what has been shipped and not yet received, which is what the receipt posts
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      IF NOT IsBarcodeAppDocument(TransportSheetHeader."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport) THEN
        EXIT;

      IF TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status" = TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT;

      DeleteBarcodeEntries(TransportSheetHeader."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport);

      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.RESET;
      ResponsibilityCenterLocation.SETRANGE("Location Code",TransportSheetHeader."Receiving Location");
      IF NOT ResponsibilityCenterLocation.FINDFIRST THEN
        ResponsibilityCenterLocation.INIT;

      TransportSheetLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",TransportSheetHeader."Document No.");
      TransportSheetLine.SETFILTER("Qty. to Receive",'>0');
      IF NOT TransportSheetLine.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      REPEAT
        BOMComponent.RESET;
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Item No.");
        BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
        IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
          REPEAT
            LineNo += 10000;
            BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(BOMComponent."No.",BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code",BOMComponent."Variant Code");
            IF UnitofMeasure.GET(BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
              BarcodeNo += '|' + BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code" + '|' + FORMAT(BOMComponent."Quantity per");
              InsertSplitBEMFEntries(TransportSheetHeader."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,TransportSheetHeader."Posting Date",TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Receive")
            END ELSE
              InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(TransportSheetHeader."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TRUE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,TransportSheetHeader."Posting Date",TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Receive");
          UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;
        END ELSE BEGIN //normal items
          LineNo += 10000;
          BarcodeNo := GetItemBarcode(TransportSheetLine."Item No.",TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure",'');
          IF UnitofMeasure.GET(TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure") AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN BEGIN
            BarcodeNo += '|' + TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure" + '|' + FORMAT(TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Receive");
            InsertSplitBEMFEntries(TransportSheetHeader."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,TransportSheetHeader."Posting Date",TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Receive")
          END ELSE
            InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(TransportSheetHeader."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",FALSE,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,FALSE,TransportSheetHeader."Posting Date",TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Receive");
        END;
      UNTIL TransportSheetLine.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillComplaintBarcodeEntries@50024(Rec@50000 : Record 50021;SkipRelease@50006 : Boolean;DocTypeLoc@50005 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima@50004 : Record 50022;
      BarcodeAppEntry@50007 : Record 50179;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      CLEAR(LineNo);

      IF NOT IsCrossDockRespCenter(Rec."Store Responsible Center") THEN
        EXIT;

      Rec.TESTFIELD(Status,Rec.Status::Released);

      DeleteBarcodeEntries(Rec."Document No.",DocTypeLoc);

      ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima.RESET;
      ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima.SETRANGE("Document No.",Rec."Document No.");
      IF NOT ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      REPEAT
        LineNo += 10000;
        BarcodeAppEntry.RESET;
        BarcodeAppEntry.INIT;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Document No." := Rec."Document No.";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Line No." := LineNo;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Document Type" := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc);
        BarcodeAppEntry."Document Date" := Rec."Shipment Date";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Source No." := Rec."Store Responsible Center";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Destination No." := Rec.Store;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Responsibility Center" := Rec."Store Responsible Center";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item No." := ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima."Complaint No.";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item Description" := FORMAT(Rec."Shipment Date")+', '+ Rec.FIELDCAPTION(Store)+ ': ' +Rec.Store+', '+ Rec.FIELDCAPTION("Store Responsible Center")+ ': ' +Rec."Store Responsible Center";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item Qty." := ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima."Package Quantity";
        BarcodeAppEntry.Barcode := ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima."Complaint No.";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Scanning Qty." := 1;
        BarcodeAppEntry.INSERT;
      UNTIL ComplaintShipmentLinesPrima.NEXT = 0;
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FillWhseShptBarcodeEntriesCrossDock@50036(WhseShpt@50002 : Record 7320;DocTypeLoc@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      BarcodeAppEntry@50000 : Record 50179;
      i@50004 : Integer;
      NoOfPallets@50006 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF (WhseShpt."No. of Pallets" = 0) AND (WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages" = 0) AND (WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages" = 0) AND (WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No." = '') THEN
        EXIT;

      CASE WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" OF
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Ready for CD Shipment":
          DocTypeLoc := DocTypeLoc::WHCDSHIP;
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD",WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)":
          DocTypeLoc := DocTypeLoc::WHCDREC;
        WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD",WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)":
          DocTypeLoc := DocTypeLoc::WHCDSHFROM;
        ELSE BEGIN
          DeleteBarcodeEntries(WhseShpt."No.",DocTypeLoc);
          EXIT;
        END;
      END;

      DeleteBarcodeEntries(WhseShpt."No.",DocTypeLoc);

      CLEAR(LineNo);

      IF WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No." <> '' THEN
        NoOfPallets := 1
      ELSE
        NoOfPallets := WhseShpt."No. of Pallets";

      FOR i := 1 TO NoOfPallets DO BEGIN
        LineNo += 10000;
        BarcodeAppEntry.RESET;
        BarcodeAppEntry.INIT;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Document No." := WhseShpt."No.";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Line No." := LineNo;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Document Type" := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc);
        BarcodeAppEntry."Document Date" := WhseShpt."Posting Date";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Source No." := WhseShpt."Location Code";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Destination No." := WhseShpt."Destination No.";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item Description" := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.", i, NoOfPallets, 2);
        IF WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No." <> '' THEN
          BarcodeAppEntry."Item Description" := BarcodeAppEntry."Item Description" + ' - ' + WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No.";
        BarcodeAppEntry.Barcode := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.", i, NoOfPallets, 1);
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item Qty." := 1;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Scanning Qty." := 1;
        BarcodeAppEntry.INSERT;
      END;

      FOR i := 1 TO WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages" DO BEGIN
        LineNo += 10000;
        BarcodeAppEntry.RESET;
        BarcodeAppEntry.INIT;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Document No." := WhseShpt."No.";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Line No." := LineNo;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Document Type" := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc);
        BarcodeAppEntry."Document Date" := WhseShpt."Posting Date";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Source No." := WhseShpt."Location Code";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Destination No." := WhseShpt."Destination No.";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item Description" := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.", i, WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages", 4);
        BarcodeAppEntry.Barcode := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.", i, WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages", 3);
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item Qty." := 1;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Scanning Qty." := 1;
        BarcodeAppEntry.INSERT;
      END;

      FOR i := 1 TO WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages" DO BEGIN
        LineNo += 10000;
        BarcodeAppEntry.RESET;
        BarcodeAppEntry.INIT;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Document No." := WhseShpt."No.";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Line No." := LineNo;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Document Type" := FORMAT(DocTypeLoc);
        BarcodeAppEntry."Document Date" := WhseShpt."Posting Date";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Source No." := WhseShpt."Location Code";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Destination No." := WhseShpt."Destination No.";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item Description" := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.", i, WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages", 6);
        BarcodeAppEntry.Barcode := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.", i, WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages", 5);
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item Qty." := 1;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Scanning Qty." := 1;
        BarcodeAppEntry.INSERT;
      END;
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE FindScannedEntries@50009(DocNo@50001 : Code[20];DocType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM,RetailShpt');
    VAR
      EntriesInserted@50004 : Boolean;
      BarcodeAppMgmt@50002 : Codeunit 50112;
      BarcodeAppRecordings@50005 : Record 50180;
    BEGIN
      CLEAR(EntriesInserted);

      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::Shipment:
          BarcodeAppRecordings.SETRANGE("Document Type",UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Shipment)));

        DocType::RetailShpt:
          BarcodeAppRecordings.SETRANGE("Document Type",UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::RetailShpt)));

        DocType::Prereceipt:
          BarcodeAppRecordings.SETRANGE("Document Type",UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Prereceipt)));
        DocType::Receipt:
          BarcodeAppRecordings.SETRANGE("Document Type",UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Receipt)));
        DocType::Transport:
          BarcodeAppRecordings.SETRANGE("Document Type",UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Transport)));
        DocType::WHCDSHIP:
          BarcodeAppRecordings.SETRANGE("Document Type",UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::WHCDSHIP)));
        DocType::WHCDREC:
          BarcodeAppRecordings.SETRANGE("Document Type",UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::WHCDREC)));
        DocType::WHCDSHFROM:
          BarcodeAppRecordings.SETRANGE("Document Type",UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::WHCDSHFROM)));
        DocType::CPCDSHIP:
          BarcodeAppRecordings.SETRANGE("Document Type",UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::CPCDSHIP)));
        DocType::CPCDREC:
          BarcodeAppRecordings.SETRANGE("Document Type",UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::CPCDREC)));
        DocType::CPCDSHFROM:
          BarcodeAppRecordings.SETRANGE("Document Type",UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::CPCDSHFROM)));
      END;

      BarcodeAppRecordings.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      IF BarcodeAppRecordings.FINDSET(TRUE) THEN REPEAT
        InsertScannedEntries(BarcodeAppRecordings,DocNo,DocType);
        BarcodeAppRecordings.DELETE(TRUE);
        COMMIT;
        EntriesInserted := TRUE;
      UNTIL BarcodeAppRecordings.NEXT = 0;

      IF EntriesInserted THEN
        DeleteBarcodeEntries(DocNo,DocType);
    END;

    PROCEDURE InsertScannedEntries@50008(BarcodeAppRecordings@50000 : Record 50180;DocNo@50004 : Code[20];DocType@50005 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM,RetailShpt');
    VAR
      ScannedDocumentLines@50001 : Record 50111;
      Item@50002 : Record 27;
      DescriptionValue@50003 : Text[110];
      NoValue@50006 : Text;
      BarcodeItem@50007 : Text;
      BarcodeQty@50008 : Decimal;
      BarcodeUoM@50009 : Text;
    BEGIN
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE(uuid,BarcodeAppRecordings."Recording Guid");
      IF NOT ScannedDocumentLines.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
        CASE DocType OF
          DocType::WHCDSHIP,DocType::WHCDREC,DocType::WHCDSHFROM:
            BEGIN
              NoValue := BarcodeAppRecordings.Barcode;
              BarcodeItem := BarcodeAppRecordings.Barcode;
              BarcodeQty := 1;
              BarcodeUoM := 'KOM';
              // todo
              // <PRICO-2025-67>
              {
              IF Item.GET(BarcodeItem) THEN
                DescriptionValue := Item."Full Description";
              }
              IF STRLEN(BarcodeItem) <= MAXSTRLEN(Item."No.") THEN
                IF Item.GET(BarcodeItem) THEN
                  DescriptionValue := Item."Full Description";
              // </PRICO-2025-67>
              // todo
            END ;
          DocType::CPCDSHIP,DocType::CPCDREC,DocType::CPCDSHFROM:
            BEGIN
              NoValue := BarcodeAppRecordings.Barcode;
              BarcodeItem := BarcodeAppRecordings.Barcode;
              BarcodeQty := BarcodeAppRecordings."Scanned Quantity";
              // todo
              // <PRICO-2025-67>
              {
              IF Item.GET(BarcodeItem) THEN
                DescriptionValue := Item."Full Description";
              }
              IF STRLEN(BarcodeItem) <= MAXSTRLEN(Item."No.") THEN
                IF Item.GET(BarcodeItem) THEN
                  DescriptionValue := Item."Full Description";
              // </PRICO-2025-67>
              // todo
            END;
          ELSE BEGIN
            GetBarcodeFields(BarcodeAppRecordings,BarcodeItem,BarcodeUoM,BarcodeQty);
            IF Item.GET(BarcodeItem) THEN;
            NoValue := Item."No.";
            DescriptionValue := Item."Full Description";
          END;
        END;

        ScannedDocumentLines.RESET;
        ScannedDocumentLines.INIT;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Document Type" := DocType;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Document No." := DocNo;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Item No." := NoValue;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Item Description" := DescriptionValue;
        ScannedDocumentLines.Barcode := BarcodeItem;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Full Barcode Value" := BarcodeAppRecordings.Barcode;
        ScannedDocumentLines.Quantity := BarcodeQty;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Scanned Quantity" := BarcodeQty;
        ScannedDocumentLines."Unit of Measure" := BarcodeUoM;
        ScannedDocumentLines."User ID" := BarcodeAppRecordings."User ID";
        ScannedDocumentLines."Creation DateTime" := BarcodeAppRecordings."Creation DateTime";
        ScannedDocumentLines.uuid := BarcodeAppRecordings."Recording Guid";
        ScannedDocumentLines."Entry Inserted" := CURRENTDATETIME;
        ScannedDocumentLines.INSERT;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetBarcodeFields@50011(BarcodeAppRecordings@50001 : Record 50180;VAR BarcodeItem@50006 : Text;VAR BarcodeUoM@50005 : Text;VAR BarcodeQty@50004 : Decimal);
    VAR
      Barcodes@50002 : Record 99001451;
      Item@50003 : Record 27;
      BarcodeNo@50000 : Text[40];
      BarcodeCode@50007 : Text;
    BEGIN
      CLEAR(BarcodeItem);
      CLEAR(BarcodeUoM);
      CLEAR(BarcodeQty);
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      // Kept even when the barcode is unknown, so the scan still shows up as surplus
      BarcodeQty := BarcodeAppRecordings."Scanned Quantity";
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
      BarcodeNo := CONVERTSTR(BarcodeAppRecordings.Barcode,',','.');

      IF STRPOS(BarcodeNo,'|') <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        // <PRICO-2025-67>
        {
        BarcodeItem := COPYSTR(BarcodeNo,1,STRPOS(BarcodeNo,'|')-1);
        BarcodeNo := COPYSTR(BarcodeNo,STRLEN(BarcodeItem)+2,STRLEN(BarcodeNo));
        BarcodeUoM := COPYSTR(BarcodeNo,1,STRPOS(BarcodeNo,'|')-1);
        BarcodeQty := BarcodeAppRecordings."Scanned Quantity";
        }
        // BARCODE|UOM|QTY: BARCODE is a barcode or an item number, the unit of measure is the printed one
        BarcodeCode := COPYSTR(BarcodeNo,1,STRPOS(BarcodeNo,'|') - 1);
        BarcodeNo := COPYSTR(BarcodeNo,STRPOS(BarcodeNo,'|') + 1);
        IF STRPOS(BarcodeNo,'|') <> 0 THEN
          BarcodeNo := COPYSTR(BarcodeNo,1,STRPOS(BarcodeNo,'|') - 1);
        BarcodeItem := FindItemNoForBarcode(BarcodeCode);
        BarcodeUoM := BarcodeNo;
        // </PRICO-2025-67>
        EXIT;
      END;

      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      {
      IF Barcodes.GET(BarcodeAppRecordings.Barcode) THEN BEGIN
        BarcodeItem := Barcodes."Item No.";
        BarcodeUoM := Barcodes."Unit of Measure Code";
        BarcodeQty := BarcodeAppRecordings."Scanned Quantity";
      END ELSE IF Item.GET(BarcodeAppRecordings.Barcode) THEN BEGIN
        BarcodeItem := Item."No.";
        BarcodeUoM := Item."Base Unit of Measure";
        BarcodeQty := BarcodeAppRecordings."Scanned Quantity";
      END;
      }
      IF STRLEN(BarcodeAppRecordings.Barcode) <= MAXSTRLEN(Barcodes."Barcode No.") THEN
        IF Barcodes.GET(BarcodeAppRecordings.Barcode) THEN BEGIN
          BarcodeItem := Barcodes."Item No.";
          BarcodeUoM := Barcodes."Unit of Measure Code";
          EXIT;
        END;

      IF STRLEN(BarcodeAppRecordings.Barcode) <= MAXSTRLEN(Item."No.") THEN
        IF Item.GET(BarcodeAppRecordings.Barcode) THEN BEGIN
          BarcodeItem := Item."No.";
          BarcodeUoM := Item."Base Unit of Measure";
        END;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteBarcodeEntries@50005(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocTypeLoc@50009 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM,RetailShpt');
    VAR
      BarcodeAppEntry@50003 : Record 50179;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      BarcodeAppEntry.SETRANGE("Document No.", DocNo);
      BarcodeAppEntry.SETRANGE("Document Type", FORMAT(DocTypeLoc));
      IF NOT BarcodeAppEntry.ISEMPTY THEN
        BarcodeAppEntry.DELETEALL;
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE DeleteBarcodeEntriesOnDeleteDocument@50042(DocNo@50000 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      BarcodeAppEntry@50003 : Record 50179;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      BarcodeAppEntry.SETRANGE("Document No.", DocNo);
      IF NOT BarcodeAppEntry.ISEMPTY THEN
        BarcodeAppEntry.DELETEALL;
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry@50014(DocNo@50009 : Code[20];DocType@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM,RetailShpt';Document_LineNo@50004 : Integer;IsBOM@50002 : Boolean;BOMComponent@50001 : Record 90;BarcodeNo@50006 : Text[200];IsRetailLoc@50020 : Boolean;DocumentDate@50022 : Date;PartialQtyOverride@50023 : Decimal);
    VAR
      Item@50000 : Record 27;
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50007 : Record 7321;
      WarehouseReceiptLine@50008 : Record 7317;
      TransportSheetLine@50014 : Record 50016;
      TransportSheetHeader@50015 : Record 50015;
      Qty@50010 : Decimal;
      LocationCode@50011 : Code[20];
      ItemNo@50012 : Code[20];
      FullItemDescription@50013 : Text[110];
      DestinationNo@50024 : Code[20];
      UomCode@50025 : Code[10];
      BarcodeAppEntry@50019 : Record 50179;
      UnitofMeasure@50021 : Record 204;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::Shipment,DocType::RetailShpt:
          IF WarehouseShipmentLine.GET(DocNo, Document_LineNo) THEN BEGIN
            Qty := WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship";
            LocationCode := WarehouseShipmentLine."Location Code";
            ItemNo := WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.";
            FullItemDescription := WarehouseShipmentLine."Full Item Description";
            DestinationNo := WarehouseShipmentLine."Destination No.";
            UomCode := WarehouseShipmentLine."Unit of Measure Code";
          END;
        DocType::Receipt:
          IF WarehouseReceiptLine.GET(DocNo, Document_LineNo) THEN BEGIN
            Qty := WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive";
            LocationCode := WarehouseReceiptLine."Location Code";
            ItemNo := WarehouseReceiptLine."Item No.";
            FullItemDescription := WarehouseReceiptLine."Full Item Description";
            UomCode := WarehouseReceiptLine."Unit of Measure Code";
          END;
        DocType::Transport:
          IF TransportSheetLine.GET(DocNo, Document_LineNo) THEN BEGIN
            TransportSheetHeader.GET(DocNo);
            IF PartialQtyOverride <> 0 THEN
              Qty := PartialQtyOverride
            ELSE
              Qty := TransportSheetLine.Quantity;
            LocationCode := TransportSheetHeader."Receiving Location";
            ItemNo := TransportSheetLine."Item No.";
            FullItemDescription := TransportSheetLine."Item Description";
            DestinationNo := TransportSheetHeader."Destination No.";
            UomCode := TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure";
          END;
      END;

      IF IsBOM THEN
        UomCode := BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code";

      BarcodeAppEntry.RESET;
      BarcodeAppEntry.SETRANGE("Document No.", DocNo);
      BarcodeAppEntry.SETRANGE("Document Type", FORMAT(DocType));
      BarcodeAppEntry.SETRANGE(Barcode, BarcodeNo);
      IF IsBOM THEN
        BarcodeAppEntry.SETRANGE("Item No.", BOMComponent."No.")
      ELSE
        BarcodeAppEntry.SETRANGE("Item No.", ItemNo);

      IF BarcodeAppEntry.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        IF IsBOM THEN
          BarcodeAppEntry."Item Qty." += Qty * BOMComponent."Quantity per"
        ELSE
          BarcodeAppEntry."Item Qty." += Qty;
        BarcodeAppEntry.MODIFY;
        EXIT;
      END;

      BarcodeAppEntry.RESET;
      BarcodeAppEntry.INIT;
      BarcodeAppEntry."Document No." := DocNo;
      LineNo += 10000;
      BarcodeAppEntry."Line No." := LineNo;
      BarcodeAppEntry."Document Type" := FORMAT(DocType);
      BarcodeAppEntry."Document Date" := DocumentDate;
      BarcodeAppEntry.Barcode := BarcodeNo;
      BarcodeAppEntry."Responsibility Center" := ResponsibilityCenterLocation."Responsibility Center";
      BarcodeAppEntry."Source No." := LocationCode;
      BarcodeAppEntry."Destination No." := DestinationNo;
      BarcodeAppEntry."Item UoM" := UomCode;
      BarcodeAppEntry."Retail Location" := IsRetailLoc;

      IF IsBOM THEN BEGIN
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item No." := BOMComponent."No.";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item Description" := BOMComponent."Full Item Description";
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item Qty." := Qty * BOMComponent."Quantity per";

        IF UnitofMeasure.GET(UomCode) AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN
          BarcodeAppEntry."Scanning Qty." := BOMComponent."Quantity per"
        ELSE IF BOMComponent."Package Qty." <> 0 THEN
          BarcodeAppEntry."Scanning Qty." := BOMComponent."Package Qty."
        ELSE IF Item.GET(BOMComponent."No.") AND (Item."Package Qty." <> 0) THEN
          BarcodeAppEntry."Scanning Qty." := Item."Package Qty."
        ELSE
          BarcodeAppEntry."Scanning Qty." := 1;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item No." := ItemNo;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item Description" := FullItemDescription;
        BarcodeAppEntry."Item Qty." := Qty;

        IF UnitofMeasure.GET(UomCode) AND UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode" THEN
          BarcodeAppEntry."Scanning Qty." := Qty
        ELSE IF Item.GET(ItemNo) AND (Item."Package Qty." <> 0) THEN
          BarcodeAppEntry."Scanning Qty." := Item."Package Qty."
        ELSE
          BarcodeAppEntry."Scanning Qty." := 1;
      END;

      BarcodeAppEntry.INSERT;
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertSplitBEMFEntries@50016(DocNo@50019 : Code[20];DocType@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM,RetailShpt,RetailRcpt';Document_LineNo@50009 : Integer;IsBOM@50006 : Boolean;BOMComponent@50004 : Record 90;BarcodeNo@50000 : Text[200];IsRetailLoc@50008 : Boolean;DocumentDate@50022 : Date;PartialQtyOverride@50023 : Decimal);
    BEGIN
      InsertBarcodeAppLineEntry(DocNo,DocType,Document_LineNo,IsBOM,BOMComponent,BarcodeNo,IsRetailLoc,DocumentDate,PartialQtyOverride); // <PRICO-2025-67/>
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsCrossDockLocation@50027(LocationNo@50000 : Code[20]) : Boolean;
    VAR
      Location@50001 : Record 14;
    BEGIN
      EXIT(Location.GET(LocationNo) AND Location."Use Cross-Dock Warehouse"); // <PRICO-2025-67/>
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsCrossDockRespCenter@50028(RespCenterNo@50000 : Code[20]) : Boolean;
    VAR
      ResponsibilityCenter@50001 : Record 5714;
    BEGIN
      EXIT(ResponsibilityCenter.GET(RespCenterNo) AND ResponsibilityCenter."Use Cross-Dock Warehouse"); // <PRICO-2025-67/>
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsValidCrossDockStatus@50022(PreviousStatusIndex@50003 : Integer;NextStatusIndex@50002 : Integer) : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF PreviousStatusIndex = NextStatusIndex THEN
        EXIT(TRUE);

      CASE TRUE OF
        (PreviousStatusIndex = 0) AND (NextStatusIndex = 1):
          EXIT(TRUE);
        (PreviousStatusIndex = 1) AND (NextStatusIndex = 2) OR (NextStatusIndex = 3):
          EXIT(TRUE);
        (NextStatusIndex = 4) AND (NextStatusIndex = 5):
          EXIT(TRUE);
        (PreviousStatusIndex = 6) AND (NextStatusIndex = 7):
          EXIT(TRUE);
        NextStatusIndex = 8:
          EXIT(TRUE);
      END;
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode@50026(DocNo@50000 : Code[40];PackageNo@50001 : Integer;TotalPackageNo@50003 : Integer;LineType@50002 : Integer) : Code[40];
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      CASE LineType OF
        1: EXIT(DocNo + 'PAL' + FORMAT(PackageNo) + '/' + FORMAT(TotalPackageNo));
        2: EXIT(DocNo + ' PALETA ' + FORMAT(PackageNo) + '/' + FORMAT(TotalPackageNo));
        3: EXIT(DocNo + 'PK' + FORMAT(PackageNo) + '/' + FORMAT(TotalPackageNo));
        4: EXIT(DocNo + ' DODATNIH PAKETA ' + FORMAT(PackageNo) + '/' + FORMAT(TotalPackageNo));
        5: EXIT(DocNo + 'PLDP' + FORMAT(PackageNo) + '/' + FORMAT(TotalPackageNo));
        6: EXIT(DocNo + ' PALETA DOD. PAKETA ' + FORMAT(PackageNo) + '/' + FORMAT(TotalPackageNo));
      END;
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetItemBarcode@50010(ItemNo@50000 : Code[20];UomCode@50003 : Code[10];VariantCode@50004 : Code[10]) : Code[50];
    VAR
      Barcodes@50001 : Record 99001451;
      Item@50002 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF (NOT Item.GET(ItemNo)) OR (Item.Type = Item.Type::Service) THEN
        EXIT;

      Barcodes.RESET;
      Barcodes.SETRANGE("Item No.",ItemNo);
      Barcodes.SETRANGE("Unit of Measure Code", UomCode);
      IF VariantCode <> '' THEN
        Barcodes.SETRANGE("Variant Code",VariantCode);
      IF Barcodes.FINDLAST THEN
        EXIT(Barcodes."Barcode No.")
      ELSE
        EXIT(ItemNo);
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Codeunit,7310,OnBeforeRelease)]
    PROCEDURE WhseShptStatus_OnBeforeRelease@50100(VAR WarehouseShipmentHeader@50000 : Record 7320);
    BEGIN

        EXIT;

      CreateBarcodeAppEntries(WarehouseShipmentHeader,FALSE);
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    [EventSubscriber(Codeunit,7310,OnBeforeReopen)]
    PROCEDURE WhseShptStatus_OnBeforeReOpen@50101(VAR WarehouseShipmentHeader@50000 : Record 7320);
    VAR
      DocTypeLoc@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
      BarcodeStatusErr@50002 : TextConst 'ENU=Barcode status of document %1 must not be %2.;HRV=Barkod status dokumenta %1 ne smije biti %2.';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.ISTEMPORARY THEN
        EXIT;

      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(BarcodeStatusErr,WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed));

      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.Status = WarehouseShipmentHeader.Status::Open THEN
        EXIT;

      DeleteBarcodeEntries(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.", DocTypeLoc::Shipment);
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    END;
