    [External]
    PROCEDURE CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument@50200(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50002 : Boolean) IsCompletedStatus : Boolean;
    VAR
      TempScan@50003 : TEMPORARY Record 50111;
      TempDocLine@50004 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;
      TempComponentLine@50005 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      // Replaces BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage. The buffer is computed in memory and written once.
      IF NOT IsBarcodeAppDocument(DocNo,DocType) THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);

      DeleteBufferLines(DocNo,DocType);

      FindScannedEntries(DocNo,DocType);

      IF NOT LoadScans(DocNo,DocType,TempScan) THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);

      CreateDocumentLines(DocNo,DocType,TempDocLine,TempComponentLine);
      AllocateExactQtyScans(TempDocLine,TempComponentLine,TempScan);
      AllocateScans(TempDocLine,TempComponentLine,TempScan);
      // Whatever no line could take is surplus, including items that are not on the document at all
      CreateSurplusLines(DocNo,DocType,TempDocLine,TempScan);
      InsertBufferLines(TempDocLine,TempComponentLine);

      IsCompletedStatus := ChangeDocumentStatus(DocNo,DocType,RunChangeDocStatus);
      IF IsCompletedStatus THEN
        DeleteBarcodeEntries(DocNo,DocType);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetIsBulkUpdate@50201(NewIsBulkUpdate@50000 : Boolean);
    BEGIN
      IsBulkUpdate := NewIsBulkUpdate; // <PRICO-2025-67/>
    END;

    PROCEDURE IsBarcodeAppDocument@50202(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM') : Boolean;
    VAR
      Location@50002 : Record 14;
      WarehouseShipmentHeader@50003 : Record 7320;
      WarehouseReceiptHeader@50004 : Record 7316;
      TransportSheetHeader@50005 : Record 50015;
      ComplaintShpt@50006 : Record 50021;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::Shipment:
          IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN
            IF Location.GET(WarehouseShipmentHeader."Location Code") THEN
              EXIT(Location."Use in BE-Mobile (shipment)");
        DocType::Receipt:
          IF WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN
            IF Location.GET(WarehouseReceiptHeader."Location Code") THEN
              EXIT(Location."Use in BE-Mobile (receipt)");
        DocType::Transport:
          IF TransportSheetHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN
            IF Location.GET(TransportSheetHeader."Receiving Location") THEN
              EXIT(Location."Use in BE-Mobile (receipt)");
        DocType::WHCDSHIP,DocType::WHCDREC,DocType::WHCDSHFROM:
          IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN
            EXIT(IsCrossDockLocation(WarehouseShipmentHeader."Location Code"));
        DocType::CPCDSHIP,DocType::CPCDREC,DocType::CPCDSHFROM:
          IF ComplaintShpt.GET(DocNo) THEN
            EXIT(IsCrossDockRespCenter(ComplaintShpt."Store Responsible Center"));
      END;

      EXIT(FALSE);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE DeleteBufferLines@50203(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      BufferLine@50002 : Record 50113;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      BufferLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      BufferLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      IF NOT BufferLine.ISEMPTY THEN
        BufferLine.DELETEALL;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE LoadScans@50204(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';VAR TempScan@50002 : TEMPORARY Record 50111) : Boolean;
    VAR
      ScannedDocumentLines@50003 : Record 50111;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      TempScan.RESET;
      TempScan.DELETEALL;

      ScannedDocumentLines.SETCURRENTKEY("Document Type","Document No.","Item No.");
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETFILTER("Scanned Quantity",'>0');
      IF NOT ScannedDocumentLines.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);

      REPEAT
        TempScan := ScannedDocumentLines;
        // Rows imported before GetBarcodeFields resolved BARCODE|UOM|QTY to an item have no item yet
        IF TempScan."Item No." = '' THEN
          TempScan."Item No." := FindItemNoForBarcode(TempScan."Full Barcode Value");
        TempScan.INSERT;
      UNTIL ScannedDocumentLines.NEXT = 0;

      EXIT(TRUE);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE FindItemNoForBarcode@50205(FullBarcodeValue@50000 : Text) : Code[20];
    VAR
      Barcodes@50001 : Record 99001451;
      Item@50002 : Record 27;
      BarcodeNo@50003 : Text;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      BarcodeNo := FullBarcodeValue;
      IF STRPOS(BarcodeNo,'|') > 0 THEN
        BarcodeNo := COPYSTR(BarcodeNo,1,STRPOS(BarcodeNo,'|') - 1);
      IF BarcodeNo = '' THEN
        EXIT('');

      IF STRLEN(BarcodeNo) <= MAXSTRLEN(Barcodes."Barcode No.") THEN
        IF Barcodes.GET(BarcodeNo) THEN
          EXIT(Barcodes."Item No.");

      IF STRLEN(BarcodeNo) <= MAXSTRLEN(Item."No.") THEN
        IF Item.GET(BarcodeNo) THEN
          EXIT(Item."No.");

      EXIT('');
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateDocumentLines@50206(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';VAR TempDocLine@50002 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempComponentLine@50003 : TEMPORARY Record 50113);
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::Shipment:
          CreateWhseShipmentLines(DocNo,DocType,TempDocLine,TempComponentLine);
        DocType::Receipt:
          CreateWhseReceiptLines(DocNo,DocType,TempDocLine,TempComponentLine);
        DocType::Transport:
          CreateTransportSheetLines(DocNo,DocType,TempDocLine,TempComponentLine);
        DocType::WHCDSHIP,DocType::WHCDREC,DocType::WHCDSHFROM:
          CreateCrossDockPackageLines(DocNo,DocType,TempDocLine,TempComponentLine);
        DocType::CPCDSHIP,DocType::CPCDREC,DocType::CPCDSHFROM:
          CreateComplaintShipmentLines(DocNo,DocType,TempDocLine,TempComponentLine);
      END;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateWhseShipmentLines@50207(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';VAR TempDocLine@50002 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempComponentLine@50003 : TEMPORARY Record 50113);
    VAR
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50004 : Record 7321;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      // Buffer lines are numbered in shipment date order, so scans fill the earliest due lines first
      WarehouseShipmentLine.SETCURRENTKEY("Shipment Date");
      WarehouseShipmentLine.SETRANGE("No.",DocNo);
      IF WarehouseShipmentLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          InsertDocumentLine(TempDocLine,TempComponentLine,DocNo,DocType,WarehouseShipmentLine."Line No.",WarehouseShipmentLine."Item No.",WarehouseShipmentLine."Full Item Description",WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship",WarehouseShipmentLine."Unit of Measure Code",WarehouseShipmentLine."Location Code",TRUE);
        UNTIL WarehouseShipmentLine.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateWhseReceiptLines@50208(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';VAR TempDocLine@50002 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempComponentLine@50003 : TEMPORARY Record 50113);
    VAR
      WarehouseReceiptLine@50004 : Record 7317;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      WarehouseReceiptLine.SETRANGE("No.",DocNo);
      IF WarehouseReceiptLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          InsertDocumentLine(TempDocLine,TempComponentLine,DocNo,DocType,WarehouseReceiptLine."Line No.",WarehouseReceiptLine."Item No.",WarehouseReceiptLine."Full Item Description",WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive",WarehouseReceiptLine."Unit of Measure Code",WarehouseReceiptLine."Location Code",TRUE);
        UNTIL WarehouseReceiptLine.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateTransportSheetLines@50209(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';VAR TempDocLine@50002 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempComponentLine@50003 : TEMPORARY Record 50113);
    VAR
      TransportSheetLine@50004 : Record 50016;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      // Shipped and not yet received: the quantity the app was given and the receipt posts
      TransportSheetLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      TransportSheetLine.SETFILTER("Qty. to Receive",'>0');
      IF TransportSheetLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          InsertDocumentLine(TempDocLine,TempComponentLine,DocNo,DocType,TransportSheetLine."Line No.",TransportSheetLine."Item No.",COPYSTR(TransportSheetLine."Item Description",1,110),TransportSheetLine."Qty. to Receive",TransportSheetLine."Unit of Measure",TransportSheetLine."Location Code",TRUE);
        UNTIL TransportSheetLine.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateCrossDockPackageLines@50210(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';VAR TempDocLine@50002 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempComponentLine@50003 : TEMPORARY Record 50113);
    VAR
      WhseShpt@50004 : Record 7320;
      NoOfPallets@50005 : Integer;
      PackageLineNo@50006 : Integer;
      PalletDescription@50007 : Text[110];
      i@50008 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF NOT WhseShpt.GET(DocNo) THEN
        EXIT;
      IF (WhseShpt."No. of Pallets" = 0) AND (WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages" = 0) AND (WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages" = 0) AND (WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No." = '') THEN
        EXIT;

      // The same packages FillWhseShptBarcodeEntriesCrossDock offers the app, or its scans could never match
      IF WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No." <> '' THEN
        NoOfPallets := 1
      ELSE
        NoOfPallets := WhseShpt."No. of Pallets";

      FOR i := 1 TO NoOfPallets DO BEGIN
        PackageLineNo += 10000;
        PalletDescription := GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.",i,NoOfPallets,2);
        IF WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No." <> '' THEN
          PalletDescription := PalletDescription + ' - ' + WhseShpt."Linked Whse. Shpt. No.";
        InsertDocumentLine(TempDocLine,TempComponentLine,DocNo,DocType,PackageLineNo,GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.",i,NoOfPallets,1),PalletDescription,1,'',WhseShpt."Location Code",FALSE);
      END;

      FOR i := 1 TO WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages" DO BEGIN
        PackageLineNo += 10000;
        InsertDocumentLine(TempDocLine,TempComponentLine,DocNo,DocType,PackageLineNo,GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.",i,WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages",3),GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.",i,WhseShpt."No. of Additional Packages",4),1,'',WhseShpt."Location Code",FALSE);
      END;

      FOR i := 1 TO WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages" DO BEGIN
        PackageLineNo += 10000;
        InsertDocumentLine(TempDocLine,TempComponentLine,DocNo,DocType,PackageLineNo,GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.",i,WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages",5),GetWhseShptCrossDockBarcode(WhseShpt."No.",i,WhseShpt."No. of Pallets Add. Packages",6),1,'',WhseShpt."Location Code",FALSE);
      END;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateComplaintShipmentLines@50211(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';VAR TempDocLine@50002 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempComponentLine@50003 : TEMPORARY Record 50113);
    VAR
      ComplaintShipmentLine@50004 : Record 50022;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      ComplaintShipmentLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      IF ComplaintShipmentLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          InsertDocumentLine(TempDocLine,TempComponentLine,DocNo,DocType,ComplaintShipmentLine."Line No.",ComplaintShipmentLine."Complaint No.",ComplaintShipmentLine."Customer No" + ' - ' + ComplaintShipmentLine."Document No.",ComplaintShipmentLine."Package Quantity",'','',FALSE);
        UNTIL ComplaintShipmentLine.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertDocumentLine@50212(VAR TempDocLine@50000 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempComponentLine@50001 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;DocNo@50002 : Code[20];DocType@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';DocumentLineNo@50004 : Integer;ItemNo@50005 : Code[40];ItemDescription@50006 : Text[110];Qty@50007 : Decimal;UomCode@50008 : Code[10];LocationCode@50009 : Code[10];ExpandBOM@50010 : Boolean);
    VAR
      BOMComponent@50011 : Record 90;
      BufferLineNo@50012 : Integer;
      ComponentLineNo@50013 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      TempDocLine.RESET;
      IF TempDocLine.FINDLAST THEN
        BufferLineNo := TempDocLine."Line No." + 10000
      ELSE
        BufferLineNo := 10000;

      TempDocLine.INIT;
      TempDocLine."Document Type" := DocType;
      TempDocLine."Document No." := DocNo;
      TempDocLine."Line No." := BufferLineNo;
      TempDocLine."Document Line No." := DocumentLineNo;
      TempDocLine.Version := 1;
      TempDocLine."Parent Line No." := BufferLineNo;
      TempDocLine.Indentation := 0;
      TempDocLine."Item No." := ItemNo;
      TempDocLine."Item Description" := ItemDescription;
      TempDocLine.Quantity := Qty;
      TempDocLine."Quantity on Document" := Qty;
      TempDocLine."Unit of Measure" := UomCode;
      TempDocLine."Location Code" := LocationCode;
      TempDocLine."Shipment No." := DocNo;
      TempDocLine.INSERT;

      IF NOT ExpandBOM THEN
        EXIT;

      // Components are numbered right after their parent, which keeps them under it in the page tree
      ComponentLineNo := BufferLineNo;
      BOMComponent.SETRANGE("Parent Item No.",ItemNo);
      BOMComponent.SETRANGE(Type,BOMComponent.Type::Item);
      IF BOMComponent.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          ComponentLineNo += 1;
          TempComponentLine.INIT;
          TempComponentLine."Document Type" := DocType;
          TempComponentLine."Document No." := DocNo;
          TempComponentLine."Line No." := ComponentLineNo;
          TempComponentLine."Document Line No." := 0;
          TempComponentLine.Version := 1;
          TempComponentLine."Parent Line No." := BufferLineNo;
          TempComponentLine."Parent Item No." := ItemNo;
          TempComponentLine.Indentation := 1;
          TempComponentLine."Item No." := BOMComponent."No.";
          TempComponentLine."Item Description" := BOMComponent."Full Item Description";
          TempComponentLine."Qty per" := BOMComponent."Quantity per";
          TempComponentLine.Quantity := Qty * BOMComponent."Quantity per";
          TempComponentLine."Quantity on Document" := TempComponentLine.Quantity;
          TempComponentLine."Unit of Measure" := BOMComponent."Unit of Measure Code";
          TempComponentLine."Location Code" := LocationCode;
          TempComponentLine."Shipment No." := DocNo;
          TempComponentLine.INSERT;
        UNTIL BOMComponent.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE AllocateExactQtyScans@50213(VAR TempDocLine@50000 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempComponentLine@50001 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempScan@50002 : TEMPORARY Record 50111);
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      // A quantity barcode (BARCODE|UOM|QTY) carries one line's exact quantity. Such a scan goes to that
      // line before the FIFO pass, which would otherwise spread it over every line of the same item.
      TempDocLine.RESET;
      IF TempDocLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          IF (TempDocLine.Quantity > 0) AND IsQtyBarcodeUoM(TempDocLine."Unit of Measure") THEN
            IF NOT HasComponentLines(TempDocLine."Line No.",TempComponentLine) THEN BEGIN
              TempScan.RESET;
              TempScan.SETCURRENTKEY("Document Type","Document No.","Item No.");
              TempScan.SETRANGE("Item No.",TempDocLine."Item No.");
              TempScan.SETRANGE("Scanned Quantity",TempDocLine.Quantity);
              IF TempScan.FINDFIRST THEN
                TakeScanQty(TempDocLine,TempScan,TempDocLine.Quantity);
            END;
        UNTIL TempDocLine.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE AllocateScans@50214(VAR TempDocLine@50000 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempComponentLine@50001 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempScan@50002 : TEMPORARY Record 50111);
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      // A BOM parent is never scanned itself: its components take the scans and it counts complete sets
      TempDocLine.RESET;
      IF TempDocLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          TempComponentLine.RESET;
          TempComponentLine.SETRANGE("Parent Line No.",TempDocLine."Line No.");
          IF TempComponentLine.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
            REPEAT
              AllocateScansToLine(TempComponentLine,TempScan);
            UNTIL TempComponentLine.NEXT = 0;
            UpdateBOMParentLine(TempDocLine,TempComponentLine);
          END ELSE
            AllocateScansToLine(TempDocLine,TempScan);
        UNTIL TempDocLine.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE AllocateScansToLine@50215(VAR TempBufferLine@50000 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempScan@50001 : TEMPORARY Record 50111);
    VAR
      RemainingQty@50002 : Decimal;
      TakeQty@50003 : Decimal;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      RemainingQty := TempBufferLine.Quantity - TempBufferLine."Scanned Quantity";
      IF RemainingQty > 0 THEN BEGIN
        TempScan.RESET;
        TempScan.SETCURRENTKEY("Document Type","Document No.","Item No.");
        TempScan.SETRANGE("Item No.",TempBufferLine."Item No.");
        IF TempScan.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            IF TempScan."Scanned Quantity" > 0 THEN BEGIN
              TakeQty := TempScan."Scanned Quantity";
              IF TakeQty > RemainingQty THEN
                TakeQty := RemainingQty;
              TakeScanQty(TempBufferLine,TempScan,TakeQty);
              RemainingQty := RemainingQty - TakeQty;
            END;
          UNTIL (RemainingQty <= 0) OR (TempScan.NEXT = 0);
      END;

      SetLineStatus(TempBufferLine);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE TakeScanQty@50216(VAR TempBufferLine@50000 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempScan@50001 : TEMPORARY Record 50111;Qty@50002 : Decimal);
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      TempBufferLine."Scanned Quantity" := TempBufferLine."Scanned Quantity" + Qty;
      IF TempScan."Creation DateTime" >= TempBufferLine."Scanning Time" THEN BEGIN
        TempBufferLine."Scanning Time" := TempScan."Creation DateTime";
        TempBufferLine."User ID" := TempScan."User ID";
      END;
      TempBufferLine.MODIFY;

      TempScan."Scanned Quantity" := TempScan."Scanned Quantity" - Qty;
      TempScan.MODIFY;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE SetLineStatus@50217(VAR TempBufferLine@50000 : TEMPORARY Record 50113);
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF TempBufferLine."Scanned Quantity" >= TempBufferLine.Quantity THEN
        TempBufferLine.Status := TempBufferLine.Status::Complete
      ELSE
        IF TempBufferLine."Scanned Quantity" = 0 THEN
          TempBufferLine.Status := TempBufferLine.Status::Missing
        ELSE
          TempBufferLine.Status := TempBufferLine.Status::Incomplete;
      TempBufferLine.MODIFY;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE UpdateBOMParentLine@50218(VAR TempDocLine@50000 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempComponentLine@50001 : TEMPORARY Record 50113);
    VAR
      CompleteSets@50002 : Decimal;
      ComponentSets@50003 : Decimal;
      AllComponentsComplete@50004 : Boolean;
      AnyComponentScanned@50005 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      // A set is complete only when every component is there, so the scarcest component decides
      AllComponentsComplete := TRUE;
      CompleteSets := TempDocLine.Quantity;

      TempComponentLine.RESET;
      TempComponentLine.SETRANGE("Parent Line No.",TempDocLine."Line No.");
      IF TempComponentLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          IF TempComponentLine.Status <> TempComponentLine.Status::Complete THEN
            AllComponentsComplete := FALSE;
          IF TempComponentLine."Scanned Quantity" > 0 THEN
            AnyComponentScanned := TRUE;
          IF TempComponentLine."Qty per" <> 0 THEN BEGIN
            ComponentSets := ROUND(TempComponentLine."Scanned Quantity" / TempComponentLine."Qty per",1,'<');
            IF ComponentSets < CompleteSets THEN
              CompleteSets := ComponentSets;
          END;
          IF TempComponentLine."Scanning Time" > TempDocLine."Scanning Time" THEN BEGIN
            TempDocLine."Scanning Time" := TempComponentLine."Scanning Time";
            TempDocLine."User ID" := TempComponentLine."User ID";
          END;
        UNTIL TempComponentLine.NEXT = 0;

      IF AllComponentsComplete THEN BEGIN
        TempDocLine."Scanned Quantity" := TempDocLine.Quantity;
        TempDocLine.Status := TempDocLine.Status::Complete;
      END ELSE BEGIN
        TempDocLine."Scanned Quantity" := CompleteSets;
        IF AnyComponentScanned THEN
          TempDocLine.Status := TempDocLine.Status::Incomplete
        ELSE
          TempDocLine.Status := TempDocLine.Status::Missing;
      END;
      TempDocLine.MODIFY;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE HasComponentLines@50219(ParentLineNo@50000 : Integer;VAR TempComponentLine@50001 : TEMPORARY Record 50113) : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      TempComponentLine.RESET;
      TempComponentLine.SETRANGE("Parent Line No.",ParentLineNo);
      EXIT(NOT TempComponentLine.ISEMPTY);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE IsQtyBarcodeUoM@50220(UomCode@50000 : Code[10]) : Boolean;
    VAR
      UnitofMeasure@50001 : Record 204;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF UomCode = '' THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);
      IF NOT UnitofMeasure.GET(UomCode) THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);
      EXIT(UnitofMeasure."Insert Qty Barcode");
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CreateSurplusLines@50221(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';VAR TempDocLine@50002 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempScan@50003 : TEMPORARY Record 50111);
    VAR
      LastLineNo@50004 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      TempDocLine.RESET;
      IF TempDocLine.FINDLAST THEN
        LastLineNo := TempDocLine."Line No.";

      // One surplus line per item and unit of measure; an unknown barcode gets a line of its own
      TempScan.RESET;
      IF TempScan.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          IF TempScan."Scanned Quantity" > 0 THEN BEGIN
            TempDocLine.RESET;
            TempDocLine.SETRANGE(Status,TempDocLine.Status::Surplus);
            TempDocLine.SETRANGE("Item No.",TempScan."Item No.");
            TempDocLine.SETRANGE("Unit of Measure",TempScan."Unit of Measure");
            IF TempScan."Item No." = '' THEN
              TempDocLine.SETRANGE("Full Barcode Value",TempScan."Full Barcode Value");
            IF NOT TempDocLine.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
              LastLineNo += 10000;
              TempDocLine.INIT;
              TempDocLine."Document Type" := DocType;
              TempDocLine."Document No." := DocNo;
              TempDocLine."Line No." := LastLineNo;
              // Not a document line: the status sync must never match it to one
              TempDocLine."Document Line No." := 0;
              TempDocLine.Version := 1;
              TempDocLine."Parent Line No." := LastLineNo;
              TempDocLine.Indentation := 0;
              TempDocLine."Item No." := TempScan."Item No.";
              TempDocLine."Item Description" := GetSurplusDescription(TempScan."Item No.",TempScan."Item Description",TempScan."Full Barcode Value");
              TempDocLine."Unit of Measure" := TempScan."Unit of Measure";
              TempDocLine.Barcode := TempScan.Barcode;
              TempDocLine."Full Barcode Value" := TempScan."Full Barcode Value";
              TempDocLine."Shipment No." := DocNo;
              TempDocLine.Status := TempDocLine.Status::Surplus;
              TempDocLine.INSERT;
            END;
            TakeScanQty(TempDocLine,TempScan,TempScan."Scanned Quantity");
          END;
        UNTIL TempScan.NEXT = 0;

      TempDocLine.RESET;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE GetSurplusDescription@50222(ItemNo@50000 : Code[40];ScanDescription@50001 : Text[110];FullBarcodeValue@50002 : Text[50]) : Text[110];
    VAR
      Item@50003 : Record 27;
      UnknownBarcodeTxt@50004 : TextConst 'ENU=Unknown barcode: %1;HRV=Nepoznat barkod: %1;SLV=Neznana ürtna koda: %1';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF ItemNo = '' THEN
        EXIT(COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(UnknownBarcodeTxt,FullBarcodeValue),1,110));
      IF ScanDescription <> '' THEN
        EXIT(ScanDescription);
      IF STRLEN(ItemNo) <= MAXSTRLEN(Item."No.") THEN
        IF Item.GET(ItemNo) THEN
          EXIT(Item."Full Description");
      EXIT('');
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE InsertBufferLines@50223(VAR TempDocLine@50000 : TEMPORARY Record 50113;VAR TempComponentLine@50001 : TEMPORARY Record 50113);
    VAR
      BufferLine@50002 : Record 50113;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      TempDocLine.RESET;
      IF TempDocLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          BufferLine := TempDocLine;
          BufferLine.INSERT;
        UNTIL TempDocLine.NEXT = 0;

      TempComponentLine.RESET;
      IF TempComponentLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          BufferLine := TempComponentLine;
          BufferLine.INSERT;
        UNTIL TempComponentLine.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE ChangeDocumentStatus@50224(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50002 : Boolean) : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::Shipment:
          EXIT(ChangeWhseShipmentStatus(DocNo,DocType,RunChangeDocStatus));
        DocType::Receipt:
          EXIT(ChangeWhseReceiptStatus(DocNo,DocType,RunChangeDocStatus));
        DocType::Transport:
          EXIT(ChangeTransportSheetStatus(DocNo,DocType,RunChangeDocStatus));
        DocType::WHCDSHIP,DocType::WHCDREC,DocType::WHCDSHFROM:
          EXIT(ChangeWhseShipmentStatusCD(DocNo,DocType,RunChangeDocStatus));
        DocType::CPCDSHIP,DocType::CPCDREC,DocType::CPCDSHFROM:
          EXIT(ChangeComplaintShipmentStatusCD(DocNo,DocType,RunChangeDocStatus));
      END;

      EXIT(FALSE);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ChangeWhseShipmentStatus@50225(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50002 : Boolean) : Boolean;
    VAR
      WarehouseShipmentHeader@50003 : Record 7320;
      WarehouseShipmentLine@50004 : Record 7321;
      BufferLine@50005 : Record 50113;
      TotalLines@50006 : Integer;
      CompletedLines@50007 : Integer;
      MissingLines@50008 : Integer;
      SurplusLines@50009 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF NOT WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);
      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);
      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized THEN BEGIN
        WarehouseShipmentHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status",WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized);
        WarehouseShipmentHeader.MODIFY;
      END;

      CountBufferLines(DocNo,DocType,TotalLines,CompletedLines,MissingLines,SurplusLines);
      IF CompletedLines <> TotalLines THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT RunChangeDocStatus THEN
          EXIT(FALSE);
        IF NOT ConfirmSyncWithDiscrepancies(MissingLines,SurplusLines) THEN
          EXIT(FALSE);
      END;

      BufferLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      BufferLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      BufferLine.SETRANGE(Indentation,0);
      BufferLine.SETFILTER("Document Line No.",'<>0');
      IF BufferLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          IF WarehouseShipmentLine.GET(DocNo,BufferLine."Document Line No.") THEN
            IF (WarehouseShipmentLine."Scanned Quantity" <> BufferLine."Scanned Quantity") OR (WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. to Ship" <> BufferLine."Scanned Quantity") THEN BEGIN
              WarehouseShipmentLine."Scanned Quantity" := BufferLine."Scanned Quantity";
              IF WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. Outstanding" <= BufferLine."Scanned Quantity" THEN
                WarehouseShipmentLine.VALIDATE("Qty. to Ship",WarehouseShipmentLine."Qty. Outstanding")
              ELSE
                WarehouseShipmentLine.VALIDATE("Qty. to Ship",BufferLine."Scanned Quantity");
              WarehouseShipmentLine.MODIFY(FALSE);
            END;
        UNTIL BufferLine.NEXT = 0;

      WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(DocNo);
      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN BEGIN
        WarehouseShipmentHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status",WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed);
        WarehouseShipmentHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;

      EXIT(TRUE);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ChangeWhseReceiptStatus@50226(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50002 : Boolean) : Boolean;
    VAR
      WarehouseReceiptHeader@50003 : Record 7316;
      WarehouseReceiptLine@50004 : Record 7317;
      BufferLine@50005 : Record 50113;
      TotalLines@50006 : Integer;
      CompletedLines@50007 : Integer;
      MissingLines@50008 : Integer;
      SurplusLines@50009 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF NOT WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);
      IF WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);
      IF WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized THEN BEGIN
        WarehouseReceiptHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status",WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized);
        WarehouseReceiptHeader.MODIFY;
      END;

      CountBufferLines(DocNo,DocType,TotalLines,CompletedLines,MissingLines,SurplusLines);
      IF CompletedLines <> TotalLines THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT RunChangeDocStatus THEN
          EXIT(FALSE);
        IF NOT ConfirmSyncWithDiscrepancies(MissingLines,SurplusLines) THEN
          EXIT(FALSE);
      END;

      BufferLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      BufferLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      BufferLine.SETRANGE(Indentation,0);
      BufferLine.SETFILTER("Document Line No.",'<>0');
      IF BufferLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          IF WarehouseReceiptLine.GET(DocNo,BufferLine."Document Line No.") THEN
            IF (WarehouseReceiptLine."Scanned Quantity" <> BufferLine."Scanned Quantity") OR (WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. to Receive" <> BufferLine."Scanned Quantity") THEN BEGIN
              WarehouseReceiptLine."Scanned Quantity" := BufferLine."Scanned Quantity";
              IF WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. Outstanding" <= BufferLine."Scanned Quantity" THEN
                WarehouseReceiptLine.VALIDATE("Qty. to Receive",WarehouseReceiptLine."Qty. Outstanding")
              ELSE
                WarehouseReceiptLine.VALIDATE("Qty. to Receive",BufferLine."Scanned Quantity");
              WarehouseReceiptLine.MODIFY(FALSE);
            END;
        UNTIL BufferLine.NEXT = 0;

      WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(DocNo);
      IF WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN BEGIN
        WarehouseReceiptHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status",WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed);
        WarehouseReceiptHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;

      EXIT(TRUE);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ChangeTransportSheetStatus@50227(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50002 : Boolean) : Boolean;
    VAR
      TransportSheetHeader@50003 : Record 50015;
      TransportSheetLine@50004 : Record 50016;
      BufferLine@50005 : Record 50113;
      TotalLines@50006 : Integer;
      CompletedLines@50007 : Integer;
      MissingLines@50008 : Integer;
      SurplusLines@50009 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF NOT TransportSheetHeader.GET(DocNo) THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);
      // Scanning sets the status itself; the barcode-admin check is for a change by hand
      TransportSheetHeader.SetCalledFromScanning(TRUE);
      IF TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status" = TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
        EXIT(TRUE);
      IF TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status" <> TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized THEN BEGIN
        TransportSheetHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status",TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Synchronized);
        TransportSheetHeader.MODIFY;
      END;

      // A transport sheet line takes its scanned quantity even before the sheet is complete
      BufferLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      BufferLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      BufferLine.SETRANGE(Indentation,0);
      BufferLine.SETFILTER("Document Line No.",'<>0');
      IF BufferLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          IF TransportSheetLine.GET(DocNo,BufferLine."Document Line No.") THEN
            IF TransportSheetLine."Scanned Quantity" <> BufferLine."Scanned Quantity" THEN BEGIN
              TransportSheetLine."Scanned Quantity" := BufferLine."Scanned Quantity";
              TransportSheetLine.MODIFY(FALSE);
            END;
        UNTIL BufferLine.NEXT = 0;

      // Its receipt cannot be posted while part of it is still to ship, so scanning cannot finish it yet
      IF TransportSheetHeader.Status = TransportSheetHeader.Status::"Partially Shipped" THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);

      CountBufferLines(DocNo,DocType,TotalLines,CompletedLines,MissingLines,SurplusLines);
      IF CompletedLines <> TotalLines THEN BEGIN
        IF NOT RunChangeDocStatus THEN
          EXIT(FALSE);
        IF NOT ConfirmSyncWithDiscrepancies(MissingLines,SurplusLines) THEN
          EXIT(FALSE);
      END;

      TransportSheetHeader.GET(DocNo);
      TransportSheetHeader.SetCalledFromScanning(TRUE);
      IF TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status" <> TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN BEGIN
        TransportSheetHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status",TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed);
        TransportSheetHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
      END;

      EXIT(TRUE);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ChangeWhseShipmentStatusCD@50228(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50002 : Boolean) IsCompleted : Boolean;
    VAR
      WhseShpt@50003 : Record 7320;
      TotalLines@50004 : Integer;
      CompletedLines@50005 : Integer;
      MissingLines@50006 : Integer;
      SurplusLines@50007 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF NOT WhseShpt.GET(DocNo) THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);

      IF NOT RunChangeDocStatus THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);

      IF IsBulkUpdate THEN
        WhseShpt.SetHideValidationDialog(TRUE);

      CountBufferLines(DocNo,DocType,TotalLines,CompletedLines,MissingLines,SurplusLines);
      IsCompleted := CompletedLines = TotalLines;

      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::WHCDSHIP:
          BEGIN
            IF NOT IsCompleted THEN BEGIN
              IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Ready for CD Shipment" THEN BEGIN
                WhseShpt.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status",WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)");
                WhseShpt.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)") OR (WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Ready for CD Shipment") THEN BEGIN
                WhseShpt.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status",WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD");
                WhseShpt.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END;
          END;

        DocType::WHCDREC:
          BEGIN
            IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::" " THEN
              EXIT(IsCompleted);

            IF NOT IsCompleted THEN BEGIN
              IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)" THEN BEGIN
                WhseShpt.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status",WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)");
                WhseShpt.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD" THEN BEGIN
                WhseShpt.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status",WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD");
                WhseShpt.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END;
          END;

        DocType::WHCDSHFROM:
          BEGIN
            IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::" " THEN
              EXIT(IsCompleted);

            IF NOT IsCompleted THEN BEGIN
              IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD (sync)" THEN BEGIN
                WhseShpt.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status",WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD (sync)");
                WhseShpt.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD" THEN BEGIN
                WhseShpt.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status",WhseShpt."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD");
                WhseShpt.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END;
          END;
      END;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ChangeComplaintShipmentStatusCD@50229(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';RunChangeDocStatus@50002 : Boolean) IsCompleted : Boolean;
    VAR
      ComplaintShptHdr@50003 : Record 50021;
      TotalLines@50004 : Integer;
      CompletedLines@50005 : Integer;
      MissingLines@50006 : Integer;
      SurplusLines@50007 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF NOT ComplaintShptHdr.GET(DocNo) THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);

      IF NOT RunChangeDocStatus THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);

      IF IsBulkUpdate THEN
        ComplaintShptHdr.SetHideValidationDialog(TRUE);

      CountBufferLines(DocNo,DocType,TotalLines,CompletedLines,MissingLines,SurplusLines);
      IsCompleted := CompletedLines = TotalLines;

      CASE DocType OF
        DocType::CPCDSHIP:
          BEGIN
            IF NOT IsCompleted THEN BEGIN
              IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Ready For Shipment" THEN BEGIN
                ComplaintShptHdr.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status",ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)");
                ComplaintShptHdr.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF (ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD (sync)") OR (ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Ready For Shipment") THEN BEGIN
                ComplaintShptHdr.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status",ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped to CD");
                ComplaintShptHdr.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END;
          END;

        DocType::CPCDREC:
          BEGIN
            IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::" " THEN
              EXIT(IsCompleted);

            IF NOT IsCompleted THEN BEGIN
              IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)" THEN BEGIN
                ComplaintShptHdr.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status",ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD (sync)");
                ComplaintShptHdr.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD" THEN BEGIN
                ComplaintShptHdr.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status",ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Received on CD");
                ComplaintShptHdr.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END;
          END;

        DocType::CPCDSHFROM:
          BEGIN
            IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" = ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::" " THEN
              EXIT(IsCompleted);

            IF NOT IsCompleted THEN BEGIN
              IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD (sync)" THEN BEGIN
                ComplaintShptHdr.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status",ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD (sync)");
                ComplaintShptHdr.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END ELSE BEGIN
              IF ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status" < ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD" THEN BEGIN
                ComplaintShptHdr.VALIDATE("Cross-Dock Shipment Status",ComplaintShptHdr."Cross-Dock Shipment Status"::"Shipped from CD");
                ComplaintShptHdr.MODIFY(FALSE);
              END;
            END;
          END;
      END;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CountBufferLines@50230(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';VAR TotalLines@50002 : Integer;VAR CompletedLines@50003 : Integer;VAR MissingLines@50004 : Integer;VAR SurplusLines@50005 : Integer);
    VAR
      BufferLine@50006 : Record 50113;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      BufferLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      BufferLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      TotalLines := BufferLine.COUNT;

      BufferLine.SETRANGE(Status,BufferLine.Status::Complete);
      CompletedLines := BufferLine.COUNT;

      BufferLine.SETFILTER(Status,'%1|%2',BufferLine.Status::Incomplete,BufferLine.Status::Missing);
      MissingLines := BufferLine.COUNT;

      BufferLine.SETRANGE(Status,BufferLine.Status::Surplus);
      SurplusLines := BufferLine.COUNT;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ConfirmSyncWithDiscrepancies@50231(MissingLines@50000 : Integer;SurplusLines@50001 : Integer) : Boolean;
    VAR
      UserSetup@50002 : Record 91;
      MissingDialogMessage@50003 : TextConst 'ENU=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.;HRV=Postoje nepodudaranja u skeniranim stavkama, molim provjerite skenirane stavke. Ako ste sigurni da ßelite aßurirati koliüine na dokumentu odaberite Da.;SLV=There are discrepancies in the scanned items, please check on the "Scanned Doc. Lines" page. If you have already checked and want to sync quantities select yes.';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF IsBulkUpdate OR NOT GUIALLOWED THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);
      IF (MissingLines = 0) AND (SurplusLines = 0) THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);
      IF NOT CONFIRM(MissingDialogMessage,FALSE) THEN
        EXIT(FALSE);

      UserSetup.GET(USERID);
      UserSetup.TESTFIELD("Scanned Barcodes Admin",TRUE);
      EXIT(TRUE);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE StartNextTransportReceipt@50232(VAR TransportSheetHeader@50000 : Record 50015);
    VAR
      TransportSheetLine@50001 : Record 50016;
      DocTypeLoc@50002 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      // A posted receipt closes its round of scanning. What is still to receive is scanned from zero, and that
      // round has to be able to reach Processed again, because the next receipt posting requires it.
      IF NOT IsBarcodeAppDocument(TransportSheetHeader."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport) THEN
        EXIT;

      ArchiveScannedEntries(TransportSheetHeader."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport);
      DeleteBufferLines(TransportSheetHeader."Document No.",DocTypeLoc::Transport);

      TransportSheetLine.SETRANGE("Document No.",TransportSheetHeader."Document No.");
      TransportSheetLine.SETFILTER("Scanned Quantity",'<>0');
      IF NOT TransportSheetLine.ISEMPTY THEN
        TransportSheetLine.MODIFYALL("Scanned Quantity",0);

      // Assigned, not validated: this runs inside the receipt posting's own status validation, and the
      // field's validation is written for a change by hand on the card
      TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status" := TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Blank;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ArchiveScannedEntries@50234(DocNo@50000 : Code[20];DocType@50001 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM');
    VAR
      ScannedDocumentLines@50002 : Record 50111;
      ScannedDocumentLinesArchive@50003 : Record 50112;
      ArchiveVersion@50004 : Integer;
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
      ScannedDocumentLines.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
      IF NOT ScannedDocumentLines.FINDSET THEN
        EXIT;

      REPEAT
        // Versioned the same way as the archive written when a scan is changed or deleted by hand
        ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.RESET;
        ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.SETRANGE("Document Type",DocType);
        ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.SETRANGE("Document No.",DocNo);
        ArchiveVersion := ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.COUNT;
        IF ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.FINDLAST THEN
          IF ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.Version > ArchiveVersion THEN
            ArchiveVersion := ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.Version;

        ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.INIT;
        ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.TRANSFERFIELDS(ScannedDocumentLines);
        ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.Version := ArchiveVersion + 1;
        ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.ArchiveDateTime := CURRENTDATETIME;
        ScannedDocumentLinesArchive."Arcived by User" := USERID;
        ScannedDocumentLinesArchive.INSERT;
      UNTIL ScannedDocumentLines.NEXT = 0;

      // Without the trigger, so the delete subscriber in BarcodeMgmt does not archive them a second time
      ScannedDocumentLines.DELETEALL;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE BulkWhseShptStatusUpdate@50235(VAR WarehouseShipmentHeader@50000 : Record 7320);
    VAR
      BarcodeAppMgmt@50001 : Codeunit 50112;
      DocType@50002 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
      BarcodeStatusQst@50003 : TextConst 'ENU=Do you want to update Barcode Status on %1 Warehouse Shipments?;HRV=¶elite li aßurirati barkod status na %1 skladiÁnih otpremnica?';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF NOT ConfirmBulkUpdate(WarehouseShipmentHeader.COUNT,BarcodeStatusQst) THEN
        EXIT;

      // A separate instance, so the bulk flag ends with this run
      BarcodeAppMgmt.SetIsBulkUpdate(TRUE);
      IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.FINDSET(TRUE) THEN
        REPEAT
          BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",DocType::Shipment,TRUE);
          BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",DocType::WHCDSHIP,TRUE);
          BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",DocType::WHCDREC,TRUE);
          BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",DocType::WHCDSHFROM,TRUE);
        UNTIL WarehouseShipmentHeader.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE BulkWhseReceiptStatusUpdate@50236(VAR WarehouseReceiptHeader@50000 : Record 7316);
    VAR
      BarcodeAppMgmt@50001 : Codeunit 50112;
      DocType@50002 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
      BarcodeStatusQst@50003 : TextConst 'ENU=Do you want to update Barcode Status on %1 Warehouse Receipts?;HRV=¶elite li aßurirati barkod status na %1 skladiÁnih primki?';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF NOT ConfirmBulkUpdate(WarehouseReceiptHeader.COUNT,BarcodeStatusQst) THEN
        EXIT;

      BarcodeAppMgmt.SetIsBulkUpdate(TRUE);
      IF WarehouseReceiptHeader.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(WarehouseReceiptHeader."No.",DocType::Receipt,TRUE);
        UNTIL WarehouseReceiptHeader.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE BulkTransportSheetStatusUpdate@50237(VAR TransportSheetHeader@50000 : Record 50015);
    VAR
      BarcodeAppMgmt@50001 : Codeunit 50112;
      DocType@50002 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';

      IF TransportSheetHeader.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(TransportSheetHeader."Document No.",DocType::Transport,TRUE);
        UNTIL TransportSheetHeader.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    PROCEDURE BulkComplaintSheetStatusUpdate@50238(VAR ComplaintShipment@50000 : Record 50021);
    VAR
      BarcodeAppMgmt@50001 : Codeunit 50112;
      DocType@50002 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
      BarcodeStatusQst@50003 : TextConst 'ENU=Do you want to update Barcode Status on %1 Complaint Shipments?;HRV=¶elite li aßurirati barkod status na %1 otprema reklamacija?';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF NOT ConfirmBulkUpdate(ComplaintShipment.COUNT,BarcodeStatusQst) THEN
        EXIT;

      BarcodeAppMgmt.SetIsBulkUpdate(TRUE);
      IF ComplaintShipment.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(ComplaintShipment."Document No.",DocType::CPCDSHIP,TRUE);
          BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(ComplaintShipment."Document No.",DocType::CPCDREC,TRUE);
          BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(ComplaintShipment."Document No.",DocType::CPCDSHFROM,TRUE);
        UNTIL ComplaintShipment.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE ConfirmBulkUpdate@50239(DocCount@50000 : Integer;ConfirmQst@50001 : Text) : Boolean;
    VAR
      NothingToUpdateMsg@50002 : TextConst 'ENU=There is nothing for update.;HRV=Ne postoji niÁta za aßuriranje.';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      IF DocCount = 0 THEN BEGIN
        IF GUIALLOWED THEN
          MESSAGE(NothingToUpdateMsg);
        EXIT(FALSE);
      END;

      IF GUIALLOWED THEN
        IF NOT CONFIRM(STRSUBSTNO(ConfirmQst,DocCount),TRUE) THEN
          EXIT(FALSE);

      EXIT(TRUE);
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    [TryFunction]
    PROCEDURE AutoUpdateBarcodeStatusOnSync@50240();
    VAR
      BarcodeAppMgmt@50000 : Codeunit 50112;
      BarcodeSyncRecord@50001 : Record 50161;
      TempProcessedRecord@50002 : TEMPORARY Record 50161;
      DocType@50003 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';
    BEGIN
      // <PRICO-2025-67>
      // Job queue: every document synced more than five minutes ago is updated once, without dialogs
      BarcodeAppMgmt.SetIsBulkUpdate(TRUE);

      BarcodeSyncRecord.SETRANGE("Entry Processed",FALSE);
      BarcodeSyncRecord.SETFILTER("Syncronization DateTime",'..%1',CURRENTDATETIME - (5 * 60 * 1000));
      IF BarcodeSyncRecord.FINDSET(TRUE) THEN
        REPEAT
          TempProcessedRecord.SETRANGE("Document No.",BarcodeSyncRecord."Document No.");
          TempProcessedRecord.SETRANGE("Document Type",BarcodeSyncRecord."Document Type");
          IF NOT TempProcessedRecord.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
            TempProcessedRecord.INIT;
            TempProcessedRecord.TRANSFERFIELDS(BarcodeSyncRecord);
            TempProcessedRecord.INSERT;

            CASE TempProcessedRecord."Document Type" OF
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Shipment)):
                BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::Shipment,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Prereceipt)):
                BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::Prereceipt,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Receipt)):
                BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::Receipt,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::Transport)):
                BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::Transport,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::WHCDSHIP)):
                BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::WHCDSHIP,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::WHCDREC)):
                BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::WHCDREC,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::WHCDSHFROM)):
                BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::WHCDSHFROM,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::CPCDSHIP)):
                BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::CPCDSHIP,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::CPCDREC)):
                BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::CPCDREC,TRUE);
              UPPERCASE(FORMAT(DocType::CPCDSHFROM)):
                BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(TempProcessedRecord."Document No.",DocType::CPCDSHFROM,TRUE);
            END;
            COMMIT;
          END;

          BarcodeSyncRecord."Entry Processed" := TRUE;
          BarcodeSyncRecord.MODIFY;
        UNTIL BarcodeSyncRecord.NEXT = 0;
      // </PRICO-2025-67>
    END;

    BEGIN
    END.
  }
}

OBJECT Page 50108 Scanned Document Lines
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=10.02.25;
    Time=15:45:17;
    Modified=Yes;
    Version List=PRIMA;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    CaptionML=[ENU=Scanned Document Lines;
               HRV=Reci skeniranih stavaka];
    SaveValues=Yes;
    SourceTable=Table50111;
    PageType=List;
    RefreshOnActivate=Yes;
    ShowFilter=No;
    OnClosePage=VAR
                  WarehouseShipmentHeader@50001 : Record 7320;
                  BEMFMgmt@50002 : Codeunit 50107;
                  WarehouseReceiptHeader@50000 : Record 7316;
                BEGIN
                  {
                  // <ZGNAIMPRRE-10>
                  Rec.RESET();
                  Rec.SETRANGE("Document No.",Rec."Document No.");
                  Rec.SETRANGE("Document Type", Rec."Document Type");
                  IF Rec.FINDFIRST() THEN
                    EXIT
                  ELSE
                    //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                    CASE Rec."Document Type" OF
                      Rec."Document Type"::Shipment:
                        BEGIN
                    //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                         IF WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(Rec."Document No.") AND
                          (WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Blank) THEN BEGIN
                            WarehouseShipmentHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Blank);
                            WarehouseShipmentHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
                            BEMFMgmt.FillWhseShptBarcodeEntries(WarehouseShipmentHeader,TRUE);
                         END;
                      // </ZGNAIMPRRE-10>
                       //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                        END;
                      Rec."Document Type"::Receipt:
                        BEGIN
                          IF WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(Rec."Document No.") AND
                          (WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" <> WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Blank) THEN BEGIN
                            WarehouseReceiptHeader.VALIDATE("Barcode Status", WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Blank);
                            WarehouseReceiptHeader.MODIFY(FALSE);
                            BEMFMgmt.FillWhseReceiptBarcodeEntries(WarehouseReceiptHeader,TRUE);
                          END;
                        END;
                    END;
                  //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                  }
                END;

    OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                       StyleTxt := SetStyle;
                     END;

    OnInsertRecord=BEGIN
                     Rec."User ID" := USERID;
                     Rec."Creation DateTime" := CURRENTDATETIME;
                   END;

  }
  CONTROLS
  {
    { 50000;0  ;Container ;
                ContainerType=ContentArea }

    { 50001;1  ;Group     ;
                Name=Group;
                GroupType=Repeater }

    { 50005;2  ;Field     ;
                SourceExpr="Document Type";
                Editable=FALSE }

    { 50003;2  ;Field     ;
                SourceExpr="Document No.";
                Editable=FALSE }

    { 50004;2  ;Field     ;
                SourceExpr="Item No.";
                StyleExpr=StyleTxt;
                OnValidate=BEGIN
                             // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
                             CASE "Document Type" OF
                                 "Document Type"::WHCDSHIP,"Document Type"::WHCDREC,"Document Type"::WHCDSHFROM,"Document Type"::CPCDSHIP,"Document Type"::CPCDREC,"Document Type"::CPCDSHFROM:
                                   BEGIN
                                     "Full Barcode Value" := "Item No.";
                                     "Item Description" := "Item No.";
                                     "Unit of Measure":= 'KOM'; //change this
                                   END ;
                                 ELSE BEGIN
                             // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
                                   IF Item.GET(Rec."Item No.") THEN BEGIN
                                     Rec."Item Description" := Item."Full Description";
                                     Rec."Unit of Measure" := Item."Base Unit of Measure";
                                   END ELSE BEGIN
                                     Rec."Item Description" := '';
                                     Rec."Unit of Measure" := '';
                                   END;
                             // <ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
                                 END;
                               END;
                             // </ZGNVSUPPCO-1245>
                           END;
                            }

    { 50006;2  ;Field     ;
                SourceExpr="Item Description";
                Editable=FALSE;
                StyleExpr=StyleTxt }

    { 50009;2  ;Field     ;
                SourceExpr="Unit of Measure";
                StyleExpr=StyleTxt }

    { 50008;2  ;Field     ;
                DecimalPlaces=0:5;
                SourceExpr="Scanned Quantity";
                StyleExpr=StyleTxt }

    { 50010;2  ;Field     ;
                SourceExpr="User ID";
                Editable=FALSE;
                StyleExpr=StyleTxt }

    { 50002;2  ;Field     ;
                SourceExpr="Creation DateTime";
                Editable=FALSE }

    { 50007;2  ;Field     ;
                SourceExpr="Full Barcode Value" }

  }
  CODE
  {
    VAR
      StyleTxt@50000 : Text;
      Item@50001 : Record 27;
      DocType@50002 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport,Receipt,WHCDSHIP,WHCDREC,WHCDSHFROM,CPCDSHIP,CPCDREC,CPCDSHFROM';

    BEGIN
    END.
  }

OBJECT Page 50109 Scanned Document Lines Archive

OBJECT Page 50110 Scanned Document Lines Buffer
{
  OBJECT-PROPERTIES
  {
    Date=06.10.26;
    Time=10:28:28;
    Modified=Yes;
    Version List=PRIMA;
  }
  PROPERTIES
  {
    CaptionML=[ENU=Scanned Document Lines;
               HRV=Reci skeniranih stavaka];
    InsertAllowed=No;
    DeleteAllowed=No;
    ModifyAllowed=No;
    SourceTable=Table50113;
    PageType=ListPart;
    RefreshOnActivate=Yes;
    OnOpenPage=BEGIN
                 //<ZGNAIMPRRE-10>
                 UserSetup.GET(USERID());
                   IF UserSetup."Scanned Barcodes Admin" THEN
                     IsVisible := TRUE;
                 //</ZGNAIMPRRE-10>
               END;

    OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                       StyleTxt := SetStyle;
                     END;

    OnQueryClosePage=VAR
                       WarehouseShipmentHeader@50001 : Record 7320;
                       BarcodeMgmt@50002 : Codeunit 50106;
                       BarcodeAppMgmt@50004 : Codeunit 50112;
                       WarehouseReceiptHeader@50003 : Record 7316;
                     BEGIN
                       //<ZGNAIMPRRE-10>Barcode
                       IF CloseAction = ACTION::OK THEN BEGIN
                          //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                           CASE Rec."Document Type" OF
                            Rec."Document Type"::Shipment: BEGIN
                          //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                             WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(Rec."Document No.");
                             IF NOT (WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed) THEN
                               // <PRICO-2025-67>
                               //IF NOT BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Type",FALSE) THEN //IF not all scanned items Compleated
                               IF NOT BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Type",FALSE) THEN //IF not all scanned items Compleated
                               // </PRICO-2025-67>
                                 EXIT;
                             END;
                       //</ZGNAIMPRRE-10>
                       //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                            Rec."Document Type"::Receipt: BEGIN
                             WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(Rec."Document No.");
                             IF NOT (WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed) THEN
                               // <PRICO-2025-67>
                               //IF NOT BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Type",FALSE) THEN //IF not all scanned items Compleated
                               IF NOT BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(Rec."Document No.",Rec."Document Type",FALSE) THEN //IF not all scanned items Compleated
                               // </PRICO-2025-67>
                                 EXIT;
                             END;
                           END;
                       END;
                       //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                     END;

    ActionList=ACTIONS
    {
      { 50020   ;    ;ActionContainer;
                      ActionContainerType=ActionItems }
      { 50019   ;1   ;Action    ;
                      Name=Update Document Qty;
                      CaptionML=[ENU=Update Document Qty;
                                 HRV=Aßuriraj koliüine za otpremu/zaprimanje;
                                 SLV=Update Document Qty];
                      Promoted=Yes;
                      PromotedIsBig=Yes;
                      Image=Add;
                      PromotedCategory=Process;
                      OnAction=VAR
                                 BarcodeMgmt@50001 : Codeunit 50106;
                                 BarcodeAppMgmt@50007 : Codeunit 50112;
                                 DocumentType@50000 : 'Shipment,Transfer Order,Inventory,Prereceipt,Transport Order,Receipt';
                                 WarehouseShipmentHeader@50002 : Record 7320;
                                 BarcodeStatusErr@50003 : TextConst 'ENU=Barcode status of document %1 must not be %2.;HRV=Barkod status dokumenta %1 ne smije biti %2.';
                                 WarehouseReceiptHeader@50004 : Record 7316;
                                 DocNotSupportedLbl@50005 : TextConst 'ENU=This action is not supported for this document type.;HRV=Ova akcija nije podrßana za ovu vrstu dokumenta.';
                                 TransportSheetHeader@50006 : Record 50015;
                               BEGIN
                                 //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                                 CASE Rec."Document Type" OF
                                   Rec."Document Type"::Shipment:
                                     BEGIN
                                 //</ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                                     //<ZGNAIMPRRE-10>
                                     WarehouseShipmentHeader.GET(Rec."Document No.");
                                     IF WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
                                       ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(BarcodeStatusErr,WarehouseShipmentHeader."No.",WarehouseShipmentHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed));
                                     // <PRICO-2025-67>
                                     //BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(Rec."Document No.",DocumentType::Shipment,TRUE);
                                     BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(Rec."Document No.",DocumentType::Shipment,TRUE);
                                     // </PRICO-2025-67>
                                     CurrPage.UPDATE;
                                     //</ZGNAIMPRRE-10>
                                     //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                                     END;
                                   Rec."Document Type"::Receipt:
                                     BEGIN
                                     WarehouseReceiptHeader.GET(Rec."Document No.");
                                     IF WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status" = WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
                                       ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(BarcodeStatusErr,WarehouseReceiptHeader."No.",WarehouseReceiptHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed));
                                     // <PRICO-2025-67>
                                     //BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(Rec."Document No.",DocumentType::Receipt,TRUE);
                                     BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(Rec."Document No.",DocumentType::Receipt,TRUE);
                                     // </PRICO-2025-67>
                                     CurrPage.UPDATE;
                                     END;
                                   Rec."Document Type"::Transport:
                                     BEGIN
                                     TransportSheetHeader.GET(Rec."Document No.");
                                     IF TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status" = TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed THEN
                                       ERROR(STRSUBSTNO(BarcodeStatusErr,TransportSheetHeader."Document No.",TransportSheetHeader."Barcode Status"::Processed));
                                     // <PRICO-2025-67>
                                     //BarcodeMgmt.OnOpenBufferPage(Rec."Document No.",DocumentType::"Transport Order",TRUE);
                                     BarcodeAppMgmt.CreateScanningEntriesOnDocument(Rec."Document No.",DocumentType::"Transport Order",TRUE);
                                     // </PRICO-2025-67>
                                     CurrPage.UPDATE;
                                     END;
                                   ELSE
                                     MESSAGE(DocNotSupportedLbl);
                                 END;
                                 //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059>
                               END;
                                }
      { 50017   ;1   ;Action    ;
                      Name=Scanned Items Administration;
                      CaptionML=[ENU=Scanned Items Administration;
                                 HRV=Administracija skeniranih stavaka];
                      Visible=IsVisible;
                      Image=TaskList;
                      OnAction=VAR
                                 ScannedDocumentLines@50000 : Record 50111;
                                 BarcodeMgmt@50001 : Codeunit 50106;
                               BEGIN
                                 //<ZGNAIMPRRE-10>Barcode
                                 ScannedDocumentLines."Document No." := Rec."Document No.";
                                 //ScannedDocumentLines."Document Type" := ScannedDocumentLines."Document Type"::Shipment; //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
                                 ScannedDocumentLines."Document Type" := Rec."Document Type"; //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
                                 ScannedDocumentLines.SETFILTER("Document No.",Rec."Document No.");
                                 //ScannedDocumentLines.SETFILTER("Document Type", FORMAT(ScannedDocumentLines."Document Type"::Shipment)); //<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
                                 ScannedDocumentLines.SETFILTER("Document Type", FORMAT(Rec."Document Type")); //<//<ZGNVSUPPCO-1059/>
                                 IF PAGE.RUNMODAL(PAGE::"Scanned Document Lines",ScannedDocumentLines)=ACTION::LookupOK THEN
                                   CurrPage.UPDATE;
                                 //</ZGNAIMPRRE-10>
                               END;
                                }
    }
  }
